+
+// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
+// Event listener utility
+// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
+/**
+ * Attaches an event listener to an element by id if the element exists.
+ * @param {any} id - Element id or record id to look up.
+ * @param {any} eventName - Input used by on.
+ * @param {any} handler - Callback function for an event.
+ */
+function on(id, eventName, handler) {
+  const el = $(id);
+  if (el) el.addEventListener(eventName, handler);
+}
+
+/**
+ * Returns true when a broad body input/change handler should skip a nested UI surface
+ * that has its own specialized event handling.
+ * @param {Event} event - DOM event from a delegated body listener.
+ */
+function shouldSkipBaseBodyEditEvent(event) {
+  const skipSelector = [
+    "[data-live-editor-step-id]",
+    "#completeStatementsToggle",
+    "#includeNotesToggle",
+    "#previewStyle",
+    "[data-preview-tab]",
+    ".visual-picker-modal-backdrop",
+    ".join-picker-backdrop",
+    ".source-replace-backdrop",
+    ".field-replace-backdrop",
+    ".condition-repair-backdrop",
+    ".logic-issue-panel",
+    ".logic-issues-backdrop",
+    ".new-rule-menu-panel",
+    ".tutorial-card",
+    ".tutorial-overlay",
+    ".modal",
+    "[data-my-audits-role]",
+    "#myAuditsSearch",
+  ].join(", ");
+  return !!event.target.closest?.(skipSelector);
+}
+
+// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
+// Top-level event bindings
+// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
+/**
+ * Registers all top-level UI event handlers.
+ */
+function bindEvents() {
+  if (window.__atlasCoreEventsBound) return;
+  window.__atlasCoreEventsBound = true;
+  if (e.completeStatements)
+    e.completeStatements.checked = state.previewStatementMode === "complete";
+  document.querySelectorAll(".rail-item").forEach((button) => {
+    button.onclick = (event) => {
+      if (button.classList.contains("is-locked")) {
+        event.preventDefault();
+        return;
+      }
+      atlasNavigateToSection(button.dataset.section).catch((err) =>
+        status(`Navigation save failed: ${err.message}`, "bad"),
+      );
+    };
+  });
+
+  document.querySelectorAll("[data-my-audits-role]").forEach((button) => {
+    button.addEventListener("click", () => {
+      const role = button.dataset.myAuditsRole;
+      if (
+        ![
+          "assigned",
+          "backup",
+          "developer",
+          "other",
+          "all",
+          "deleted",
+        ].includes(role)
+      )
+        return;
+      state.myAuditsRoleFilter = role;
+      if (role === "deleted") atlasRefreshDeletedAudits().catch(() => {});
+      renderMyAudits?.();
+    });
+  });
+  on("myAuditsSearch", "input", (event) => {
+    state.myAuditsSearch = event.target.value;
+    renderMyAudits?.();
+  });
+  on("createAuditBtn", "click", async () => {
+    const name = prompt("Name the new audit:", "New Audit Logic Flow");
+    if (!name) return;
+    if (!(await atlasSaveIfDirty("new audit"))) return;
+    state.audit = defaultAudit();
+    state.audit.name = name;
+    state.selectedStepId = null;
+    state.activeAuditValue = `draft:${state.audit.id}`;
+    markAuditSelected();
+    renderAll();
+    showSection("overview");
+    scheduleAutosave();
+    updateActiveAuditBaseline();
+    renderActiveAuditSelect?.();
+    atlasCreateCurrentAudit("Created audit").catch((err) =>
+      status(`Database create failed: ${err.message}`, "bad"),
+    );
+  });
+  on("renameAuditBtn", "click", () => {
+    const name = prompt("Rename active audit:", state.audit.name);
+    if (!name) return;
+    state.audit.name = name;
+    renderAll();
+    scheduleAutosave();
+  });
+  on("auditVersionHistoryBtn", "click", () =>
+    atlasOpenAuditHistory?.().catch((err) => status(err.message, "bad")),
+  );
+  on("stepVersionHistoryBtn", "click", () =>
+    atlasOpenStepHistory?.().catch((err) => status(err.message, "bad")),
+  );
+  on("duplicateAuditBtn", "click", async () => {
+    if (!(await atlasSaveIfDirty("duplicate audit"))) return;
+    capture();
+    const clone = structuredClone(state.audit);
+    clone.id = uid("audit");
+    clone.name = `${clone.name} Copy`;
+    clone.steps = clone.steps.map((step) => ({
+      ...step,
+      id: step.autoGeneratedReference
+        ? `reference_${uid("source")}`
+        : uid("step"),
+      parentId: null,
+    }));
+    state.audit = clone;
+    state.selectedStepId = state.audit.steps[0]?.id || null;
+    state.activeAuditValue = `draft:${state.audit.id}`;
+    markAuditSelected();
+    renderAll();
+    showSection("overview");
+    scheduleAutosave();
+    updateActiveAuditBaseline();
+    renderActiveAuditSelect?.();
+    atlasCreateCurrentAudit("Duplicated audit").catch((err) =>
+      status(`Database create failed: ${err.message}`, "bad"),
+    );
+  });
+  on("newAuditBtn", "click", async () => {
+    if (
+      confirm(
+        "Start a new audit logic flow? Unsaved changes remain in autosave until cleared.",
+      )
+    ) {
+      if (!(await atlasSaveIfDirty("new audit"))) return;
+      state.audit = defaultAudit();
+      state.selectedStepId = null;
+      state.activeAuditValue = `draft:${state.audit.id}`;
+      markAuditSelected();
+      renderAll();
+      showSection("overview");
+      scheduleAutosave();
+      updateActiveAuditBaseline();
+      renderActiveAuditSelect?.();
+      atlasCreateCurrentAudit("Created audit").catch((err) =>
+        status(`Database create failed: ${err.message}`, "bad"),
+      );
+    }
+  });
+
+  on("exportCurrentBtn", "click", exportCurrent);
+  on("downloadDocBtn", "click", exportCurrent);
+  on("downloadCsvBtn", "click", exportCsv);
+  on("downloadExcelBtn", "click", () =>
+    exportAuditExcel().catch((err) =>
+      status(`Excel export failed: ${err.message}`, "bad"),
+    ),
+  );
+  on("downloadQtestExcelBtn", "click", () =>
+    exportQtestExcel().catch((err) =>
+      status(`Qtest Excel export failed: ${err.message}`, "bad"),
+    ),
+  );
+  on("downloadHtmlBtn", "click", exportAuditHtml);
+  on("copyHtmlBtn", "click", () => copyAuditHtml());
+  on("copyMarkdownBtn", "click", copyMarkdown);
+  on("completeStatementsToggle", "change", () => {
+    state.previewStatementMode = e.completeStatements?.checked
+      ? "complete"
+      : "nested";
+    localStorage.setItem(
+      "atlasAlignPreviewStatementMode",
+      state.previewStatementMode,
+    );
+    atlasRequestRender?.({ preview: true });
+  });
+  on("includeNotesToggle", "change", () =>
+    atlasRequestRender?.({ preview: true }),
+  );
+  on("previewStyle", "change", () => atlasRequestRender?.({ preview: true }));
+  on("addStepBtn", "click", (event) => atlasToggleNewRuleTypeMenu?.(event));
+  on("loadLibraryAuditBtn", "click", () =>
+    loadSelectedLibraryAudit().catch((err) =>
+      status(`Audit library load failed: ${err.message}`, "bad"),
+    ),
+  );
+  on("refreshLibraryBtn", "click", () =>
+    loadRepoLibrary().catch((err) =>
+      status(`Database audit refresh failed: ${err.message}`, "bad"),
+    ),
+  );
+  on("saveFlowBtn", "click", () =>
+    saveFlow().catch((err) => status(err.message, "bad")),
+  );
+  if (!document.__atlasSaveShortcutBound) {
+    document.addEventListener("keydown", (event) => {
+      const isSaveShortcut =
+        String(event.key || "").toLowerCase() === "s" &&
+        (event.ctrlKey || event.metaKey) &&
+        !event.altKey;
+      if (!isSaveShortcut) return;
+      event.preventDefault();
+      if (!hasSelectedAudit?.())
+        return status("Select or create an audit before saving.", "warn");
+      atlasSaveIfDirty("keyboard shortcut").catch((err) =>
+        status(err.message, "bad"),
+      );
+    });
+    document.__atlasSaveShortcutBound = true;
+  }
+  on("loadFlowBtn", "click", () =>
+    loadFlow().catch((err) => status(err.message, "bad")),
+  );
+  on("deleteFlowBtn", "click", () =>
+    deleteFlow().catch((err) => status(err.message, "bad")),
+  );
+  on("exportAllBtn", "click", exportAll);
+  on("clearAutosaveBtn", "click", () => clearAutosave());
+  on("resetLocalDataBtn", "click", () =>
+    resetLocalAppData().catch((err) =>
+      status(`Clear saved data failed: ${err.message}`, "bad"),
+    ),
+  );
+  on("activeAuditSelect", "change", () => {
+    state.pendingActiveAuditValue = $("activeAuditSelect")?.value || "";
+    renderActiveAuditSelect?.();
+  });
+  on("confirmActiveAuditBtn", "click", () => {
+    const value =
+      state.pendingActiveAuditValue || $("activeAuditSelect")?.value || "";
+    if (!value || value === state.activeAuditValue) {
+      renderActiveAuditSelect?.();
+      return;
+    }
+    switchActiveAudit(value)
+      .then(() => {
+        state.pendingActiveAuditValue = "";
+        renderActiveAuditSelect?.();
+      })
+      .catch((err) => {
+        status(`Audit switch failed: ${err.message}`, "bad");
+        renderActiveAuditSelect?.();
+      });
+  });
+  on("fieldDisplayModeBtn", "click", () => {
+    toggleFieldDisplayMode?.();
+    renderFieldDisplayModeButton?.();
+  });
+  on("themeBtn", "click", () =>
+    setTheme(themeMode() === "dark" ? "light" : "dark"),
+  );
+  on("helpBtn", "click", () => startTutorial("global"));
+  on("auditTutorialBtn", "click", () => startTutorial("audit"));
+  on("fieldsTutorialBtn", "click", () => startTutorial("fields"));
+  on("stepsTutorialBtn", "click", () => startTutorial("steps"));
+  on("saveTutorialBtn", "click", () => startTutorial("save"));
+
+  const importAllFile = $("importAllFile");
+  if (importAllFile) {
+    importAllFile.onchange = async (event) => {
+      const file = event.target.files[0];
+      try {
+        if (file) await atlasImportAuditJsonFile(file);
+      } catch (err) {
+        status(`Audit import failed: ${err.message}`, "bad");
+      } finally {
+        event.target.value = "";
+      }
+    };
+  }
+  const importCurrentFile = $("importCurrentFile");
+  if (importCurrentFile) {
+    importCurrentFile.onchange = async (event) => {
+      const file = event.target.files[0];
+      try {
+        if (!file) return;
+        const result = await atlasImportAuditJsonFile(file);
+        const first = result.imported?.[0];
+        if (first?.audit?.id) {
+          await atlasLoadDatabaseAudit(first.audit.id);
+          showSection("overview");
+        }
+      } catch (err) {
+        status(`Audit import failed: ${err.message}`, "bad");
+      } finally {
+        event.target.value = "";
+      }
+    };
+  }
+
+  document.body.addEventListener("input", (event) => {
+    if (shouldSkipBaseBodyEditEvent(event)) return;
+    capture();
+    atlasRequestRender?.({ preview: true });
+    scheduleAutosave();
+  });
+  document.body.addEventListener("change", (event) => {
+    if (event.target.closest("#activeAuditSelect, #auditLibrarySelect")) return;
+    if (shouldSkipBaseBodyEditEvent(event)) return;
+    capture();
+    atlasRequestRender?.({
+      selectedStep: true,
+      preview: true,
+      logicTable: true,
+      validation: true,
+    });
+    scheduleAutosave();
+  });
+  document.body.addEventListener("click", (event) => {
+    const restoreButton = event.target.closest("[data-restore-deleted-audit]");
+    if (restoreButton) {
+      atlasRestoreDeletedAudit(restoreButton.dataset.restoreDeletedAudit).catch(
+        (error) => status(error.message, "bad"),
+      );
+      return;
+    }
+    const permanentButton = event.target.closest(
+      "[data-permanently-delete-audit]",
+    );
+    if (permanentButton) {
+      atlasPermanentlyDeleteAudit(
+        permanentButton.dataset.permanentlyDeleteAudit,
+      ).catch((error) => status(error.message, "bad"));
+      return;
+    }
+    const openAuditButton = event.target.closest("[data-open-database-audit]");
+    if (openAuditButton) {
+      const auditId = String(
+        openAuditButton.dataset.openDatabaseAudit || "",
+      ).trim();
+      if (!auditId) return;
+      event.preventDefault();
+      atlasLoadDatabaseAudit(auditId).catch((err) =>
+        status(`Audit load failed: ${err.message}`, "bad"),
+      );
+      return;
+    }
+    const card = event.target.closest(".step-card");
+    if (event.target.matches("[data-select-step]") && card) {
+      if (typeof atlasSelectStepWithoutListRender === "function") {
+        atlasSelectStepWithoutListRender(card.dataset.id);
+      } else {
+        state.selectedStepId = card.dataset.id;
+        renderSteps();
+        renderSelectedStep();
+      }
+    }
+    if (event.target.matches("[data-add-child-step]") && card) {
+      capture();
+      const parentStep = state.audit.steps.find(
+        (item) => item.id === card.dataset.id,
+      );
+      if (parentStep?.autoGeneratedReference)
+        return status(
+          "Generated reference steps cannot have child logic steps.",
+          "warn",
+        );
+      if (parentStep) addChildStep(parentStep);
+      return;
+    }
+    if (event.target.matches("[data-delete-step]") && card) {
+      capture();
+      const step = state.audit.steps.find(
+        (item) => item.id === card.dataset.id,
+      );
+      if (step?.autoGeneratedReference)
+        return status(
+          "Generated reference steps are removed by removing the source file.",
+          "warn",
+        );
+      state.audit.steps = state.audit.steps.filter(
+        (item) => item.id !== card.dataset.id,
+      );
+      atlasRequestStructuralStepRender?.();
+      scheduleAutosave();
+    }
+    if (event.target.matches("[data-indent]") && card) {
+      capture();
+      const step = state.audit.steps.find(
+        (item) => item.id === card.dataset.id,
+      );
+      if (step?.autoGeneratedReference)
+        return status("Generated reference steps cannot be nested.", "warn");
+      if (!makeChildOfPrevious(step))
+        status("This step cannot be nested under the previous step.", "warn");
+      atlasRequestStructuralStepRender?.();
+      scheduleAutosave();
+    }
+    if (event.target.matches("[data-outdent]") && card) {
+      capture();
+      const step = state.audit.steps.find(
+        (item) => item.id === card.dataset.id,
+      );
+      if (!outdentStep(step)) status("This step is already top-level.", "warn");
+      atlasRequestStructuralStepRender?.();
+      scheduleAutosave();
+    }
+  });
+  window.addEventListener("resize", positionTutorial);
+  if (!window.__atlasPageHideSaveBound) {
+    window.addEventListener("pagehide", atlasSaveOnPageHide);
+    window.__atlasPageHideSaveBound = true;
+  }
+}
+
+// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
+// Startup sequence
+// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
+/**
+ * Initializes theme, catalogs, events, autosave restore, and the initial render.
+ */
+async function init() {
+  setTheme(localStorage.getItem("atlasAlignTheme") || "light");
+
+  // Make the already-loaded shell interactive before awaiting remote startup data.
+  bindEvents();
+  bindCatalogFieldBrowserEvents?.();
+  showSection("my-audits");
+  updateNavigationLockState?.();
+  renderFlowNavControls?.();
+
+  const essentialStartup = await Promise.allSettled([
+    initPhraseCatalog?.(),
+    atlasInitSourceDatabase?.(),
+    atlasInitAuditDatabase?.({ render: false }),
+    atlasLoadCurrentUser?.({ render: false }),
+  ]);
+  renderCurrentUserProfile?.();
+  renderMyAudits?.();
+  renderStepTemplateOptions?.();
+
+  await loadRepoLibrary?.({ render: false, refreshAudits: false });
+  const restored = await restoreAutosave().catch(() => false);
+  if (!restored) state.selectedStepId = state.audit.steps[0]?.id || null;
+  syncAutoReferenceSteps?.();
+  updateNavigationLockState?.();
+  renderFlowNavControls?.();
+  renderActiveAuditSelect?.();
+  renderFieldDisplayModeButton?.();
+
+  const startupFailures = essentialStartup.filter(
+    (result) => result.status === "rejected",
+  );
+  status(
+    startupFailures.length
+      ? "Ready with startup warnings. Review the visible loading or error messages."
+      : "Ready. Select source files, build logic steps, and export the audit.",
+    startupFailures.length ? "warn" : "ok",
+  );
+}
+init();
+
+
+
+================================================================================
+END FILE: js/06-events-bootstrap.js
+================================================================================
+
+================================================================================
+BEGIN FILE: js/08-step-templates-validation.js
+================================================================================

+/**
+ * 08-step-templates-validation.js
+ *
+ * Legacy step-template helpers and validation-panel logic. Template UI may be hidden, but validation still evaluates audit completeness and step consistency.
+ * Auto-generated documentation comments explain function purpose, expected inputs, and module sections without changing runtime behavior.
+ */
+

+// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
+// Template insertion
+// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
+/**
+ * Populates the legacy step-template selector if present.
+ */
+function renderStepTemplateOptions() {
+  const select = $("stepTemplateSelect");
+  if (!select) return;
+  select.innerHTML = STEP_TEMPLATES.map(
+    (template) =>
+      `<option value="${template.id}">${esc(template.label)}</option>`,
+  ).join("");
+}
+
+/**
+ * Inserts the selected legacy step template.
+ */
+function insertSelectedStepTemplate() {
+  capture();
+  const selected = $("stepTemplateSelect")?.value;
+  const template =
+    STEP_TEMPLATES.find((item) => item.id === selected) || STEP_TEMPLATES[0];
+  if (!template) return;
+  const step = template.step();
+  state.audit.steps.push(step);
+  state.selectedStepId = step.id;
+  atlasInvalidatePages?.(["steps", "preview", "review"]);
+  showSection("steps");
+  atlasRenderActivePage?.({ force: true });
+  scheduleAutosave();
+  status(`Inserted template: ${template.label}`, "ok");
+}
+
+// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
+// Audit validation
+// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
+/**
+ * Builds validation issues for audit setup and step consistency.
+ */
+
+function atlasKnownFieldOptionValues() {
+  const values = new Set();
+  (state.audit.fields || []).forEach((field) => {
+    if (field.name) values.add(field.name);
+    if (field.rawFieldName) values.add(field.rawFieldName);
+  });
+  (repoLibraryState?.sourceFiles || []).forEach((source) => {
+    if (source.sourceKey) values.add(source.sourceKey);
+    (source.fields || []).forEach((field) => {
+      const raw =
+        field.fieldName || field.rawFieldName || field.name || field.label;
+      if (raw) values.add(raw);
+      if (field.vanityLabel) values.add(field.vanityLabel);
+      if (field.label) values.add(field.label);
+    });
+  });
+  return values;
+}
+
+function validateAudit() {
+  return window.ATLASAuditIssues?.legacyIssues?.() || [];
+}
+// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
+// Validation rendering
+// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
+/** Renders the canonical issue result through the legacy Readiness panel. */
+function renderValidationPanel() {
+  if (window.ATLASAuditIssues?.render) {
+    window.ATLASAuditIssues.render();
+    return;
+  }
+  return atlasMeasure?.("atlas.validation", () => {
+    const target = $("validationPanel");
+    if (!target) return;
+    const issues = validateAudit();
+    if (!issues.length) {
+      target.innerHTML =
+        '<div class="validation-ok">No validation issues found.</div>';
+      return;
+    }
+    target.innerHTML = `<div class="validation-list">${issues
+      .map(
+        (issue) =>
+          `<div class="validation-item ${issue.level}"><strong>${
+            issue.level === "error" ? "Error" : "Warning"
+          }:</strong> ${esc(issue.message)}</div>`,
+      )
+      .join("")}</div>`;
+  });
+}
+
+
+================================================================================
+END FILE: js/08-step-templates-validation.js
+================================================================================
+
+================================================================================
+BEGIN FILE: js/10-field-picker.js
+================================================================================
+
+/**
+ * Canonical source identity resolver.
+ * Resolves legacy names and aliases against the catalog while preserving the
+ * stable key, physical file name, and display alias as separate values.
+ */
+function atlasResolveSourceIdentity(sourceLike = {}, catalog = null) {
+  const input =
+    typeof sourceLike === "string" ? { name: sourceLike } : sourceLike || {};
+  const clean = (value) => String(value || "").trim();
+  const normalize = (value) => clean(value).toLowerCase();
+  const catalogSources = Array.isArray(catalog)
+    ? catalog
+    : typeof repoLibraryState !== "undefined" &&
+        Array.isArray(repoLibraryState?.sourceFiles)
+      ? repoLibraryState.sourceFiles
+      : [];
+  const uniqueMatches = (inputValues, sourceValues) => {
+    const wanted = new Set(inputValues.map(normalize).filter(Boolean));
+    if (!wanted.size) return [];
+    return catalogSources.filter((source) =>
+      sourceValues(source)
+        .map(normalize)
+        .filter(Boolean)
+        .some((value) => wanted.has(value)),
+    );
+  };
+  const stableValues = (source) => [
+    source.sourceKey,
+    source.catalogKey,
+    source.sourceReferenceId,
+    source.id,
+  ];
+  const fallbackValues = (source) => [
+    source.alias,
+    source.sourceFile,
+    source.file,
+    source.name,
+  ];
+  const inputStableValues = stableValues(input);
+  let matches = uniqueMatches(inputStableValues, stableValues);
+  if (matches.length !== 1)
+    matches = uniqueMatches(inputStableValues, fallbackValues);
+  if (matches.length !== 1) {
+    matches = uniqueMatches(fallbackValues(input), (source) => [
+      ...stableValues(source),
+      ...fallbackValues(source),
+    ]);
+  }
+  const match = matches.length === 1 ? matches[0] : {};
+  const ambiguous = matches.length > 1;
+  const sourceKey = clean(
+    match.sourceKey ||
+      match.catalogKey ||
+      input.sourceKey ||
+      input.catalogKey ||
+      match.sourceReferenceId ||
+      input.sourceReferenceId ||
+      match.id ||
+      input.id ||
+      match.alias ||
+      input.alias ||
+      match.sourceFile ||
+      input.sourceFile ||
+      match.file ||
+      input.file ||
+      input.name,
+  );
+  const sourceFile = clean(
+    match.sourceFile ||
+      input.sourceFile ||
+      match.file ||
+      input.file ||
+      match.name ||
+      input.name ||
+      sourceKey,
+  );
+  const alias = clean(
+    input.alias || match.alias || input.name || match.name || sourceFile,
+  );
+  return { sourceKey, sourceFile, alias, catalogSource: match, ambiguous };
+}
+function atlasNormalizeAuditSourceIdentity(audit) {
+  if (!audit || typeof audit !== "object") return audit;
+  audit.sources = Array.isArray(audit.sources) ? audit.sources : [];
+  audit.sources = audit.sources.map((source) => {
+    const identity = atlasResolveSourceIdentity(source);
+    return {
+      ...source,
+      sourceKey: identity.sourceKey,
+      sourceFile: identity.sourceFile,
+      alias: identity.alias,
+      name:
+        source.name ||
+        identity.alias ||
+        identity.sourceFile ||
+        identity.sourceKey,
+    };
+  });
+  const byAnyIdentity = (value) => atlasResolveSourceIdentity(value);
+  (Array.isArray(audit.fields) ? audit.fields : []).forEach((field) => {
+    const identity = byAnyIdentity(
+      field.sourceKey || field.sourceFile || field.sourceAlias || field.source,
+    );
+    if (identity.sourceKey) field.sourceKey = identity.sourceKey;
+    if (identity.sourceFile) field.sourceFile = identity.sourceFile;
+    if (identity.alias) field.sourceAlias = identity.alias;
+  });
+  (Array.isArray(audit.steps) ? audit.steps : []).forEach((step) => {
+    const params =
+      step.params && typeof step.params === "object"
+        ? step.params
+        : (step.params = {});
+    const identity = byAnyIdentity(
+      step.sourceKey ||
+        params.source_key ||
+        step.source ||
+        params.source_file ||
+        params.source_alias,
+    );
+    if (!identity.sourceKey) return;
+    step.sourceKey = identity.sourceKey;
+    step.sourceFile = identity.sourceFile;
+    step.sourceAlias = identity.alias;
+    step.source = identity.sourceKey;
+    params.source_key = identity.sourceKey;
+    params.source_file = identity.sourceFile;
+    params.source_alias = identity.alias;
+  });
+  return audit;
+}
+
+let atlasSourceDerivedVersion = 0;
+let atlasSourceCatalogCache = {
+  version: -1,
+  entries: [],
+  byStable: new Map(),
+  byFallback: new Map(),
+};
+function atlasInvalidateSourceDerivedState() {
+  atlasSourceDerivedVersion += 1;
+  atlasSourceCatalogCache.version = -1;
+  atlasMarkFieldPickerDirty?.();
+}
+function atlasInvalidateSourceBrowserCaches() {
+  atlasInvalidateSourceDerivedState();
+}
+function atlasSourceLookupAdd(map, value, source) {
+  const key = String(value || "")
+    .trim()
+    .toLowerCase();
+  if (!key) return;
+  const matches = map.get(key) || [];
+  matches.push(source);
+  map.set(key, matches);
+}
+
+const SOURCE_COLOR_PALETTE = [
+  "#fdd0ec",
+  "#b3f5ff",
+  "#abf5d1",
+  "#fffae6",
+  "#c0b6f2",
+  "#e6fcff",
+  "#ffd9c2",
+  "#d8f3dc",
+  "#dbeafe",
+  "#fde68a",
+];
+
+function sourceCatalogEntries() {
+  if (atlasSourceCatalogCache.version === atlasSourceDerivedVersion)
+    return atlasSourceCatalogCache.entries;
+  const entries = new Map();
+  (repoLibraryState?.sourceFiles || []).forEach((source) => {
+    const identity = atlasResolveSourceIdentity(source);
+    const key = identity.sourceKey;
+    if (!key) return;
+    entries.set(key, {
+      ...source,
+      sourceKey: key,
+      sourceFile: identity.sourceFile,
+      alias: identity.alias,
+      fields: Array.isArray(source.fields) ? source.fields : [],
+      fieldCount: source.fieldCount || source.fields?.length || 0,
+    });
+  });
+  (state.audit.sources || []).forEach((source) => {
+    const identity = atlasResolveSourceIdentity(source);
+    const key = identity.sourceKey;
+    if (!key || entries.has(key)) return;
+    entries.set(key, {
+      ...source,
+      sourceKey: key,
+      sourceFile: identity.sourceFile,
+      alias: identity.alias,
+      fields: [],
+      fieldCount: 0,
+    });
+  });
+  const sorted = [...entries.values()].sort((a, b) =>
+    sourceCatalogDisplayName(a).localeCompare(
+      sourceCatalogDisplayName(b),
+      undefined,
+      { sensitivity: "base", numeric: true },
+    ),
+  );
+  const byStable = new Map(),
+    byFallback = new Map();
+  sorted.forEach((entry) => {
+    [
+      entry.sourceKey,
+      entry.catalogKey,
+      entry.sourceReferenceId,
+      entry.id,
+    ].forEach((value) => atlasSourceLookupAdd(byStable, value, entry));
+    [entry.alias, entry.sourceFile, entry.file, entry.name].forEach((value) =>
+      atlasSourceLookupAdd(byFallback, value, entry),
+    );
+  });
+  atlasSourceCatalogCache = {
+    version: atlasSourceDerivedVersion,
+    entries: sorted,
+    byStable,
+    byFallback,
+  };
+  return sorted;
+}
+
+function selectedSourceDetail(sourceKeyOrFile) {
+  const value = String(sourceKeyOrFile || "").trim();
+  if (!value) return null;
+  const normalized = value.toLowerCase();
+  sourceCatalogEntries();
+  const stableMatches = atlasSourceCatalogCache.byStable.get(normalized) || [];
+  if (stableMatches.length === 1) return stableMatches[0];
+  if (stableMatches.length > 1) return null;
+  const fallbackMatches =
+    atlasSourceCatalogCache.byFallback.get(normalized) || [];
+  return fallbackMatches.length === 1 ? fallbackMatches[0] : null;
+}
+
+function fieldsForSource(sourceKeyOrFile) {
+  const match = selectedSourceDetail(sourceKeyOrFile);
+  if (match?.fields?.length) return match.fields;
+  const sourceKey = match?.sourceKey || String(sourceKeyOrFile || "").trim();
+  const sourceFile = match?.sourceFile || String(sourceKeyOrFile || "").trim();
+  return (state.audit.fields || [])
+    .filter(
+      (field) =>
+        !sourceKeyOrFile ||
+        field.sourceKey === sourceKey ||
+        (!field.sourceKey && field.sourceFile === sourceFile),
+    )
+    .map((field, index) => ({
+      order: index + 1,
+      fieldName: field.rawFieldName || field.name,
+      vanityLabel: field.name,
+      label: field.name,
+      ...field,
+    }));
+}
+
+function sourceOptionsHtml(selected = "") {
+  return [
+    '<option value="">Select source file...</option>',
+    ...sourceCatalogEntries().map((source) => {
+      const key = source.sourceKey;
+      return `<option value="${esc(key)}" ${selected === key || selected === source.sourceFile ? "selected" : ""}>${esc(sourceCatalogDisplayName(source))}</option>`;
+    }),
+  ].join("");
+}
+
+function fieldOptionsHtml(sourceFile = "", selected = "") {
+  const fields = fieldsForSource(sourceFile);
+  return [
+    '<option value="">Select field...</option>',
+    ...fields.map((field) => {
+      const parts = fieldDisplayParts(field, sourceFile);
+      const label = parts.alternate
+        ? `${parts.display} (${parts.alternate})`
+        : parts.display;
+      return `<option value="${esc(parts.raw)}" ${selected === parts.raw || selected === parts.vanity ? "selected" : ""}>${esc(label)}</option>`;
+    }),
+  ].join("");
+}
+
+function nextSourceColor() {
+  const used = new Set(
+    (state.audit.sources || []).map((source) => source.color).filter(Boolean),
+  );
+  return (
+    SOURCE_COLOR_PALETTE.find((color) => !used.has(color)) ||
+    SOURCE_COLOR_PALETTE[
+      (state.audit.sources || []).length % SOURCE_COLOR_PALETTE.length
+    ]
+  );
+}
+
+function atlasSourceMutationKey(source = {}) {
+  if (typeof atlasSourceCanonicalKey === "function")
+    return atlasSourceCanonicalKey(source);
+  if (typeof sourceCatalogKey === "function") return sourceCatalogKey(source);
+  return String(
+    source.sourceKey || source.name || source.sourceFile || "",
+  ).trim();
+}
+
+function atlasAddSourceState(sourceKeyOrFile) {
+  const source = selectedSourceDetail(sourceKeyOrFile);
+  if (!source) {
+    status(
+      `Source file ${sourceKeyOrFile} was not found in the source library.`,
+      "warn",
+    );
+    return {
+      changed: false,
+      reason: "not-found",
+      sourceKey: String(sourceKeyOrFile || ""),
+    };
+  }
+
+  const key = atlasSourceMutationKey(source);
+  const sourceFile = source.sourceFile || key;
+  state.audit.sources = state.audit.sources || [];
+
+  if (
+    state.audit.sources.some((item) => atlasSourceMutationKey(item) === key)
+  ) {
+    return {
+      changed: false,
+      reason: "already-selected",
+      sourceKey: key,
+      sourceFile,
+    };
+  }
+
+  state.audit.sources.push({
+    id: uid("source"),
+    sourceKey: key,
+    name: key,
+    sourceFile,
+    alias: source.alias || sourceFile,
+    color: nextSourceColor(),
+    description:
+      source.alias && source.alias !== sourceFile
+        ? `Alias: ${source.alias}`
+        : "",
+    sourceMetadata: source.sourceMetadata || {},
+  });
+
+  state.audit.fields = (state.audit.fields || []).filter((field) => {
+    const fieldKey = String(field.sourceKey || "").trim();
+    return fieldKey ? fieldKey !== key : field.sourceFile !== sourceFile;
+  });
+
+  (source.fields || []).forEach((field) => {
+    const raw = field.fieldName || field.name || field.label;
+    const label = field.vanityLabel || field.label || raw;
+    if (!raw) return;
+    state.audit.fields.push({
+      id: uid("field"),
+      name: label,
+      type: "Source",
+      sourceKey: key,
+      sourceFile,
+      sourceAlias: source.alias || sourceFile,
+      rawFieldName: raw,
+      description: `${sourceFile} / ${raw}`,
+    });
+  });
+
+  atlasInvalidateSourceDerivedState();
+  return { changed: true, action: "add", sourceKey: key, sourceFile };
+}
+
+function atlasRemoveSourceState(sourceKeyOrFile) {
+  const selected =
+    typeof atlasSelectedSourceByKey === "function"
+      ? atlasSelectedSourceByKey(sourceKeyOrFile)
+      : (state.audit.sources || []).find((source) =>
+          [
+            source.sourceKey,
+            source.name,
+            source.sourceFile,
+            source.alias,
+          ].includes(sourceKeyOrFile),
+        );
+
+  if (!selected) {
+    status(`Source ${sourceKeyOrFile} is not selected for this audit.`, "warn");
+    return {
+      changed: false,
+      reason: "not-selected",
+      sourceKey: String(sourceKeyOrFile || ""),
+    };
+  }
+
+  const key = atlasSourceMutationKey(selected);
+  const sourceFile = selected.sourceFile || selected.name || key;
+  const generatedStepIds = new Set(
+    (state.audit.steps || [])
+      .filter(
+        (step) =>
+          step.autoGeneratedReference &&
+          atlasSourceMutationKey({
+            sourceKey: step.source,
+            sourceFile: step.source,
+          }) === key,
+      )
+      .map((step) => step.id),
+  );
+
+  state.audit.sources = (state.audit.sources || []).filter(
+    (source) => atlasSourceMutationKey(source) !== key,
+  );
+  state.audit.fields = (state.audit.fields || []).filter((field) => {
+    const fieldKey = String(field.sourceKey || "").trim();
+    return fieldKey ? fieldKey !== key : field.sourceFile !== sourceFile;
+  });
+  state.audit.steps = (state.audit.steps || []).filter(
+    (step) => !generatedStepIds.has(step.id),
+  );
+
+  if (state.selectedStepId && generatedStepIds.has(state.selectedStepId)) {
+    state.selectedStepId = null;
+  }
+
+  atlasInvalidateSourceDerivedState();
+  return {
+    changed: true,
+    action: "remove",
+    sourceKey: key,
+    sourceFile,
+    selectedStepRemoved: generatedStepIds.size > 0 && !state.selectedStepId,
+  };
+}
+
+function atlasCommitSourceMutationFallback(action, sourceKeyOrFile) {
+  const previousSelectedStepId = state.selectedStepId;
+  const result =
+    action === "remove"
+      ? atlasRemoveSourceState(sourceKeyOrFile)
+      : atlasAddSourceState(sourceKeyOrFile);
+  if (!result.changed) return result;
+
+  syncAutoReferenceSteps();
+  if (
+    state.selectedStepId &&
+    !state.audit.steps.some((step) => step.id === state.selectedStepId)
+  ) {
+    state.selectedStepId = state.audit.steps[0]?.id || null;
+  }
+  if (!state.selectedStepId && previousSelectedStepId) {
+    state.selectedStepId = state.audit.steps[0]?.id || null;
+  }
+
+  atlasMarkFieldPickerDirty?.();
+  atlasInvalidatePages?.(["fields", "steps", "preview", "review"]);
+  atlasRenderActivePage?.({ force: true });
+  scheduleAutosave();
+  status(
+    `${action === "remove" ? "Removed" : "Added"} ${result.sourceFile || sourceKeyOrFile} ${action === "remove" ? "from" : "to"} this audit.`,
+    "ok",
+  );
+  return result;
+}
+
+function addSourceFileToAudit(sourceKeyOrFile) {
+  if (typeof atlasCommitAuditSourceChange === "function") {
+    return atlasCommitAuditSourceChange({ action: "add", sourceKeyOrFile });
+  }
+  return atlasCommitSourceMutationFallback("add", sourceKeyOrFile);
+}
+
+function removeSourceFileFromAudit(sourceKeyOrFile) {
+  if (typeof atlasCommitAuditSourceChange === "function") {
+    return atlasCommitAuditSourceChange({ action: "remove", sourceKeyOrFile });
+  }
+  return atlasCommitSourceMutationFallback("remove", sourceKeyOrFile);
+}
+
+function referenceStepForSource(source, index) {
+  return {
+    id: `reference_${source.id || source.name}`,
+    enabled: true,
+    autoGeneratedReference: true,
+    locked: true,
+    parentId: null,
+    branchType: "root",
+    sortOrder: index + 1,
+    title: `Reference ${source.name}`,
+    type: "source",
+    step_type: "reference_source",
+    source: source.name,
+    field: "",
+    action: "",
+    notes:
+      "Generated automatically from the source files selected in Sources & Fields.",
+    conditions: [],
+    params: {
+      source_file: source.name,
+      source_alias:
+        source.alias || sourceAliasFor?.(source.name) || source.name,
+    },
+  };
+}
+
+function syncAutoReferenceSteps() {
+  const selected = state.audit.sources || [];
+  const manualSteps = (state.audit.steps || []).filter(
+    (step) => !step.autoGeneratedReference,
+  );
+  const referenceSteps = selected.map((source, index) => {
+    const existing = (state.audit.steps || []).find(
+      (step) => step.autoGeneratedReference && step.source === source.name,
+    );
+    return {
+      ...(existing || referenceStepForSource(source, index)),
+      ...referenceStepForSource(source, index),
+      id: existing?.id || referenceStepForSource(source, index).id,
+    };
+  });
+  referenceSteps.forEach((step, index) => {
+    step.sortOrder = index + 1;
+  });
+  manualSteps.forEach((step, index) => {
+    step.sortOrder = selected.length + index + 1;
+  });
+  state.audit.steps = [...referenceSteps, ...manualSteps];
+}
+
+function applyLibraryFieldToSelectedStep(sourceFile, fieldName) {
+  const step = state.audit.steps.find(
+    (item) => item.id === state.selectedStepId,
+  );
+  if (!step)
+    return status("Select a step before applying a source field.", "warn");
+  if (step.autoGeneratedReference)
+    return status(
+      "Generated reference steps are controlled by selected sources. Select or add a logic step to apply a field.",
+      "warn",
+    );
+  const sourceResult = atlasAddSourceState(sourceFile);
+  if (sourceResult.changed) syncAutoReferenceSteps();
+  normalizeCatalogStep?.(step);
+  step.source = sourceFile;
+  step.field = fieldName;
+  step.params.source_file = sourceFile;
+  step.params.source_alias = sourceAliasFor?.(sourceFile) || sourceFile;
+  step.params.field_name = fieldName;
+  const conditions = parseStepConditions?.(step) || [];
+  if (step.step_type === "if_then" && conditions.length) {
+    conditions[0].sourceFile = sourceFile;
+    conditions[0].sourceAlias = step.params.source_alias;
+    conditions[0].fieldName = fieldName;
+    step.params.conditions_json = JSON.stringify(conditions);
+  }
+  atlasMarkFieldPickerDirty?.();
+  atlasInvalidatePages?.(["fields", "steps", "preview", "review"]);
+  showSection("steps");
+  atlasRenderActivePage?.({ force: true });
+  scheduleAutosave();
+  status(`Applied ${sourceFile}.${fieldName} to the selected step.`, "ok");
+}
+
+function allFieldOptions() {
+  const options = [];
+  (state.audit.fields || []).forEach((field) => {
+    if (!field.name) return;
+    options.push({
+      value: field.name,
+      label: `${field.name} (${field.type || "Field"})`,
+      kind: field.type || "Field",
+      source: field.sourceFile || "Selected Audit Fields",
+      raw: field.rawFieldName || field.name,
+    });
+    if (field.rawFieldName)
+      options.push({
+        value: field.rawFieldName,
+        label: `${field.rawFieldName} (${field.sourceFile || "Raw Field"})`,
+        kind: "Raw Field",
+        source: field.sourceFile || "Selected Audit Fields",
+        raw: field.rawFieldName,
+      });
+  });
+  sourceCatalogEntries().forEach((source) => {
+    options.push({
+      value: source.sourceKey,
+      label: `${sourceCatalogDisplayName(source)} (Source)`,
+      kind: "Source",
+      source: source.sourceKey,
+    });
+    (source.fields || []).forEach((field) => {
+      const parts = fieldDisplayParts(field, source.sourceFile);
+      if (parts.raw)
+        options.push({
+          value: parts.raw,
+          label: `${parts.display} (${source.sourceFile}.${parts.raw})`,
+          kind: "Source Field",
+          source: source.sourceFile,
+          raw: parts.raw,
+        });
+    });
+  });
+  workbookFieldOptions?.().forEach((field) => options.push(field));
+  const seen = new Set();
+  return options
+    .filter((option) => {
+      const key = `${option.value}::${option.label}`;
+      if (!option.value || seen.has(key)) return false;
+      seen.add(key);
+      return true;
+    })
+    .sort((a, b) => a.label.localeCompare(b.label));
+}
+
+function refreshFieldPickerOptions() {
+  return atlasMeasure?.("atlas.field-picker.build", () => {
+    let fieldList = $("fieldPickerOptions");
+    let sourceList = $("sourcePickerOptions");
+    if (!fieldList) {
+      fieldList = document.createElement("datalist");
+      fieldList.id = "fieldPickerOptions";
+      document.body.append(fieldList);
+    }
+    if (!sourceList) {
+      sourceList = document.createElement("datalist");
+      sourceList.id = "sourcePickerOptions";
+      document.body.append(sourceList);
+    }
+    fieldList.innerHTML = allFieldOptions()
+      .map(
+        (option) =>
+          `<option value="${esc(option.value)}" label="${esc(option.label)}"></option>`,
+      )
+      .join("");
+    sourceList.innerHTML = sourceCatalogEntries()
+      .map(
+        (source) =>
+          `<option value="${esc(source.sourceKey)}" label="${esc(sourceCatalogDisplayName(source))}"></option>`,
+      )
+      .join("");
+    atlasFieldPickerState.initialized = true;
+    atlasFieldPickerState.dirty = false;
+  });
+}
+
+function applyFieldPickerAttributes() {
+  document
+    .querySelectorAll("[data-step-field], [data-cond-field]")
+    .forEach((input) => input.setAttribute("list", "fieldPickerOptions"));
+  document
+    .querySelectorAll("[data-step-source]")
+    .forEach((input) => input.setAttribute("list", "sourcePickerOptions"));
+}
+
+function renderFieldPickerPanel() {
+  const panel = $("fieldPickerSummary");
+  if (!panel) return;
+  const sources = sourceCatalogEntries();
+  const fieldCount = sources.reduce(
+    (total, source) => total + (source.fields?.length || 0),
+    0,
+  );
+  const selectedCount = (state.audit.sources || []).length;
+  panel.innerHTML = sources.length
+    ? `<p class="hint">${sources.length} source file(s) and ${fieldCount} field(s) available from the local JSON source library. ${selectedCount} source file(s) selected for this audit.</p>`
+    : '<p class="hint">No source fields found yet. Confirm audit-index.json points to catalogs/source-file-index.json and run through http://localhost or GitHub Pages.</p>';
+}
+
+const atlasFieldPickerState = { initialized: false, dirty: true };
+
+function atlasMarkFieldPickerDirty() {
+  atlasFieldPickerState.dirty = true;
+}
+
+function atlasEnsureFieldPickerOptions() {
+  if (!atlasFieldPickerState.initialized || atlasFieldPickerState.dirty) {
+    refreshFieldPickerOptions();
+  }
+}
+
+// Build backing datalists only when a field/source input is first used.
+document.addEventListener(
+  "focusin",
+  (event) => {
+    if (
+      !event.target.matches?.(
+        "[data-step-field], [data-cond-field], [data-step-source]",
+      )
+    )
+      return;
+    atlasEnsureFieldPickerOptions();
+    applyFieldPickerAttributes();
+  },
+  true,
+);
+
+
+================================================================================
+END FILE: js/10-field-picker.js
+================================================================================

+================================================================================
+BEGIN FILE: js/15b-source-library-ui.js
+================================================================================
+
+/**
+ * 15b-source-library-ui.js
+ * Split from 15-visual-ui-upgrades.js.
+ * Audit/source contacts-style browser refinements and selected-source rendering/actions.
+ * IMPORTANT: classic browser script; preserve the 15a-15g load order.
+ */
+/* Spacing/data-browser refinement layer */
+let activeSourceLibraryTab = "all";
+function sourceSystem(source) {
+  const text = `${source.sourceFile || ""} ${source.alias || ""}`.toLowerCase();
+  if (
+    text.includes("workday") ||
+    text.includes("wd.") ||
+    text.includes("wd ") ||
+    text.startsWith("wd")
+  )
+    return "workday";
+  if (
+    text.includes("sap") ||
+    text.includes("sap.") ||
+    /^zhr|^zhre|^hi\d|^pa\s/i.test(source.sourceFile || "")
+  )
+    return "sap";
+  return "other";
+}
+function selectedSourceSet() {
+  return new Set(
+    (state.audit.sources || [])
+      .map((source) => atlasSourceMutationKey(source))
+      .filter(Boolean),
+  );
+}
+function sourceMatchesSearch(source, query) {
+  if (!query) return true;
+  return [
+    source.sourceFile,
+    source.alias,
+    ...(source.fields || []).flatMap((field) => [
+      field.fieldName,
+      field.vanityLabel,
+      field.label,
+    ]),
+  ]
+    .join(" ")
+    .toLowerCase()
+    .includes(query);
+}
+
+let activeSelectedSourceName = "";
+
+function firstLetter(value) {
+  const c = String(value || "")
+    .trim()
+    .charAt(0)
+    .toUpperCase();
+  return /[A-Z]/.test(c) ? c : "#";
+}
+function alphaSortItems(items, getName) {
+  return [...items].sort((a, b) =>
+    String(getName(a) || "").localeCompare(
+      String(getName(b) || ""),
+      undefined,
+      { sensitivity: "base", numeric: true },
+    ),
+  );
+}
+function alphaLetters(items, getName) {
+  return [
+    ...new Set(
+      alphaSortItems(items, getName).map((item) => firstLetter(getName(item))),
+    ),
+  ];
+}
+function scrollContactsLetter(containerSelector, letter) {
+  const container = document.querySelector(containerSelector);
+  const target = container?.querySelector(
+    `[data-alpha-letter="${CSS.escape(letter)}"]`,
+  );
+  if (container && target)
+    container.scrollTo({
+      top: target.offsetTop - container.offsetTop,
+      behavior: "smooth",
+    });
+}
+
+function allAuditLibraryItems() {
+  const items = [];
+  (repoLibraryState?.audits || []).forEach((audit) =>
+    items.push({
+      value: `repo:${audit.id}`,
+      title: audit.name || audit.id,
+      detail: audit.description || audit.file || "",
+      kind: "Repo Audit",
+    }),
+  );
+  (state.savedFlows || []).forEach((flow) =>
+    items.push({
+      value: `local:${flow.id}`,
+      title: flow.name || "Saved audit",
+      detail: flow.updatedAt
+        ? `Saved ${new Date(flow.updatedAt).toLocaleString()}`
+        : "Saved in this browser",
+      kind: "Saved Audit",
+    }),
+  );
+  if (typeof workbookState !== "undefined" && workbookState.loaded)
+    (workbookState.auditSheets || []).forEach((name) =>
+      items.push({
+        value: `workbook:${name}`,
+        title: name,
+        detail: "Workbook audit sheet",
+        kind: "Workbook Import",
+      }),
+    );
+  return alphaSortItems(items, (item) => item.title);
+}
+
+renderAuditLibrarySelect = function renderAuditLibraryContacts() {
+  const select = $("auditLibrarySelect");
+  const visual = $("auditLibraryVisual");
+  const allItems = allAuditLibraryItems();
+  const currentSearch = $("auditLibrarySearch")?.value || "";
+  const query = currentSearch.toLowerCase().trim();
+  const items = allItems.filter((item) =>
+    `${item.title} ${item.detail} ${item.kind}`.toLowerCase().includes(query),
+  );
+
+  if (select) {
+    select.innerHTML = allItems.length
+      ? allItems
+          .map(
+            (item) =>
+              `<option value="${esc(item.value)}">${esc(item.title)}</option>`,
+          )
+          .join("")
+      : '<option value="">No audits available</option>';
+  }
+
+  if (!visual) return;
+
+  const letters = alphaLetters(items, (item) => item.title);
+  const rows = items.length
+    ? items
+        .map((item, idx) => {
+          const letter = firstLetter(item.title);
+          const previousLetter = idx ? firstLetter(items[idx - 1].title) : "";
+          const header =
+            letter !== previousLetter
+              ? `<div class="contacts-letter-header audit-letter-header" data-alpha-letter="${esc(letter)}">${esc(letter)}</div>`
+              : "";
+          return `${header}<button type="button" class="contacts-row audit-contact-row" data-audit-library-value="${esc(item.value)}"><span class="audit-contact-content"><span class="contacts-title audit-contact-title">${esc(item.title)}</span><span class="contacts-detail audit-contact-detail">${esc(item.kind)}${item.detail ? ` · ${esc(item.detail)}` : ""}</span></span></button>`;
+        })
+        .join("")
+    : '<p class="hint contacts-empty audit-contacts-empty">No audits match the current search.</p>';
+
+  visual.innerHTML = `<div class="contacts-menu-header audit-library-search-row"><input id="auditLibrarySearch" value="${esc(currentSearch)}" placeholder="Search audits..."><button type="button" class="small" data-new-audit-from-library>+ New Audit</button></div><div class="contacts-list-shell audit-library-shell"><div class="contacts-list audit-library-list" data-contacts-list="audit">${rows}</div><div class="contacts-letter-index audit-letter-index">${letters.map((letter) => `<button type="button" data-scroll-letter="${esc(letter)}" data-scroll-scope="audit">${esc(letter)}</button>`).join("")}</div></div>`;
+};
+
+function filteredSourceEntries() {
+  const tab = activeSourceLibraryTab || "all";
+  const query = ($("sourceFieldSearch")?.value || "").toLowerCase().trim();
+  const selected = selectedSourceSet();
+  return alphaSortItems(
+    sourceCatalogEntries(),
+    (source) => source.sourceFile,
+  ).filter((source) => {
+    if (tab === "selected" && !selected.has(sourceCatalogKey(source)))
+      return false;
+    if (tab !== "all" && tab !== "selected" && sourceSystem(source) !== tab)
+      return false;
+    return sourceMatchesSearch(source, query);
+  });
+}
+
+let atlasSourceUsageCache = { version: -1, audit: null, byKey: new Map() };
+function atlasBuildSourceUsageIndex() {
+  if (
+    atlasSourceUsageCache.version === atlasSourceDerivedVersion &&
+    atlasSourceUsageCache.audit === state.audit
+  )
+    return atlasSourceUsageCache.byKey;
+  const byKey = new Map();
+  const ensure = (sourceLike) => {
+    const identity = atlasResolveSourceIdentity(sourceLike);
+    if (!identity.sourceKey || identity.ambiguous) return null;
+    if (!byKey.has(identity.sourceKey))
+      byKey.set(identity.sourceKey, {
+        manualSelected: false,
+        logicDerived: false,
+        displayDerived: false,
+        included: false,
+        color: SOURCE_COLOR_PALETTE?.[0] || "#4d6fa9",
+      });
+    return byKey.get(identity.sourceKey);
+  };
+  (state.audit.sources || []).forEach((source) => {
+    const usage = ensure(source);
+    if (!usage) return;
+    usage.manualSelected = true;
+    usage.color = source.color || usage.color;
+  });
+  const markLogic = (value) => {
+    const usage = ensure(value);
+    if (usage) usage.logicDerived = true;
+  };
+  (state.audit.steps || []).forEach((step) => {
+    if (step.autoGeneratedReference) return;
+    const p = step.params || {};
+    [
+      step.sourceKey,
+      step.source,
+      p.source_key,
+      p.source_file,
+      p.left_source,
+      p.right_source,
+      p.then_value_source_file,
+      p.concat_source_1,
+      p.concat_source_2,
+      p.string_source_file,
+      p.then_string_source_file,
+    ].forEach(markLogic);
+    try {
+      (JSON.parse(p.conditions_json || "[]") || []).forEach((condition) => {
+        [
+          condition.sourceKey,
+          condition.sourceFile,
+          condition.compareSourceKey,
+          condition.compareSourceFile,
+        ].forEach(markLogic);
+      });
+    } catch (_error) {
+      /* Preserve malformed legacy condition behavior. */
+    }
+  });
+  if (typeof atlasLogicFieldReferences === "function")
+    atlasLogicFieldReferences().forEach((reference) =>
+      markLogic(
+        reference.sourceKey || reference.sourceFile || reference.sourceAlias,
+      ),
+    );
+  (state.audit.fields || []).forEach((field) => {
+    const displayed = [field.displayed, field.display, field.isDisplayed].some(
+      (value) => value === true || String(value || "").toLowerCase() === "yes",
+    );
+    if (!displayed) return;
+    const usage = ensure(
+      field.sourceKey || field.sourceFile || field.sourceAlias,
+    );
+    if (usage) usage.displayDerived = true;
+  });
+  byKey.forEach((usage) => {
+    usage.included =
+      usage.manualSelected || usage.logicDerived || usage.displayDerived;
+  });
+  atlasSourceUsageCache = {
+    version: atlasSourceDerivedVersion,
+    audit: state.audit,
+    byKey,
+  };
+  return byKey;
+}
+function atlasSourceUsageState(source) {
+  const identity = atlasResolveSourceIdentity(source);
+  const usage = !identity.ambiguous
+    ? atlasBuildSourceUsageIndex().get(identity.sourceKey)
+    : null;
+  return (
+    usage || {
+      manualSelected: false,
+      logicDerived: false,
+      displayDerived: false,
+      included: false,
+      color: source.sourceColor || SOURCE_COLOR_PALETTE?.[0] || "#4d6fa9",
+    }
+  );
+}
+function atlasSourceLibraryPill(source) {
+  const usage = atlasSourceUsageState(source);
+  const alias = source.alias || source.sourceFile;
+  const badges = [
+    usage.manualSelected
+      ? '<span class="source-inclusion-badge manual">Manual</span>'
+      : "",
+    usage.logicDerived
+      ? '<span class="source-inclusion-badge logic">Used in Logic</span>'
+      : "",
+    usage.displayDerived
+      ? '<span class="source-inclusion-badge display">Has Displayed Fields</span>'
+      : "",
+    `<span class="source-inclusion-badge ${usage.included ? "included" : "available"}">${usage.included ? "Included" : "Available"}</span>`,
+  ]
+    .filter(Boolean)
+    .join("");
+  return `<article class="audit-source-picker-pill ${usage.included ? "included" : "available"}"${usage.included ? ` style="--source-pill-color:${esc(usage.color)}"` : ""}>
+    <span class="source-color-dot" aria-hidden="true"></span>
+    <button type="button" class="audit-source-picker-main" data-source-detail-name="${esc(sourceCatalogKey(source))}">
+      <strong>${esc(source.sourceFile)}</strong>
+      ${alias && alias !== source.sourceFile ? `<span>${esc(alias)}</span>` : ""}
+      <small>${esc(sourceSystem(source).toUpperCase())} · ${source.fields?.length || 0} fields</small>
+    </button>
+    <span class="source-inclusion-badges">${badges}</span>
+    <button type="button" class="btn small source-library-action-button ${usage.manualSelected ? "danger source-library-remove-button" : "primary source-library-add-button"}" data-source-action="${usage.manualSelected ? "remove" : "add"}" data-source-key="${esc(source.sourceKey || source.sourceFile)}">${usage.manualSelected ? "Remove" : "Add"}</button>
+  </article>`;
+}
+
+function atlasSourceLibraryTabsHtml(allSources) {
+  const tabs = [
+    ["all", "All", allSources],
+    [
+      "workday",
+      "Workday",
+      allSources.filter((source) => sourceSystem(source) === "workday"),
+    ],
+    [
+      "sap",
+      "SAP",
+      allSources.filter((source) => sourceSystem(source) === "sap"),
+    ],
+    [
+      "other",
+      "Other / Unknown",
+      allSources.filter((source) => sourceSystem(source) === "other"),
+    ],
+    [
+      "selected",
+      "Included",
+      allSources.filter((source) => atlasSourceUsageState(source).included),
+    ],
+  ];
+  return tabs
+    .map(
+      ([key, label, items]) =>
+        `<button type="button" class="source-library-tab ${activeSourceLibraryTab === key ? "active" : ""}" data-source-tab="${key}">${esc(label)} <span>${items.length}</span></button>`,
+    )
+    .join("");
+}
+
+renderSourceFieldBrowser = function renderSourceFieldBrowserAuditAwareInline() {
+  const panel = $("sourceFieldBrowser");
+  const tabRoot = $("sourceLibraryTabs");
+  if (!panel) return;
+  const allSources = sourceCatalogEntries();
+  const sources = filteredSourceEntries();
+  const includedCount = allSources.filter(
+    (source) => atlasSourceUsageState(source).included,
+  ).length;
+
+  if (tabRoot) tabRoot.innerHTML = atlasSourceLibraryTabsHtml(allSources);
+  panel.innerHTML = `
+    <div class="source-library-inline-summary" aria-live="polite">
+      <span><strong>${includedCount}</strong> included for this audit</span>
+      <span>${allSources.length} available</span>
+    </div>
+    <div class="source-library-picker-results source-library-inline-results" data-source-library-results>
+      ${sources.length ? sources.map(atlasSourceLibraryPill).join("") : '<p class="hint contacts-empty">No source files match the current search.</p>'}
+    </div>`;
+};
+
+function fieldRowsForSource(sourceName) {
+  return fieldsForSource(sourceName).map((field, index) => {
+    const raw =
+      field.fieldName || field.rawFieldName || field.name || field.label || "";
+    const vanity =
+      field.vanityLabel ||
+      field.label ||
+      (field.name !== raw ? field.name : "") ||
+      "";
+    return {
+      order: field.order || index + 1,
+      fieldName: raw,
+      vanityLabel: vanity,
+      formatting:
+        field.formatting || field.format || field.formattingNotes || "",
+      displayed: field.displayed || field.display || "",
+      used: field.used || field.usedInAudit || "",
+    };
+  });
+}
+function renderSelectedSourceColorControl(source) {
+  const color = source.color || SOURCE_COLOR_PALETTE[0];
+  return `<details class="compact-color-picker"><summary><span class="single-color-swatch" style="background:${esc(color)}"></span><span>${esc(color)}</span></summary><div class="compact-color-menu">${SOURCE_COLOR_PALETTE.map((paletteColor) => `<button type="button" class="color-swatch ${paletteColor === color ? "active" : ""}" data-source-palette-color="${esc(paletteColor)}" style="background:${esc(paletteColor)}" title="${esc(paletteColor)}"></button>`).join("")}<label class="custom-color-label">Custom <input type="color" data-source-custom-color value="${esc(color)}"></label></div></details>`;
+}
+function renderSelectedSourceTable(sourceName) {
+  if (!sourceName)
+    return '<p class="hint">Select a source to view its fields.</p>';
+  const rows = fieldRowsForSource(sourceName);
+  const source = (state.audit.sources || []).find(
+    (item) => item.name === sourceName,
+  );
+  if (!source) return '<p class="hint">Select a source to view its fields.</p>';
+  return `<section class="selected-source-data-view"><div class="title-row"><div><h4>${esc(source.name)}</h4><p class="hint">${esc(source.alias || sourceAliasFor?.(source.name) || source.name)} · ${rows.length} fields</p></div><button type="button" class="secondary small" data-close-source-data>Back to selected sources</button></div><div class="selected-source-table-wrap"><table class="selected-source-table"><thead><tr><th>#</th><th>Field Name</th><th>Vanity Label</th><th>Formatting</th><th>Used</th><th>Displayed</th></tr></thead><tbody>${rows.map((row) => `<tr><td>${esc(row.order)}</td><td>${esc(row.fieldName)}</td><td>${esc(row.vanityLabel)}</td><td>${esc(row.formatting)}</td><td>${esc(row.used)}</td><td>${esc(row.displayed)}</td></tr>`).join("")}</tbody></table></div></section>`;
+}
+renderSources = function renderCombinedSelectedSources() {
+  const target = e.sourcesList;
+  const sources = state.audit.sources || [];
+  if (!sources.length) {
+    target.innerHTML =
+      '<p class="hint">No source files selected yet. Use the Source Library above to add files to this audit.</p>';
+    return;
+  }
+  if (
+    activeSelectedSourceName &&
+    !sources.some((source) => source.name === activeSelectedSourceName)
+  )
+    activeSelectedSourceName = "";
+  if (activeSelectedSourceName) {
+    target.innerHTML = renderSelectedSourceTable(activeSelectedSourceName);
+    return;
+  }
+  target.innerHTML = `<div class="selected-source-list">${sources
+    .map((source) => {
+      const rows = fieldRowsForSource(source.name);
+      return `<div class="selected-source-compact-card" data-id="${esc(source.id)}" style="--source-color:${esc(source.color || SOURCE_COLOR_PALETTE[0])}"><button type="button" class="selected-source-main" data-source-detail-name="${esc(source.name)}"><span class="single-color-swatch" style="background:${esc(source.color || SOURCE_COLOR_PALETTE[0])}"></span><span><strong>${esc(source.name)}</strong><small>${esc(source.alias || sourceAliasFor?.(source.name) || source.name)} · ${rows.length} fields</small></span></button>${renderSelectedSourceColorControl(source)}<button type="button" class="danger small" data-remove-selected-source="${esc(source.name)}">Remove</button></div>`;
+    })
+    .join("")}</div>`;
+};
+renderFields = function renderFieldsMergedIntoSources() {
+  if (e.fieldsList) e.fieldsList.innerHTML = "";
+};
+
+function atlasCommitAuditSourceChange({ action, sourceKeyOrFile }) {
+  const previousSelectedStepId = state.selectedStepId;
+  const result =
+    action === "remove"
+      ? atlasRemoveSourceState(sourceKeyOrFile)
+      : atlasAddSourceState(sourceKeyOrFile);
+
+  if (!result.changed) return result;
+
+  // Source mutation has completed. Synchronize generated references exactly once.
+  syncAutoReferenceSteps();
+
+  if (
+    state.selectedStepId &&
+    !state.audit.steps.some((step) => step.id === state.selectedStepId)
+  ) {
+    state.selectedStepId = state.audit.steps[0]?.id || null;
+  }
+  if (!state.selectedStepId && previousSelectedStepId) {
+    state.selectedStepId = state.audit.steps[0]?.id || null;
+  }
+
+  // Reconcile the Sources page synchronously so the clicked action is visible immediately.
+  renderSources?.();
+  renderSourceFieldBrowser?.();
+  renderFieldPickerPanel?.();
+  updateDashboardMetrics?.();
+
+  // Mark expensive backing data and hidden pages dirty; render them on first use.
+  atlasMarkFieldPickerDirty?.();
+  if (visualPickerState) renderMiniPicker?.();
+  atlasInvalidatePages?.(["steps", "preview", "review", "overview"]);
+
+  // Persist exactly once, after all state and UI work is complete.
+  scheduleAutosave();
+  status(
+    `${action === "remove" ? "Removed" : "Added"} ${result.sourceFile || sourceKeyOrFile} ${action === "remove" ? "from" : "to"} this audit.`,
+    "ok",
+  );
+  return result;
+}
+
+function atlasSourceActionFromControl(control) {
+  if (control.matches("[data-remove-selected-source]")) return "remove";
+  if (control.matches("[data-source-action]"))
+    return control.dataset.sourceAction === "remove" ? "remove" : "add";
+  return "add";
+}
+
+function atlasSourceKeyFromControl(control) {
+  return (
+    control.dataset.sourceKey ||
+    control.dataset.removeSelectedSource ||
+    control.dataset.addSourceToAudit ||
+    control.dataset.sourceName ||
+    ""
+  );
+}
+
+// One capture-phase route owns both Source Library and Selected Sources & Fields actions.
+document.addEventListener(
+  "click",
+  (event) => {
+    const control = event.target.closest(
+      "[data-source-action], [data-add-source-to-audit], [data-remove-selected-source]",
+    );
+    if (!control) return;
+    const sourceKeyOrFile = atlasSourceKeyFromControl(control);
+    if (!sourceKeyOrFile) return;
+    event.preventDefault();
+    event.stopImmediatePropagation();
+    atlasCommitAuditSourceChange({
+      action: atlasSourceActionFromControl(control),
+      sourceKeyOrFile,
+    });
+  },
+  true,
+);
+
+const previousSpacingClickHandler = true;
+document.addEventListener("click", (event) => {
+  const newAudit = event.target.closest("[data-new-audit-from-library]");
+  if (newAudit) {
+    $("createAuditBtn")?.click();
+    return;
+  }
+  const scrollButton = event.target.closest("[data-scroll-letter]");
+  if (scrollButton) {
+    scrollContactsLetter(
+      scrollButton.dataset.scrollScope === "audit"
+        ? '[data-contacts-list="audit"]'
+        : '[data-contacts-list="source"]',
+      scrollButton.dataset.scrollLetter || "",
+    );
+    return;
+  }
+  const sourceDetail = event.target.closest("[data-source-detail-name]");
+  if (sourceDetail && sourceDetail.closest("#sourcesList")) {
+    activeSelectedSourceName = sourceDetail.dataset.sourceDetailName || "";
+    renderSources();
+    return;
+  }
+  if (sourceDetail && sourceDetail.closest("#sourceFieldBrowser")) {
+    const sourceName = sourceDetail.dataset.sourceDetailName || "";
+    if (selectedSourceSet().has(sourceName)) {
+      activeSelectedSourceName = sourceName;
+      renderSources();
+    }
+    return;
+  }
+  const closeSourceData = event.target.closest("[data-close-source-data]");
+  if (closeSourceData) {
+    activeSelectedSourceName = "";
+    renderSources();
+    return;
+  }
+});
+
+document.addEventListener("input", (event) => {
+  if (event.target?.id === "auditLibrarySearch") renderAuditLibrarySelect();
+});
+
+document.addEventListener("input", (event) => {
+  if (event.target?.id === "sourceFieldSearch") renderSourceFieldBrowser();
+});
+
+document.addEventListener("click", (event) => {
+  const palette = event.target.closest("[data-source-palette-color]");
+  if (!palette) return;
+  const card = palette.closest(".selected-source-compact-card");
+  if (!card) return;
+  const source = (state.audit.sources || []).find(
+    (item) => item.id === card.dataset.id,
+  );
+  if (source) {
+    source.color = palette.dataset.sourcePaletteColor;
+    atlasInvalidatePages?.(["fields", "steps", "preview", "review"]);
+    atlasRenderActivePage?.({ force: true });
+    scheduleAutosave();
+  }
+});
+
+document.addEventListener("input", (event) => {
+  if (!event.target.matches("[data-source-custom-color]")) return;
+  const card = event.target.closest(".selected-source-compact-card");
+  if (!card) return;
+  const source = (state.audit.sources || []).find(
+    (item) => item.id === card.dataset.id,
+  );
+  if (source) {
+    source.color = event.target.value;
+    atlasInvalidatePages?.(["fields", "steps", "preview", "review"]);
+    atlasRenderActivePage?.({ force: true });
+    scheduleAutosave();
+  }
+});
+

+================================================================================
+BEGIN FILE: js/15d-logic-rules-list.js
