+/**
+ * 15d-logic-rules-list.js
+ * Split from 15-visual-ui-upgrades.js.
+ * Selected-step editor redesign and editor-aware picker commit behavior.
+ * IMPORTANT: classic browser script; preserve the 15a-15g load order.
+ */
+/* Logic Builder selected-step editor redesign */
+function atlasStepTypeLabel(step) {
+  return (
+    (STEP_TYPE_OPTIONS || []).find(
+      ([value]) => value === step.step_type,
+    )?.[1] ||
+    step.step_type ||
+    "Logic Step"
+  );
+}
+function atlasSelectOptions(options, selected) {
+  return options
+    .map((option) =>
+      Array.isArray(option)
+        ? `<option value="${esc(option[0])}" ${selected === option[0] ? "selected" : ""}>${esc(option[1])}</option>`
+        : `<option value="${esc(option)}" ${selected === option ? "selected" : ""}>${esc(option)}</option>`,
+    )
+    .join("");
+}
+function atlasVisibleStepEntries() {
+  return hierarchicalStepEntries({ includeDisabled: true });
+}
+function atlasEditorValue(root, selector, fallback = "") {
+  const field = root.querySelector(selector);
+  if (!field) return fallback;
+  if (field.type === "checkbox") return field.checked;
+  return field.value;
+}
+function atlasCaptureGuidedFields(root, step) {
+  normalizeCatalogStep?.(step);
+  step.params =
+    step.params && typeof step.params === "object" ? step.params : {};
+  root.querySelectorAll("[data-param]").forEach((input) => {
+    const key = input.dataset.param;
+    if (!key) return;
+    step.params[key] =
+      input.type === "checkbox" ? (input.checked ? "Yes" : "No") : input.value;
+  });
+  const conditionRows = [...root.querySelectorAll(".guided-condition-row")];
+  if (conditionRows.length) {
+    const conditions = conditionRows.map((row, index) => {
+      const get = (field) =>
+        row.querySelector(`[data-condition-field="${field}"]`)?.value || "";
+      const sourceFile = get("sourceFile");
+      const compareSourceFile = get("compareSourceFile") || sourceFile;
+      return {
+        id: row.dataset.conditionId || uid("cond"),
+        connector: index === 0 ? "IF" : get("connector") || "AND",
+        conditionType: "row_compare",
+        sourceFile,
+        sourceAlias: sourceAliasFor(sourceFile),
+        fieldName: get("fieldName"),
+        operator: get("operator") || "is equal to",
+        valueMode: get("valueMode") || "values",
+        compareValue: get("compareValue"),
+        compareSourceFile,
+        compareSourceAlias: sourceAliasFor(compareSourceFile),
+        compareFieldName: get("compareFieldName"),
+        expression: get("expression"),
+      };
+    });
+    step.params.conditions_json = JSON.stringify(conditions);
+    step.conditions = conditions.map((condition) => ({
+      id: condition.id,
+      field: condition.fieldName,
+      operator: condition.operator,
+      value: condition.compareValue,
+    }));
+  }
+  if (step.params.source_file) {
+    step.source = step.params.source_file;
+    step.params.source_alias = sourceAliasFor(step.params.source_file);
+  }
+  if (step.params.field_name) step.field = step.params.field_name;
+  if (step.params.column_name) step.field = step.params.column_name;
+}
+function atlasRefreshLogicRuleCard(step) {
+  if (!step?.id) return;
+
+  const card = document.querySelector(
+    `.step-card[data-id="${CSS.escape(step.id)}"]`,
+  );
+  if (!card) return;
+
+  const logicTarget = card.querySelector(
+    ".compact-rule-logic, .logic-rule-summary",
+  );
+  if (logicTarget) {
+    logicTarget.innerHTML = renderLogicWithPills(analystLogic(step));
+  }
+
+  card.classList.toggle("selected", state.selectedStepId === step.id);
+}
+
+function atlasCommitLiveEditor({ rerender = false } = {}) {
+  const root = document.querySelector("[data-live-editor-step-id]");
+  if (!root) return;
+  const step = state.audit.steps.find(
+    (item) => item.id === root.dataset.liveEditorStepId,
+  );
+  if (!step || step.autoGeneratedReference) return;
+  const nextType = atlasEditorValue(
+    root,
+    "[data-live-step-type-select]",
+    step.step_type || "if_then",
+  );
+  const typeChanged = nextType && nextType !== step.step_type;
+  step.step_type = nextType;
+  step.type = legacyTypeFromStepType?.(step.step_type) || step.type;
+  if (typeChanged) {
+    step.params = {};
+    normalizeCatalogStep?.(step);
+    if (typeof atlasRequestStructuralStepRender === "function") {
+      atlasRequestStructuralStepRender();
+    } else {
+      renderSteps?.();
+      renderSelectedStep?.();
+      renderLogicTablePreviewRows?.();
+      renderPreview?.();
+      renderValidationPanel?.();
+    }
+    scheduleAutosave();
+    return;
+  }
+  step.title = atlasEditorValue(
+    root,
+    "[data-live-step-title]",
+    step.title || atlasStepTypeLabel(step),
+  );
+  const parentId =
+    atlasEditorValue(root, "[data-live-parent]", step.parentId || "") || null;
+  if ((parentId || null) !== (step.parentId || null))
+    setStepParent(step, parentId);
+  step.branchType = step.parentId
+    ? atlasEditorValue(root, "[data-live-branch]", step.branchType || "then")
+    : "root";
+  step.enabled = !!atlasEditorValue(
+    root,
+    "[data-live-enabled]",
+    step.enabled !== false,
+  );
+  step.notes = atlasEditorValue(
+    root,
+    "[data-step-notes-live]",
+    step.notes || "",
+  );
+  atlasCaptureGuidedFields(root, step);
+  normalizeCatalogStep?.(step);
+  if (rerender) {
+    if (typeof atlasRequestStructuralStepRender === "function") {
+      atlasRequestStructuralStepRender();
+    } else {
+      renderSteps?.();
+      renderSelectedStep?.();
+      renderLogicTablePreviewRows?.();
+      renderPreview?.();
+      renderValidationPanel?.();
+    }
+  } else {
+    const output = root.querySelector("[data-live-generated-output]");
+    if (output) {
+      output.innerHTML = renderLogicWithPills(analystLogic(step));
+    }
+
+    atlasRefreshLogicRuleCard(step);
+    renderPreview?.();
+    renderLogicTablePreviewRows?.();
+    renderValidationPanel?.();
+  }
+  scheduleAutosave();
+}
+function atlasRenderParentOptions(step) {
+  return parentOptionsForStep(step)
+    .map(
+      (option) =>
+        `<option value="${esc(option.id)}" ${(step.parentId || "") === option.id ? "selected" : ""}>${esc(option.label)}</option>`,
+    )
+    .join("");
+}
+function atlasRenderBranchEditor(step) {
+  if (!step.parentId)
+    return '<input type="hidden" data-live-branch value="root"><p class="hint">Top-level rule. Use Indent to nest it under the previous rule.</p>';
+  return `<label><span>Branch Type</span><select data-live-branch>${branchTypeOptions(step)}</select></label>`;
+}
+function atlasRenderStepEditor(step) {
+  if (!step || step.autoGeneratedReference) {
+    return '<div class="logic-editor-section"><p class="hint">Generated reference steps are controlled by selected source files. Select a manual rule to edit its logic criteria.</p></div>';
+  }
+  normalizeCatalogStep?.(step);
+  return `<div class="logic-editor-form" data-live-editor-step-id="${esc(step.id)}">
+    <section class="logic-editor-section">
+      <h3>Step settings</h3>
+      <div class="logic-editor-grid">
+        <label class="full"><span>Step Name</span><input data-live-step-title value="${esc(step.title || atlasStepTypeLabel(step))}" placeholder="Name this rule"></label>
+        <label><span>Step Type</span><select data-live-step-type-select>${atlasSelectOptions(
+          (STEP_TYPE_OPTIONS || []).filter(
+            ([value]) => value !== "reference_source",
+          ),
+          step.step_type,
+        )}</select></label>
+        <label><span>Parent Step</span><select data-live-parent>${atlasRenderParentOptions(step)}</select></label>
+        ${atlasRenderBranchEditor(step)}
+        <label><span class="check"><input type="checkbox" data-live-enabled ${step.enabled !== false ? "checked" : ""}> Enabled</span></label>
+      </div>
+      <div class="logic-editor-toolbar">
+        <button type="button" class="btn secondary small" data-indent>Indent</button>
+        <button type="button" class="btn secondary small" data-outdent>Outdent</button>
+        <button type="button" class="btn secondary small" data-add-child-step>Add Child</button>
+        <button type="button" class="btn danger small" data-delete-step>Delete</button>
+      </div>
+    </section>
+    <section class="logic-editor-section">
+      <h3>Criteria</h3>
+      ${renderGuidedBuilder(step)}
+    </section>
+    <section class="logic-editor-section">
+      <h3>Notes</h3>
+      <label class="full"><span>Manual Notes</span><textarea data-step-notes-live placeholder="Notes for this step">${esc(step.notes || "")}</textarea></label>
+    </section>
+  </div>`;
+}
+renderSelectedStep = function renderSelectedStepEditorPane() {
+  const step = state.audit.steps.find(
+    (item) => item.id === state.selectedStepId,
+  );
+  const target = e.selectedStepDetails || $("selectedStepDetails");
+  if (!target) return;
+  if (!step) {
+    target.innerHTML =
+      '<p class="hint">Select or create a rule to edit step criteria.</p>';
+    setText?.("selectedRuleBadge", "No rule selected");
+    return;
+  }
+  const number = stepNumberFor(step.id) || "?";
+  setText?.("selectedRuleBadge", `Step ${number}`);
+  target.innerHTML = `<div class="logic-editor-header"><div class="logic-editor-header-main"><span class="badge blue">Step ${esc(number)}</span><strong>${esc(step.title || atlasStepTypeLabel(step))}</strong><span class="small">${esc(atlasStepTypeLabel(step))} · ${step.autoGeneratedReference ? "Generated reference step" : "Editable rule"}</span></div></div><div class="callout logic-editor-generated"><strong>Live Standardised Output</strong><div class="logic-output-text" data-live-generated-output>${renderLogicWithPills(analystLogic(step))}</div></div>${atlasRenderStepEditor(step)}`;
+};
+function renderLogicTablePreviewRows() {
+  const target = $("logicBuilderTableBody");
+  if (!target) return;
+  const entries = atlasVisibleStepEntries();
+  target.innerHTML = entries.length
+    ? entries
+        .map(
+          (entry) =>
+            `<tr class="${state.selectedStepId === entry.step.id ? "selected-row" : ""}" data-table-step-id="${esc(entry.step.id)}"><td>${esc(entry.number)}</td><td>${esc(atlasStepTypeLabel(entry.step))}</td><td>${renderLogicWithPills(analystLogic(entry.step))}</td><td>${entry.step.enabled === false ? '<span class="badge red">Disabled</span>' : '<span class="badge blue">Active</span>'}</td></tr>`,
+        )
+        .join("")
+    : '<tr><td colspan="4" class="hint">No logic steps yet.</td></tr>';
+}
+function atlasRenderHiddenPreserveFields(step) {
+  return `<div class="logic-rule-hidden-fields"><input type="hidden" data-step-parent value="${esc(step.parentId || "")}"><input type="hidden" data-step-branch value="${esc(step.branchType || (step.parentId ? "then" : "root"))}"><input type="hidden" data-step-title value="${esc(step.title || atlasStepTypeLabel(step))}"><input type="hidden" data-step-type value="${esc(step.step_type || "")}"><input type="hidden" data-step-source value="${esc(step.source || "")}"><input type="hidden" data-step-field value="${esc(step.field || "")}"><input type="hidden" data-step-action value="${esc(step.action || "")}"><textarea data-step-notes>${esc(step.notes || "")}</textarea><input type="checkbox" data-step-enabled ${step.enabled !== false ? "checked" : ""}></div>`;
+}
+function renderLogicRuleCard(entry) {
+  const step = entry.step;
+  const selected = state.selectedStepId === step.id;
+  const generated = !!step.autoGeneratedReference;
+  const cardClass = `logic-rule-card step-card ${generated ? "generated-reference-step" : "visual-step-card"} ${selected ? "selected" : ""}`;
+  const draggable = generated ? "" : ' draggable="true"';
+  const title = generated ? "Reference Source" : atlasStepTypeLabel(step);
+  const sourceName = generated
+    ? step.source || step.params?.source_file || "Selected source"
+    : "";
+  const sourceAlias = generated
+    ? step.params?.source_alias || sourceAliasFor?.(sourceName) || sourceName
+    : "";
+  const referenceContext = generated
+    ? `<div class="logic-source-reference-layout" aria-label="Source and generated reference relationship">
+        <div class="logic-source-reference-item logic-source-reference-source"><span class="logic-source-reference-label">Source</span><strong>${esc(sourceName)}</strong></div>
+        <span class="logic-source-reference-connector" aria-hidden="true">→</span>
+        <div class="logic-source-reference-item logic-source-reference-generated"><span class="logic-source-reference-label">Reference Source</span><strong>${esc(sourceAlias)}</strong><span class="small">Generated from the selected source file</span></div>
+      </div>`
+    : "";
+  const status =
+    step.enabled === false
+      ? '<span class="badge red">Disabled</span>'
+      : generated
+        ? '<span class="badge blue">Generated</span>'
+        : '<span class="badge green">Editable</span>';
+  return `<div class="${cardClass}" data-id="${esc(step.id)}" data-parent-id="${esc(step.parentId || "")}" style="--depth:${entry.level}"${draggable}>
+    <div class="logic-rule-title-row">
+      <div class="logic-rule-title-main"><span class="drag-handle" title="Drag to reorder">⋮⋮</span><span class="step-number">${esc(entry.number)}</span><span class="logic-rule-title-text"><strong>${esc(title)}</strong><span class="small">${esc(entry.branchLabel)}${step.title && step.title !== title ? ` · ${esc(step.title)}` : ""}</span></span></div>
+      <div class="step-meta">${status}</div>
+    </div>
+    ${referenceContext}
+    <div class="logic-rule-summary">${renderLogicWithPills(analystLogic(step))}</div>
+    <div class="logic-rule-actions">
+      <button type="button" class="btn ghost small" data-select-step>Edit</button>
+      ${generated ? "" : '<button type="button" class="btn secondary small" data-indent>Indent</button><button type="button" class="btn secondary small" data-outdent>Outdent</button><button type="button" class="btn secondary small" data-add-child-step>Add Child</button><button type="button" class="btn danger small" data-delete-step>Delete</button>'}
+    </div>
+    ${atlasRenderHiddenPreserveFields(step)}
+  </div>`;
+}
+renderSteps = function renderStepsEditorRedesign() {
+  state.audit.steps.forEach((step, index) =>
+    normalizeCatalogStep?.(step, index),
+  );
+  const entries = atlasVisibleStepEntries();
+  e.stepsList.innerHTML = entries
+    .map((entry) => renderLogicRuleCard(entry))
+    .join("");
+  renderSelectedStep();
+  renderLogicTablePreviewRows();
+};
+const atlasPreviousSetPickerTargetValue =
+  typeof setPickerTargetValue === "function" ? setPickerTargetValue : null;
+setPickerTargetValue = function setPickerTargetValueEditorAware(
+  value,
+  sourceFile = "",
+) {
+  const target = visualPickerState?.target;
+  if (!target) return;
+  const container =
+    target.closest(".step-card") ||
+    target.closest(".guided-condition-row") ||
+    target.closest("[data-live-editor-step-id]");
+  if (visualPickerState.targetType === "condition") {
+    const hidden = container?.querySelector(
+      `[data-condition-field="${CSS.escape(visualPickerState.name)}"]`,
+    );
+    if (hidden) hidden.value = value;
+    if (visualPickerState.kind === "field") {
+      const sourceHidden = container?.querySelector(
+        '[data-condition-field="sourceFile"]',
+      );
+      if (sourceHidden && !sourceHidden.value) sourceHidden.value = sourceFile;
+    }
+  } else {
+    const hidden = container?.querySelector(
+      `[data-param="${CSS.escape(visualPickerState.name)}"]`,
+    );
+    if (hidden) hidden.value = value;
+    if (visualPickerState.kind === "field") {
+      const sourceHidden = container?.querySelector(
+        '[data-param="source_file"]',
+      );
+      if (sourceHidden && !sourceHidden.value) sourceHidden.value = sourceFile;
+    }
+  }
+  visualPickerState = null;
+  document
+    .querySelector(".visual-picker-modal-backdrop.dynamic-mini-picker")
+    ?.remove();
+  atlasCommitLiveEditor({ rerender: true });
+};
+document.addEventListener(
+  "change",
+  (event) => {
+    const root = event.target.closest("[data-live-editor-step-id]");
+    if (!root) return;
+    atlasCommitLiveEditor({
+      rerender: event.target.matches(
+        '[data-live-step-type-select], [data-live-parent], [data-live-branch], [data-param="source_file"], [data-condition-field="sourceFile"], [data-condition-field="valueMode"]',
+      ),
+    });
+  },
+  true,
+);
+document.addEventListener(
+  "input",
+  (event) => {
+    const root = event.target.closest("[data-live-editor-step-id]");
+    if (!root) return;
+    atlasCommitLiveEditor({ rerender: false });
+  },
+  true,
+);
+document.addEventListener(
+  "click",
+  (event) => {
+    const card = event.target.closest(".logic-rule-card");
+    if (card && !event.target.closest("button, input, select, textarea, a")) {
+      state.selectedStepId = card.dataset.id;
+      renderSteps();
+      return;
+    }
+    const tableRow = event.target.closest("[data-table-step-id]");
+    if (tableRow) {
+      state.selectedStepId = tableRow.dataset.tableStepId;
+      renderSteps();
+    }
+  },
+  true,
+);
+
+// ATLAS-MATH-OPERATIONS-UPDATE:BEGIN
+const atlasCaptureGuidedFieldsBeforeMathUpdate = atlasCaptureGuidedFields;
+atlasCaptureGuidedFields = function atlasCaptureGuidedFieldsWithMath(
+  root,
+  step,
+) {
+  root.querySelectorAll("[data-math-editor]").forEach((editor) => {
+    const hidden = editor.querySelector("[data-math-json]");
+    if (hidden)
+      hidden.value = atlasSerializeMathOperations(
+        atlasMathOperationsFromContainer(editor),
+      );
+  });
+  atlasCaptureGuidedFieldsBeforeMathUpdate(root, step);
+  const conditionRows = [...root.querySelectorAll(".guided-condition-row")];
+  if (!conditionRows.length) return;
+  const conditions = conditionRows.map((row, index) => {
+    const get = (field) =>
+      row.querySelector(`[data-condition-field="${field}"]`)?.value || "";
+    const sourceFile = get("sourceFile");
+    const compareSourceFile = get("compareSourceFile") || sourceFile;
+    const mathEditor = row.querySelector('[data-math-param="compareMathJson"]');
+    const compareMathJson = mathEditor
+      ? atlasSerializeMathOperations(
+          atlasMathOperationsFromContainer(mathEditor),
+        )
+      : get("compareMathJson");
+    return {
+      id: row.dataset.conditionId || uid("cond"),
+      connector: index === 0 ? "IF" : get("connector") || "AND",
+      conditionType: "row_compare",
+      sourceFile,
+      sourceAlias: sourceAliasFor(sourceFile),
+      fieldName: get("fieldName"),
+      operator: get("operator") || "is equal to",
+      valueMode: get("valueMode") || "values",
+      compareValue: get("compareValue"),
+      compareSourceFile,
+      compareSourceAlias: sourceAliasFor(compareSourceFile),
+      compareFieldName: get("compareFieldName"),
+      expression: get("expression"),
+      compareMathJson,
+    };
+  });
+  atlasWriteLiveEditorConditions(step, conditions);
+};
+// ATLAS-MATH-OPERATIONS-UPDATE:END
+
+// Phase 3: list-driven mutations use the explicit render invalidation contract.
+document.addEventListener("click", (event) => {
+  const mutation = event.target.closest(
+    "[data-add-child-step], [data-delete-step], [data-indent], [data-outdent], [data-duplicate-step], [data-add-step]",
+  );
+  if (!mutation) return;
+  queueMicrotask(() => atlasInvalidateStepMutation?.({ immediate: true }));
+});

+================================================================================
+END FILE: js/15d-logic-rules-list.js

+================================================================================
+BEGIN FILE: js/15e-logic-step-editor.js

+/**
+ * 15e-logic-step-editor.js
+ * Split from 15-visual-ui-upgrades.js.
+ * Compact rule cards, selected-source picker restrictions, hierarchy actions, and rule menus.
+ * IMPORTANT: classic browser script; preserve the 15a-15g load order.
+ */
+/* Compact Rules + selected-source editor/picker refinement */
+let atlasFieldPickerSortMode = "source";
+
+function atlasSelectedAuditSources() {
+  const catalogSources = sourceCatalogEntries();
+  return (state.audit.sources || []).map((selectedSource) => {
+    const identity = atlasResolveSourceIdentity(selectedSource, catalogSources);
+    const catalogSource = identity.ambiguous
+      ? {}
+      : identity.catalogSource || {};
+    return {
+      ...catalogSource,
+      ...selectedSource,
+      sourceKey: selectedSource.sourceKey || identity.sourceKey,
+      sourceFile:
+        selectedSource.sourceFile ||
+        identity.sourceFile ||
+        selectedSource.name ||
+        selectedSource.sourceKey ||
+        "",
+      alias: selectedSource.alias || identity.alias,
+      fields: catalogSource.fields?.length
+        ? catalogSource.fields
+        : selectedSource.fields || [],
+    };
+  });
+}
+
+function atlasNormalizedSourceIdentity(value) {
+  return String(value || "")
+    .trim()
+    .toLowerCase();
+}
+
+function atlasSourceIdentityCandidates(source) {
+  if (!source) return [];
+  if (typeof source === "string") {
+    const normalized = atlasNormalizedSourceIdentity(source);
+    return normalized ? [normalized] : [];
+  }
+  return [
+    source.sourceReferenceId,
+    source.sourceKey,
+    source.baseSourceKey,
+    source.id,
+    source.name,
+    source.sourceFile,
+    source.alias,
+  ]
+    .map(atlasNormalizedSourceIdentity)
+    .filter(Boolean)
+    .filter((value, index, values) => values.indexOf(value) === index);
+}
+
+function atlasResolveSelectedSource(sourceLike) {
+  const selectedSources = atlasSelectedAuditSources();
+  const input =
+    typeof sourceLike === "string" ? { name: sourceLike } : sourceLike || {};
+  const stableKeys = [
+    input.sourceReferenceId,
+    input.sourceKey,
+    input.baseSourceKey,
+    input.id,
+  ]
+    .map(atlasNormalizedSourceIdentity)
+    .filter(Boolean);
+  const stableMatches = selectedSources.filter((source) => {
+    const sourceStableKeys = [
+      source.sourceReferenceId,
+      source.sourceKey,
+      source.baseSourceKey,
+      source.id,
+    ]
+      .map(atlasNormalizedSourceIdentity)
+      .filter(Boolean);
+    return stableKeys.some((key) => sourceStableKeys.includes(key));
+  });
+  if (stableMatches.length === 1) return stableMatches[0];
+  if (stableMatches.length > 1) return null;
+  const fallbackKeys = [input.name, input.sourceFile, input.alias]
+    .map(atlasNormalizedSourceIdentity)
+    .filter(Boolean);
+  const fallbackMatches = selectedSources.filter((source) => {
+    const sourceFallbackKeys = [source.name, source.sourceFile, source.alias]
+      .map(atlasNormalizedSourceIdentity)
+      .filter(Boolean);
+    return fallbackKeys.some((key) => sourceFallbackKeys.includes(key));
+  });
+  return fallbackMatches.length === 1 ? fallbackMatches[0] : null;
+}
+
+function atlasSourceStableKey(sourceLike) {
+  const source = atlasResolveSelectedSource(sourceLike) || sourceLike || {};
+  if (typeof source === "string") return atlasNormalizedSourceIdentity(source);
+  return String(
+    source.sourceReferenceId ||
+      source.sourceKey ||
+      source.baseSourceKey ||
+      source.id ||
+      source.sourceFile ||
+      source.name ||
+      "",
+  ).trim();
+}
+
+function atlasEnsureAuditSourceColors() {
+  const sources = state.audit.sources || [];
+  const palette =
+    Array.isArray(SOURCE_COLOR_PALETTE) && SOURCE_COLOR_PALETTE.length
+      ? SOURCE_COLOR_PALETTE
+      : ["#4d6fa9", "#b54b4b", "#2e7d5b", "#a66b11", "#7c5aa6"];
+  const used = new Set(sources.map((source) => source.color).filter(Boolean));
+  sources.forEach((source) => {
+    if (source.color) return;
+    source.color =
+      palette.find((color) => !used.has(color)) ||
+      palette[sources.indexOf(source) % palette.length];
+    used.add(source.color);
+  });
+  return sources;
+}
+
+function atlasSelectedSourceColor(sourceLike) {
+  atlasEnsureAuditSourceColors();
+  return atlasResolveSelectedSource(sourceLike)?.color || "";
+}
+
+function atlasSelectedSourceAlias(sourceLike) {
+  const source = atlasResolveSelectedSource(sourceLike);
+  const sourceFile =
+    source?.sourceFile ||
+    source?.name ||
+    (typeof sourceLike === "string"
+      ? sourceLike
+      : sourceLike?.sourceFile || "");
+  return source?.alias || sourceAliasFor?.(sourceFile) || sourceFile;
+}
+
+function atlasSourceOptionsHtml(selected = "") {
+  const sources = atlasSelectedAuditSources();
+  if (!sources.length)
+    return '<option value="">Select sources on the Fields page first...</option>';
+  return [
+    '<option value="">Select source file...</option>',
+    ...sources.map(
+      (source) =>
+        `<option value="${esc(source.sourceFile)}" ${selected === source.sourceFile ? "selected" : ""}>${esc(source.sourceFile)}${source.alias && source.alias !== source.sourceFile ? ` (${esc(source.alias)})` : ""}</option>`,
+    ),
+  ].join("");
+}
+
+function atlasFieldOptionsHtml(sourceFile = "", selected = "") {
+  const sources = sourceFile
+    ? atlasSelectedAuditSources().filter(
+        (source) => source.sourceFile === sourceFile,
+      )
+    : atlasSelectedAuditSources();
+  const fields = sources.flatMap((source) =>
+    (source.fields || []).map((field) => ({ source, field })),
+  );
+  if (!fields.length)
+    return '<option value="">Select a selected source first...</option>';
+  return [
+    '<option value="">Select field...</option>',
+    ...fields.map(({ source, field }) => {
+      const parts = fieldDisplayParts(field, source.sourceFile);
+      const displayWithAlternate = parts.alternate
+        ? `${parts.display} (${parts.alternate})`
+        : parts.display;
+      const optionLabel = sourceFile
+        ? displayWithAlternate
+        : `${source.sourceFile}.${displayWithAlternate}`;
+      return `<option value="${esc(parts.raw)}" data-source-file="${esc(source.sourceFile)}" ${selected === parts.raw || selected === parts.vanity ? "selected" : ""}>${esc(optionLabel)}</option>`;
+    }),
+  ].join("");
+}
+
+function atlasIsCustomField(field) {
+  if (!field) return false;
+  const category = String(
+    field.dataCategory || field.category || field.type || field.fieldType || "",
+  ).toLowerCase();
+  return !!(
+    field.calculated ||
+    field.created ||
+    field.custom ||
+    category.includes("calculated") ||
+    category.includes("custom column") ||
+    category.includes("created column")
+  );
+}
+
+renderMiniPickerTrigger = function atlasSourceAwareMiniPickerTrigger(
+  kind,
+  name,
+  value,
+  label,
+  sourceFile = "",
+) {
+  const sourceLike = kind === "field" ? sourceFile : value;
+  const source = atlasResolveSelectedSource(sourceLike);
+  const color = source ? atlasSelectedSourceColor(source) : "";
+  const sourceKey = source ? atlasSourceStableKey(source) : "";
+  const visibleValue =
+    kind === "field" ? fieldDisplayName(value, sourceFile) : value;
+  const detail =
+    kind === "source"
+      ? source?.alias || sourceAliasFor?.(value) || ""
+      : sourceFile
+        ? `${atlasSelectedSourceAlias(sourceFile)}.${value || "[field]"}`
+        : value || "";
+  const empty = !value;
+  return `<button type="button" class="visual-picker-trigger-button ${kind}-token ${empty ? "empty" : ""}" data-open-mini-picker="${esc(kind)}" data-param-name="${esc(name)}" data-picker-source="${esc(sourceFile)}" data-source-key="${esc(sourceKey)}" style="${color ? `--source-color:${esc(color)}` : ""}">${color ? '<span class="source-color-dot" aria-hidden="true"></span>' : ""}<span class="visual-picker-trigger-copy"><strong>${esc(visibleValue || label || "Choose...")}</strong>${detail ? `<small>${esc(detail)}</small>` : ""}</span><span aria-hidden="true">⌄</span></button>`;
+};
+
+renderConditionPickerTrigger = function atlasSourceAwareConditionPickerTrigger(
+  kind,
+  fieldName,
+  value,
+  label,
+  sourceFile = "",
+) {
+  const selectedSourceFile = kind === "field" && !value ? "" : sourceFile;
+  const sourceLike = kind === "field" ? selectedSourceFile : value;
+  const source = atlasResolveSelectedSource(sourceLike);
+  const color = source ? atlasSelectedSourceColor(source) : "";
+  const visibleValue =
+    kind === "field" ? fieldDisplayName(value, selectedSourceFile) : value;
+  return `<input type="hidden" data-condition-field="${esc(fieldName)}" value="${esc(value || "")}"><button type="button" class="visual-picker-trigger-button ${kind}-token ${value ? "" : "empty"}" data-open-mini-picker="${esc(kind)}" data-condition-name="${esc(fieldName)}" data-picker-source="${esc(selectedSourceFile)}" data-source-key="${esc(source ? atlasSourceStableKey(source) : "")}" style="${color ? `--source-color:${esc(color)}` : ""}">${color ? '<span class="source-color-dot" aria-hidden="true"></span>' : ""}<span class="visual-picker-trigger-copy"><strong>${esc(visibleValue || label || "Choose...")}</strong>${selectedSourceFile ? `<small>${esc(atlasSelectedSourceAlias(selectedSourceFile))}</small>` : ""}</span><span aria-hidden="true">⌄</span></button>`;
+};
+
+sourceInput = function atlasSelectedSourceVisualInput(
+  name,
+  value,
+  placeholder = "Select source file",
+) {
+  return renderMiniPickerTrigger(
+    "source",
+    name,
+    value || "",
+    value || placeholder,
+  );
+};
+fieldInput = function atlasSelectedFieldVisualInput(
+  name,
+  value,
+  placeholder = "Select field",
+  sourceFile = "",
+) {
+  const selectedSourceFile = value ? sourceFile || "" : "";
+  return renderMiniPickerTrigger(
+    "field",
+    name,
+    value || "",
+    value || placeholder,
+    selectedSourceFile,
+  );
+};
+
+function pickerSourceOptions() {
+  return atlasSelectedAuditSources().map((source) => ({
+    sourceFile: source.sourceFile,
+    sourceAlias: source.alias || source.sourceFile,
+    fieldName: "",
+    label: source.sourceFile,
+    detail: source.alias || source.sourceFile,
+    sourceColor: atlasSelectedSourceColor(source),
+    sourceKey: atlasSourceStableKey(source),
+  }));
+}
+
+function pickerFieldOptions(sourceFile = "") {
+  const sources = sourceFile
+    ? atlasSelectedAuditSources().filter(
+        (source) => source.sourceFile === sourceFile,
+      )
+    : atlasSelectedAuditSources();
+  return sources.flatMap((source) =>
+    (source.fields || []).map((field) => {
+      const parts = fieldDisplayParts(field, source.sourceFile);
+      return {
+        sourceFile: source.sourceFile,
+        sourceAlias: source.alias || source.sourceFile,
+        fieldName: parts.raw,
+        rawFieldName: parts.raw,
+        vanityLabel: parts.vanity,
+        label: parts.display,
+        detail: parts.alternate,
+        sourceColor: atlasSelectedSourceColor(source),
+        sourceKey: atlasSourceStableKey(source),
+        customField: atlasIsCustomField(field),
+      };
+    }),
+  );
+}
+
+function atlasSortedPickerItems(items) {
+  const list = [...items];
+  if (atlasFieldPickerSortMode === "alpha") {
+    return list.sort((a, b) =>
+      `${a.label} ${a.fieldName}`.localeCompare(
+        `${b.label} ${b.fieldName}`,
+        undefined,
+        { sensitivity: "base", numeric: true },
+      ),
+    );
+  }
+  return list.sort((a, b) =>
+    `${a.sourceAlias} ${a.label} ${a.fieldName}`.localeCompare(
+      `${b.sourceAlias} ${b.label} ${b.fieldName}`,
+      undefined,
+      { sensitivity: "base", numeric: true },
+    ),
+  );
+}
+
+function renderMiniPicker() {
+  document
+    .querySelector(".visual-picker-modal-backdrop.dynamic-mini-picker")
+    ?.remove();
+  if (!visualPickerState) return;
+  atlasEnsureAuditSourceColors();
+  const options =
+    visualPickerState.kind === "source"
+      ? pickerSourceOptions()
+      : pickerFieldOptions(visualPickerState.sourceFile);
+  const query = visualPickerState.query || "";
+  const filtered = atlasSortedPickerItems(
+    options.filter((option) =>
+      `${option.sourceFile} ${option.sourceAlias} ${option.fieldName} ${option.label}`
+        .toLowerCase()
+        .includes(query.toLowerCase()),
+    ),
+  );
+  const groups = new Map();
+  filtered.forEach((option) => {
+    const group =
+      atlasFieldPickerSortMode === "alpha"
+        ? firstLetter(option.label || option.fieldName || option.sourceFile)
+        : visualPickerState.kind === "source"
+          ? "Selected Sources"
+          : option.sourceFile;
+    if (!groups.has(group)) groups.set(group, []);
+    groups.get(group).push(option);
+  });
+
+  const selectedValue = String(
+    visualPickerState.target?.dataset?.currentValue ||
+      visualPickerState.target?.querySelector?.("strong")?.textContent ||
+      "",
+  ).trim();
+  const groupMarkup = [...groups.entries()]
+    .map(([group, items]) => {
+      const rows = items
+        .map((item) => {
+          const isSource = visualPickerState.kind === "source";
+          const value = isSource ? item.sourceFile : item.fieldName;
+          const selected = value === selectedValue;
+          const primary = isSource ? item.sourceAlias : item.label;
+          const secondary = isSource
+            ? item.sourceFile
+            : `${item.sourceAlias || item.sourceFile}.${item.fieldName}`;
+          const classes = [
+            "visual-picker-option",
+            isSource ? "source-picker-option" : "field-picker-option",
+            item.customField ? "custom-field-pill" : "",
+            selected ? "selected" : "",
+          ]
+            .filter(Boolean)
+            .join(" ");
+          return `<button type="button" class="${classes}" data-mini-picker-value="${esc(value)}" data-mini-picker-source="${esc(item.sourceFile)}" data-source-key="${esc(item.sourceKey || atlasSourceStableKey(item.sourceFile))}" style="--source-color:${esc(item.sourceColor)}" aria-pressed="${selected ? "true" : "false"}"><span class="source-color-dot" aria-hidden="true"></span><span class="visual-picker-option-copy"><strong>${esc(primary)}</strong><small>${esc(secondary)}</small></span>${selected ? '<span class="selection-status">Selected</span>' : ""}</button>`;
+        })
+        .join("");
+      return `<section class="visual-picker-group"><div class="visual-picker-group-title"><strong>${esc(group)}</strong><span class="badge blue">${items.length}</span></div><div class="visual-picker-option-list">${rows}</div></section>`;
+    })
+    .join("");
+
+  const root = document.createElement("div");
+  root.className = "visual-picker-modal-backdrop dynamic-mini-picker";
+  root.innerHTML = `<div class="visual-picker-modal"><div class="visual-picker-modal-header"><div><h2 class="visual-picker-modal-title">${visualPickerState.kind === "source" ? "Choose Selected Source File" : "Choose Field"}</h2><p class="visual-picker-modal-subtitle">Only sources selected for this audit are available here.</p></div><button type="button" class="visual-picker-close-button" data-close-mini-picker aria-label="Close picker">×</button></div><div class="visual-picker-search-row"><input data-mini-picker-search value="${esc(query)}" placeholder="Search selected sources and fields..."><div class="mini-picker-sort-toggle"><button type="button" class="secondary small ${atlasFieldPickerSortMode === "source" ? "active" : ""}" data-picker-sort="source">Sort by Source</button><button type="button" class="secondary small ${atlasFieldPickerSortMode === "alpha" ? "active" : ""}" data-picker-sort="alpha">A-Z</button></div></div><div class="visual-picker-content">${groupMarkup || '<div class="contacts-empty">No selected source fields match the current search.</div>'}</div></div>`;
+  document.body.append(root);
+  root.querySelector("[data-mini-picker-search]")?.focus();
+}
+// Phase 2: picker commit behavior is centralized in 15g.
+
+function atlasFieldMatchForSource(value, sourceLike) {
+  const normalized = String(value || "").trim();
+  const source = atlasSelectedAuditSources().find(
+    (candidate) =>
+      atlasSourceStableKey(candidate) === atlasSourceStableKey(sourceLike),
+  );
+  if (!source) return null;
+  const field = (source.fields || []).find((item) => {
+    const parts = fieldDisplayParts(item, source.sourceFile);
+    return [
+      parts.raw,
+      parts.vanity,
+      parts.display,
+      `${source.sourceFile}.${parts.raw}`,
+      `${source.alias || source.sourceFile}.${parts.raw}`,
+    ].includes(normalized);
+  });
+  return field
+    ? {
+        sourceFile: source.sourceFile,
+        sourceKey: atlasSourceStableKey(source),
+        color: atlasSelectedSourceColor(source),
+        kind: atlasIsCustomField(field) ? "custom" : "field",
+        field,
+      }
+    : null;
+}
+
+function atlasSourceMatchForValue(value, preferredSource = null) {
+  const normalized = String(value || "").trim();
+  if (!normalized) return null;
+  const direct = atlasResolveSelectedSource(normalized);
+  if (direct) {
+    return {
+      sourceFile: direct.sourceFile || direct.name,
+      sourceKey: atlasSourceStableKey(direct),
+      color: atlasSelectedSourceColor(direct),
+      kind: "source",
+    };
+  }
+  if (preferredSource) {
+    const preferredField = atlasFieldMatchForSource(
+      normalized,
+      preferredSource,
+    );
+    if (preferredField) return preferredField;
+  }
+  for (const source of atlasSelectedAuditSources()) {
+    const fieldMatch = atlasFieldMatchForSource(normalized, source);
+    if (fieldMatch) return fieldMatch;
+  }
+  return null;
+}
+
+renderLogicWithPills = function atlasRenderLogicWithSourceAndFieldPills(text) {
+  const parts = String(text || "").split(/("[^"]+")/g);
+  let activeSource = null;
+  return parts
+    .map((part) => {
+      if (!part.startsWith('"') || !part.endsWith('"')) return esc(part);
+      const value = part.slice(1, -1);
+      const match = atlasSourceMatchForValue(value, activeSource);
+      if (match?.kind === "source") activeSource = match;
+      const klass =
+        match?.kind === "custom"
+          ? "logic-pill custom-field-token"
+          : match?.kind === "field"
+            ? "logic-pill field-pill-token field-token"
+            : "logic-pill source-pill source-token";
+      const style = match
+        ? ` style="--pill-bg:${esc(match.color)};--pill-color:${esc(match.color)};--source-color:${esc(match.color)}"`
+        : "";
+      const dot = match
+        ? `<span class="source-color-dot" aria-hidden="true"></span>`
+        : "";
+      const displayValue =
+        match?.kind === "field" || match?.kind === "custom"
+          ? fieldDisplayParts(match.field || value, match.sourceFile).display
+          : value;
+      return `<span class="${klass}" data-source-key="${esc(match?.sourceKey || "")}"${style}>${dot}${esc(displayValue)}</span>`;
+    })
+    .join("");
+};
+
+function atlasActionAvailability(step) {
+  const reference =
+    !!step?.autoGeneratedReference || step?.step_type === "reference_source";
+  return {
+    edit: !reference,
+    indent: !reference,
+    outdent: !reference && !!step?.parentId,
+    addChild: true,
+    addAfter: !reference,
+    duplicate: !reference,
+    delete: !reference,
+  };
+}
+
+function atlasAddStepAfter(stepId) {
+  capture();
+  const index = state.audit.steps.findIndex((step) => step.id === stepId);
+  const current = state.audit.steps[index];
+  const nextStep = {
+    id: uid("step"),
+    enabled: true,
+    parentId: current?.parentId || null,
+    branchType: current?.parentId ? current.branchType || "then" : "root",
+    sortOrder: (Number(current?.sortOrder) || index + 1) + 0.1,
+    title: "IF / THEN",
+    type: "if",
+    step_type: "if_then",
+    source: current?.source || state.audit.sources?.[0]?.name || "",
+    field: "",
+    action: "",
+    notes: "",
+    conditions: [
+      { id: uid("cond"), field: "", operator: "is equal to", value: "" },
+    ],
+    params: {},
+  };
+  if (index >= 0) state.audit.steps.splice(index + 1, 0, nextStep);
+  else state.audit.steps.push(nextStep);
+  state.audit.steps.forEach((step, order) => {
+    if (!step.autoGeneratedReference) step.sortOrder = order + 1;
+  });
+  state.selectedStepId = nextStep.id;
+  renderAll();
+  scheduleAutosave();
+}
+
+function atlasDuplicateStepById(stepId) {
+  capture();
+  const step = state.audit.steps.find((item) => item.id === stepId);
+  if (!step || step.autoGeneratedReference) return;
+  const copy = structuredClone(step);
+  copy.id = uid("step");
+  copy.autoGeneratedReference = false;
+  copy.conditions = (copy.conditions || []).map((condition) => ({
+    ...condition,
+    id: uid("cond"),
+  }));
+  const index = state.audit.steps.indexOf(step);
+  state.audit.steps.splice(index + 1, 0, copy);
+  state.audit.steps.forEach((item, order) => {
+    if (!item.autoGeneratedReference) item.sortOrder = order + 1;
+  });
+  state.selectedStepId = copy.id;
+  renderAll();
+  scheduleAutosave();
+}
+
+function renderLogicRuleCard(entry) {
+  const step = entry.step;
+  const selected = state.selectedStepId === step.id;
+  const generated = !!step.autoGeneratedReference;
+  const linkedInverse =
+    step.params?.inverse_else === "Yes" && !!step.params?.inverse_of_step_id;
+  const availability = atlasActionAvailability(step);
+  const cardClass = `logic-rule-card compact-logic-rule-card step-card ${generated ? "generated-reference-step" : "visual-step-card"} ${linkedInverse ? "linked-inverse-step inverse-else-step" : ""} ${selected ? "selected" : ""}`;
+  const draggable = generated ? "" : ' draggable="true"';
+  const originalStepNumber = linkedInverse
+    ? stepNumberFor?.(step.params.inverse_of_step_id) || "?"
+    : "";
+  const statusLabel = generated
+    ? "Generated"
+    : linkedInverse
+      ? `Linked Inverse of ${originalStepNumber}`
+      : "Editable";
+  const statusClass = generated ? "blue" : linkedInverse ? "amber" : "green";
+  return `<div class="${cardClass}" data-id="${esc(step.id)}" data-parent-id="${esc(step.parentId || "")}" style="--depth:${entry.level}"${draggable}>
+    <div class="compact-rule-grid">
+      <span class="drag-handle compact-dragger" title="Drag to reorder">⋮⋮</span>
+      <span class="step-number compact-step-number">${esc(entry.number)}</span>
+      <div class="compact-rule-logic" data-select-step>${linkedInverse ? `<div class="linked-inverse-subtitle inverse-else-subtitle">Complete inverse of Step ${esc(originalStepNumber)}</div>` : ""}${renderLogicWithPills(analystLogic(step))}</div>
+      <span class="badge ${statusClass} compact-status-badge">${statusLabel}</span>
+      <details class="compact-action-menu"><summary title="Step actions" aria-label="Step actions" aria-haspopup="menu" aria-expanded="false" data-rule-menu-toggle="${esc(step.id)}">⋯</summary><div class="compact-action-menu-panel" role="menu" data-rule-menu-panel="${esc(step.id)}" hidden>
+        ${availability.edit ? '<button type="button" role="menuitem" data-select-step>Edit</button>' : ""}
+        ${availability.indent ? '<button type="button" role="menuitem" data-indent>Indent</button>' : ""}
+        ${availability.outdent ? '<button type="button" role="menuitem" data-outdent>Outdent</button>' : ""}
+        ${
+          availability.addChild
+            ? generated
+              ? '<button type="button" role="menuitem" data-add-reference-child-type="if_then">Add condition</button><button type="button" role="menuitem" data-add-reference-child-type="create_column">Create Column</button>'
+              : '<button type="button" role="menuitem" data-add-child-step>Add Child</button>'
+            : ""
+        }
+        ${step.step_type === "if_then" && !linkedInverse ? `<button type="button" role="menuitem" data-create-inverse-else-step="${esc(step.id)}">Create or Open Linked Inverse</button>` : ""}
+        ${availability.addAfter ? '<button type="button" role="menuitem" data-add-step-after>Add Step After</button>' : ""}
+        ${availability.duplicate ? '<button type="button" role="menuitem" data-duplicate-logic-step>Duplicate</button>' : ""}
+        ${availability.delete ? '<button type="button" role="menuitem" class="danger-menu-action" data-delete-step>Delete</button>' : ""}
+      </div></details>
+    </div>
+    ${atlasRenderHiddenPreserveFields(step)}
+  </div>`;
+}
+
+function atlasRenderFieldSortControl() {
+  return `<div class="field-picker-sort-row"><span class="small">Field picker sort</span><button type="button" class="secondary small ${atlasFieldPickerSortMode === "source" ? "active" : ""}" data-picker-sort="source">By Source</button><button type="button" class="secondary small ${atlasFieldPickerSortMode === "alpha" ? "active" : ""}" data-picker-sort="alpha">A-Z</button></div>`;
+}
+
+const atlasPreviousRenderSelectedStep = renderSelectedStep;
+renderSelectedStep = function renderSelectedStepEditorPaneCompact() {
+  const step = state.audit.steps.find(
+    (item) => item.id === state.selectedStepId,
+  );
+  const target = e.selectedStepDetails || $("selectedStepDetails");
+  if (!target) return;
+  if (!step) {
+    target.innerHTML =
+      '<p class="hint">Select or create a rule to edit step criteria.</p>';
+    setText?.("selectedRuleBadge", "No rule selected");
+    return;
+  }
+  const number = stepNumberFor(step.id) || "?";
+  setText?.("selectedRuleBadge", `Step ${number}`);
+  target.innerHTML = `<div class="logic-editor-header"><div class="logic-editor-header-main"><span class="badge blue">Step ${esc(number)}</span><strong>${esc(step.title || atlasStepTypeLabel(step))}</strong><span class="small">${esc(atlasStepTypeLabel(step))} · ${step.autoGeneratedReference ? "Generated reference step" : "Editable rule"}</span></div></div><div class="callout logic-editor-generated"><strong>Live Standardised Output</strong><div class="logic-output-text" data-live-generated-output>${renderLogicWithPills(analystLogic(step))}</div></div>${atlasRenderFieldSortControl()}${atlasRenderStepEditor(step)}`;
+};
+
+const atlasPreviousRenderGuidedBuilder = renderGuidedBuilder;
+renderGuidedBuilder = function renderGuidedBuilderSelectedSourcesOnly(step) {
+  normalizeCatalogStep(step);
+  const html = atlasPreviousRenderGuidedBuilder(step);
+  return `<div class="selected-source-only-builder">${html}</div>`;
+};
+
+function atlasCloseRuleMenus(exceptId = "") {
+  document.querySelectorAll("[data-rule-menu-panel]").forEach((panel) => {
+    const keepOpen = !!exceptId && panel.dataset.ruleMenuPanel === exceptId;
+    panel.hidden = !keepOpen;
+
+    const details = panel.closest("details.compact-action-menu");
+    if (details) details.open = keepOpen;
+
+    const toggle = details?.querySelector("[data-rule-menu-toggle]");
+    toggle?.setAttribute("aria-expanded", keepOpen ? "true" : "false");
+  });
+}
+
+document.addEventListener(
+  "click",
+  (event) => {
+    const menuToggle = event.target.closest("[data-rule-menu-toggle]");
+    if (menuToggle) {
+      event.preventDefault();
+      event.stopImmediatePropagation();
+
+      const ruleId = menuToggle.dataset.ruleMenuToggle || "";
+      if (!ruleId) return;
+
+      const panel = document.querySelector(
+        `[data-rule-menu-panel="${CSS.escape(ruleId)}"]`,
+      );
+      if (!panel) return;
+
+      atlasCloseRuleMenus(panel.hidden ? ruleId : "");
+      return;
+    }
+
+    if (!event.target.closest("[data-rule-menu-panel]")) {
+      atlasCloseRuleMenus();
+    }
+
+    const sort = event.target.closest("[data-picker-sort]");
+    if (sort) {
+      atlasFieldPickerSortMode = sort.dataset.pickerSort || "source";
+      if (visualPickerState) renderMiniPicker();
+      else renderSelectedStep();
+      return;
+    }
+    const after = event.target.closest("[data-add-step-after]");
+    if (after) {
+      const card = after.closest(".step-card");
+      if (card) atlasAddStepAfter(card.dataset.id);
+      atlasCloseRuleMenus();
+      return;
+    }
+    const duplicate = event.target.closest("[data-duplicate-logic-step]");
+    if (duplicate) {
+      const card = duplicate.closest(".step-card");
+      if (card) atlasDuplicateStepById(card.dataset.id);
+      atlasCloseRuleMenus();
+      return;
+    }
+  },
+  true,
+);
+
+document.addEventListener(
+  "keydown",
+  (event) => {
+    if (event.key !== "Escape") return;
+
+    const openPanel = [
+      ...document.querySelectorAll("[data-rule-menu-panel]"),
+    ].find((panel) => !panel.hidden);
+    if (!openPanel) return;
+
+    const ruleId = openPanel.dataset.ruleMenuPanel || "";
+    atlasCloseRuleMenus();
+    document
+      .querySelector(`[data-rule-menu-toggle="${CSS.escape(ruleId)}"]`)
+      ?.focus();
+  },
+  true,
+);
+
+if (typeof sourceOptionsHtml === "function") {
+  sourceOptionsHtml = function atlasSelectedSourceOptionsHtmlForGuidedBuilders(
+    selected = "",
+  ) {
+    return atlasSourceOptionsHtml(selected || "");
+  };
+}
+
+if (typeof fieldOptionsHtml === "function") {
+  fieldOptionsHtml = function atlasSelectedFieldOptionsHtmlForGuidedBuilders(
+    sourceFile = "",
+    selected = "",
+  ) {
+    return atlasFieldOptionsHtml(sourceFile || "", selected || "");
+  };
+}
+
+/* Restored Reference sub-step behavior. */
+function atlasInheritedReferenceSourceForStep(step) {
+  let parent = stepById?.(step?.parentId);
+  while (parent) {
+    if (
+      parent.autoGeneratedReference ||
+      parent.step_type === "reference_source"
+    )
+      return parent.source || parent.params?.source_file || "";
+    parent = stepById?.(parent.parentId);
+  }
+  return "";
+}
+function atlasNormalizeReferenceSubstep(step) {
+  const inherited = atlasInheritedReferenceSourceForStep(step);
+  if (!inherited || !step || step.autoGeneratedReference) return inherited;
+  const alias =
+    atlasSelectedSourceAlias?.(inherited) ||
+    sourceAliasFor?.(inherited) ||
+    inherited;
+  step.source = inherited;
+  step.params =
+    step.params && typeof step.params === "object" ? step.params : {};
+  step.params.source_file = inherited;
+  step.params.source_alias = alias;
+  const conditions = (parseStepConditions?.(step) || []).map((condition) => ({
+    ...condition,
+    sourceFile: inherited,
+    sourceAlias: alias,
+    compareSourceFile: condition.compareSourceFile || inherited,
+    compareSourceAlias:
+      condition.compareSourceAlias ||
+      sourceAliasFor?.(condition.compareSourceFile || inherited) ||
+      alias,
+  }));
+  if (conditions.length) atlasWriteLiveEditorConditions?.(step, conditions);
+  return inherited;
+}
+function atlasAddReferenceChildStep(referenceStep, requestedType = "if_then") {
+  const childType = ["if_then", "create_column"].includes(requestedType)
+    ? requestedType
+    : "if_then";
+  const inherited =
+    referenceStep?.source || referenceStep?.params?.source_file || "";
+  if (!inherited) {
+    status?.("Reference source could not be determined.", "warn");
+    return null;
+  }
+  capture?.();
+  const alias =
+    atlasSelectedSourceAlias?.(inherited) ||
+    sourceAliasFor?.(inherited) ||
+    inherited;
+  const child = {
+    id: uid("step"),
+    enabled: true,
+    parentId: referenceStep.id,
+    branchType: "then",
+    sortOrder: childrenOf(referenceStep.id).length + 1,
+    title: childType === "create_column" ? "Create Column" : "IF / THEN",
+    type:
+      legacyTypeFromStepType?.(childType) ||
+      (childType === "create_column" ? "create" : "if"),
+    step_type: childType,
+    source: inherited,
+    field: "",
+    action: "",
+    notes: "",
+    autoGeneratedReference: false,
+    conditions: [],
+    params: {
+      source_file: inherited,
+      source_alias: alias,
+    },
+  };
+  normalizeCatalogStep?.(child);
+  if (childType === "if_then") {
+    const condition = defaultGuidedCondition?.(child) || {
+      id: uid("cond"),
+      connector: "IF",
+      conditionType: "row_compare",
+      sourceFile: inherited,
+      sourceAlias: alias,
+      fieldName: "",
+      operator: "is equal to",
+      valueMode: "values",
+      compareValue: "",
+    };
+    condition.sourceFile = inherited;
+    condition.sourceAlias = alias;
+    atlasWriteLiveEditorConditions?.(child, [condition]);
+    child.params.then_action_type = child.params.then_action_type || "freeform";
+  }
+  state.audit.steps.push(child);
+  atlasNormalizeStepOrderGroups?.();
+  state.selectedStepId = child.id;
+  atlasRequestStructuralStepRender?.();
+  scheduleAutosave?.();
+  status?.(
+    childType === "create_column"
+      ? "Added a Create Column step under the Reference step."
+      : "Added an IF / THEN condition under the Reference step.",
+    "ok",
+  );
+  return child;
+}
+const atlasReferenceParentOptionsBase = parentOptionsForStep;
+parentOptionsForStep = function parentOptionsForStepReferenceJoinGuard(step) {
+  const options = atlasReferenceParentOptionsBase(step);
+  if (step?.step_type !== "join") return options;
+  return options.filter(
+    (option) =>
+      !option.id ||
+      !(
+        stepById?.(option.id)?.autoGeneratedReference ||
+        stepById?.(option.id)?.step_type === "reference_source"
+      ),
+  );
+};
+const atlasReferenceSetParentBase = setStepParent;
+setStepParent = function setStepParentReferenceJoinGuard(step, parentId) {
+  const parent = parentId ? stepById?.(parentId) : null;
+  if (
+    step?.step_type === "join" &&
+    parent &&
+    (parent.autoGeneratedReference || parent.step_type === "reference_source")
+  ) {
+    status?.(
+      "Join / Union steps must be top-level and cannot be Reference sub-steps.",
+      "warn",
+    );
+    return false;
+  }
+  const changed = atlasReferenceSetParentBase(step, parentId);
+  if (changed) atlasNormalizeReferenceSubstep(step);
+  return changed;
+};
+document.addEventListener(
+  "click",
+  (event) => {
+    const referenceControl = event.target.closest(
+      "[data-add-reference-child-type]",
+    );
+    const control =
+      referenceControl || event.target.closest("[data-add-child-step]");
+    if (!control) return;
+    const step = stepById?.(control.closest(".step-card")?.dataset.id || "");
+    if (!step) return;
+    event.preventDefault();
+    event.stopImmediatePropagation();
+    if (referenceControl) {
+      if (step.autoGeneratedReference || step.step_type === "reference_source")
+        atlasAddReferenceChildStep(
+          step,
+          referenceControl.dataset.addReferenceChildType || "if_then",
+        );
+    } else {
+      addChildStep(step);
+    }
+    atlasCloseRuleMenus?.();
+  },
+  true,
+);
+const atlasReferenceCommitBase = atlasCommitLiveEditor;
+atlasCommitLiveEditor = function atlasCommitLiveEditorReferenceContext(
+  options = {},
+) {
+  const stepId =
+    document.querySelector("[data-live-editor-step-id]")?.dataset
+      .liveEditorStepId || state.selectedStepId;
+  const result = atlasReferenceCommitBase(options);
+  const step = stepById?.(stepId);
+  if (step) atlasNormalizeReferenceSubstep(step);
+  return result;
+};
+const atlasReferenceSyncBase = syncAutoReferenceSteps;
+syncAutoReferenceSteps = function syncAutoReferenceStepsWithDescendantCleanup(
+  ...args
+) {
+  const before = (state.audit.steps || []).map((step) => ({
+    id: step.id,
+    parentId: step.parentId || null,
+    reference:
+      !!step.autoGeneratedReference || step.step_type === "reference_source",
+  }));
+  const result = atlasReferenceSyncBase(...args);
+  const remaining = new Set((state.audit.steps || []).map((step) => step.id));
+  const removeIds = new Set(
+    before
+      .filter((step) => step.reference && !remaining.has(step.id))
+      .map((step) => step.id),
+  );
+  let changed = true;
+  while (changed) {
+    changed = false;
+    before.forEach((step) => {
+      if (
+        step.parentId &&
+        removeIds.has(step.parentId) &&
+        !removeIds.has(step.id)
+      ) {
+        removeIds.add(step.id);
+        changed = true;
+      }
+    });
+  }
+  if (removeIds.size)
+    state.audit.steps = (state.audit.steps || []).filter(
+      (step) => !removeIds.has(step.id),
+    );
+  if (state.selectedStepId && removeIds.has(state.selectedStepId))
+    state.selectedStepId = state.audit.steps[0]?.id || null;
+  return result;
+};
+
+// ATLAS-STRING-TRANSFORM-UPDATE:BEGIN
+const atlasSelectedAuditSourcesBeforeStringTransform =
+  atlasSelectedAuditSources;
+atlasSelectedAuditSources =
+  function atlasSelectedAuditSourcesWithCalculatedFields() {
+    const sources = atlasSelectedAuditSourcesBeforeStringTransform();
+    const createdFields = (state.audit.fields || []).filter(
+      (field) => field.calculated || field.created || field.custom,
+    );
+    if (
+      createdFields.length &&
+      !sources.some((source) => source.sourceFile === "Calculated Field")
+    ) {
+      sources.push({
+        sourceFile: "Calculated Field",
+        alias: "Calculated Field",
+        fields: createdFields,
+        sourceKey: "calculated-field",
+      });
+    }
+    return sources;
+  };
+
+// Phase 2: string field/source behavior is handled by explicit commit adapters in 15g.
+// ATLAS-STRING-TRANSFORM-UPDATE:END
+
+// Content-only editor commits must not recreate the selected-step DOM.
+// Structural commits are rendered by the base implementation when rerender is true.
+const atlasDeterministicCommitBase = atlasCommitLiveEditor;
+atlasCommitLiveEditor = function atlasCommitLiveEditorDeterministic(
+  options = {},
+) {
+  return atlasDeterministicCommitBase(options);
+};

+/**
+ * 15f-join-picker.js
+ * Split from 15-visual-ui-upgrades.js.
+ * JOIN type picker, JOIN developer logic, and Venn visualization refinements.
+ * IMPORTANT: classic browser script; preserve the 15a-15g load order.
+ */
+/* JOIN Venn Diagram Picker */
+let atlasJoinPickerState = null;
+
+function atlasJoinTypeDisplayName(joinType) {
+  const normalized = String(joinType || "INNER JOIN").toUpperCase();
+  if (normalized === "LEFT JOIN") return "LEFT JOIN";
+  if (normalized === "RIGHT JOIN") return "RIGHT JOIN";
+  if (normalized === "FULL OUTER JOIN") return "FULL OUTER JOIN";
+  if (normalized === "UNION") return "UNION";
+  return "INNER JOIN";
+}
+
+function atlasJoinKeptRowsDescription(joinType) {
+  const normalized = atlasJoinTypeDisplayName(joinType);
+  if (normalized === "LEFT JOIN")
+    return "All rows from the left source, plus matching rows from the right source.";
+  if (normalized === "RIGHT JOIN")
+    return "All rows from the right source, plus matching rows from the left source.";
+  if (normalized === "FULL OUTER JOIN")
+    return "All rows from both sources, whether matched or unmatched.";
+  if (normalized === "UNION")
+    return "Append rows from both sources into one combined output. Matching keys are not required.";
+  return "Only rows where the left and right sources match.";
+}
+
+function atlasJoinSourceLabel(sourceFile) {
+  if (!sourceFile) return "[source not selected]";
+  const alias = sourceAliasFor?.(sourceFile) || sourceFile;
+  return alias && alias !== sourceFile
+    ? `${alias} (${sourceFile})`
+    : sourceFile;
+}
+
+function atlasJoinDeveloperLogic(step) {
+  normalizeCatalogStep?.(step);
+  const p = step.params || {};
+  const joinType = atlasJoinTypeDisplayName(p.join_type);
+  const leftSource = p.left_source || "[left source]";
+  const rightSource = p.right_source || "[right source]";
+  const leftAlias = sourceAliasFor?.(leftSource) || leftSource;
+  const rightAlias = sourceAliasFor?.(rightSource) || rightSource;
+  if (joinType === "UNION") {
+    const outputSource = p.output_source || step.source || "[combined output]";
+    return `UNION: append rows from ${leftAlias} and ${rightAlias} into ${outputSource}. Matching keys are not required.`;
+  }
+  const leftField = p.left_field || "[left key]";
+  const rightField = p.right_field || "[right key]";
+  return `${joinType}: ${atlasJoinKeptRowsDescription(joinType)} Join ${leftAlias}.${leftField} to ${rightAlias}.${rightField}.`;
+}
+
+function atlasRenderJoinTypePickerButton(step) {
+  const joinType = atlasJoinTypeDisplayName(step.params?.join_type);
+  return `<div class="join-type-trigger"><span>Join Type</span><input type="hidden" data-param="join_type" value="${esc(joinType)}"><div class="join-type-trigger-row"><span class="join-type-value-pill">${esc(joinType)}</span><button type="button" class="btn secondary small" data-open-join-picker="${esc(step.id)}">Choose Join Type</button></div><p class="hint">${esc(atlasJoinKeptRowsDescription(joinType))}</p></div>`;
+}
+
+const atlasJoinPreviousRenderGuidedBuilder = renderGuidedBuilder;
+renderGuidedBuilder = function renderGuidedBuilderWithJoinVenn(step) {
+  normalizeCatalogStep?.(step);
+  if (step.step_type !== "join")
+    return atlasJoinPreviousRenderGuidedBuilder(step);
+  const p = step.params || {};
+  const union = atlasJoinTypeDisplayName(p.join_type) === "UNION";
+  const inputPair = union
+    ? `<div class="logic-picker-pair join-union-input-pair"><label>Left Source${sourceInput("left_source", p.left_source)}</label><label>Right Source${sourceInput("right_source", p.right_source)}</label></div>`
+    : `<div class="logic-picker-pair join-union-input-pair"><input type="hidden" data-param="left_source" value="${esc(p.left_source || "")}"><label>Left Field${fieldInput("left_field", p.left_field, "Select left field")}</label><input type="hidden" data-param="right_source" value="${esc(p.right_source || "")}"><label>Right Field${fieldInput("right_field", p.right_field, "Select right field")}</label></div>`;
+  return `<div class="guided-builder join-guided-builder"><h4>${union ? "Union Sources" : "Join"}</h4>${atlasRenderJoinTypePickerButton(step)}${inputPair}${union ? `<label>Output Source${sourceInput("output_source", p.output_source)}</label><p class="hint join-builder-union-hint">Union appends rows from both files into one combined output. No matching key is needed.</p>` : ""}</div>`;
+};
+
+function atlasOpenJoinPicker(stepId) {
+  const root = document.querySelector("[data-live-editor-step-id]");
+  if (root) atlasCommitLiveEditor?.({ rerender: false });
+  const step = state.audit.steps.find(
+    (item) => item.id === stepId || item.id === state.selectedStepId,
+  );
+  if (!step || step.step_type !== "join") return;
+  normalizeCatalogStep?.(step);
+  atlasJoinPickerState = {
+    stepId: step.id,
+    pendingJoinType: atlasJoinTypeDisplayName(step.params?.join_type),
+  };
+  atlasRenderJoinPickerModal();
+}
+
+function atlasRenderJoinPickerModal() {
+  document.querySelector(".join-picker-backdrop")?.remove();
+  if (!atlasJoinPickerState) return;
+  const step = state.audit.steps.find(
+    (item) => item.id === atlasJoinPickerState.stepId,
+  );
+  if (!step) return;
+  const p = step.params || {};
+  const joinType = atlasJoinTypeDisplayName(
+    atlasJoinPickerState.pendingJoinType,
+  );
+  const leftColor =
+    atlasSelectedSourceColor?.(p.left_source) ||
+    SOURCE_COLOR_PALETTE?.[0] ||
+    "#4d6fa9";
+  const rightColor =
+    atlasSelectedSourceColor?.(p.right_source) ||
+    SOURCE_COLOR_PALETTE?.[1] ||
+    "#b54b4b";
+  const leftLabel = atlasJoinSourceLabel(p.left_source);
+  const rightLabel = atlasJoinSourceLabel(p.right_source);
+  const root = document.createElement("div");
+  root.className = "join-picker-backdrop";
+  root.innerHTML = `<div class="join-picker-modal" role="dialog" aria-modal="true"><div class="join-picker-header"><div><h2 class="join-picker-title">Choose Join Type</h2><p class="hint">Preview which data will be kept before confirming the JOIN type.</p></div><button type="button" class="btn ghost small" data-close-join-picker>Cancel</button></div><div class="join-picker-body"><section class="join-picker-stage"><div class="join-venn-wrap" data-join-type="${esc(joinType)}" style="--join-left-color:${esc(leftColor)};--join-right-color:${esc(rightColor)}"><div class="join-venn-circle join-venn-left"></div><div class="join-venn-circle join-venn-right"></div><div class="join-venn-intersection"></div><div class="join-venn-label left">${esc(leftLabel)}</div><div class="join-venn-label right">${esc(rightLabel)}</div></div><div class="join-picker-option-grid">${["INNER JOIN", "LEFT JOIN", "RIGHT JOIN", "FULL OUTER JOIN"].map((type) => `<button type="button" class="join-picker-option ${type === joinType ? "active" : ""}" data-join-type-option="${esc(type)}">${esc(type)}</button>`).join("")}</div></section><section class="join-picker-details"><div class="join-picker-summary-card"><strong>${esc(joinType)}</strong><p>${esc(atlasJoinKeptRowsDescription(joinType))}</p></div><div class="join-picker-table-wrap"><table><thead><tr><th>Join Input</th><th>Dynamic Label</th><th>Join Key</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td>Left Source</td><td>${esc(leftLabel)}</td><td>${esc(p.left_field || "[left key not selected]")}</td></tr><tr><td>Right Source</td><td>${esc(rightLabel)}</td><td>${esc(p.right_field || "[right key not selected]")}</td></tr><tr><td>Developer Logic</td><td colspan="2">${esc(atlasJoinDeveloperLogic({ ...step, params: { ...p, join_type: joinType } }))}</td></tr></tbody></table></div></section></div><div class="join-picker-footer"><p class="hint">Confirm is required to preserve the selected JOIN type.</p><div class="toolbar compact-toolbar"><button type="button" class="btn ghost" data-close-join-picker>Cancel</button><button type="button" class="btn primary" data-confirm-join-picker>Confirm</button></div></div></div>`;
+  document.body.append(root);
+}
+
+function atlasCloseJoinPicker({ restoreFocus = true } = {}) {
+  const stepId = atlasJoinPickerState?.stepId || "";
+  atlasJoinPickerState = null;
+  document.querySelector(".join-picker-backdrop")?.remove();
+  if (restoreFocus && stepId) {
+    document
+      .querySelector(`[data-open-join-picker="${CSS.escape(stepId)}"]`)
+      ?.focus();
+  }
+}
+function atlasConfirmJoinPicker() {
+  if (!atlasJoinPickerState) return;
+  const step = state.audit.steps.find(
+    (item) => item.id === atlasJoinPickerState.stepId,
+  );
+  if (!step) return;
+  step.params = step.params || {};
+  step.params.join_type = atlasJoinTypeDisplayName(
+    atlasJoinPickerState.pendingJoinType,
+  );
+  const editor = document.querySelector(
+    `[data-live-editor-step-id="${CSS.escape(step.id)}"]`,
+  );
+  const hidden = editor?.querySelector('[data-param="join_type"]');
+  if (hidden) hidden.value = step.params.join_type;
+  atlasCloseJoinPicker({ restoreFocus: false });
+  renderAll();
+  scheduleAutosave();
+}
+
+document.addEventListener(
+  "click",
+  (event) => {
+    const openButton = event.target.closest("[data-open-join-picker]");
+    if (openButton) {
+      atlasOpenJoinPicker(
+        openButton.dataset.openJoinPicker || state.selectedStepId,
+      );
+      return;
+    }
+    const joinOption = event.target.closest("[data-join-type-option]");
+    if (joinOption && atlasJoinPickerState) {
+      atlasJoinPickerState.pendingJoinType = joinOption.dataset.joinTypeOption;
+      atlasRenderJoinPickerModal();
+      return;
+    }
+    if (event.target.closest("[data-confirm-join-picker]")) {
+      atlasConfirmJoinPicker();
+      return;
+    }
+    if (event.target.closest("[data-close-join-picker]")) {
+      atlasCloseJoinPicker();
+    }
+  },
+  true,
+);
+
+const atlasJoinPreviousRenderLogicTablePreviewRows =
+  typeof renderLogicTablePreviewRows === "function"
+    ? renderLogicTablePreviewRows
+    : null;
+renderLogicTablePreviewRows =
+  function renderLogicTablePreviewRowsWithDeveloperLogic() {
+    const target = $("logicBuilderTableBody");
+    if (!target) return;
+    const entries = atlasVisibleStepEntries
+      ? atlasVisibleStepEntries()
+      : hierarchicalStepEntries({ includeDisabled: true });
+    target.innerHTML = entries.length
+      ? entries
+          .map((entry) => {
+            const dev =
+              typeof developerLogic === "function"
+                ? developerLogic(entry.step)
+                : "";
+            return `<tr class="${state.selectedStepId === entry.step.id ? "selected-row" : ""}" data-table-step-id="${esc(entry.step.id)}"><td>${esc(entry.number)}</td><td>${esc(atlasStepTypeLabel ? atlasStepTypeLabel(entry.step) : entry.step.step_type || "Logic Step")}</td><td>${renderLogicWithPills(analystLogic(entry.step))}</td><td>${esc(dev)}</td><td>${entry.step.enabled === false ? '<span class="badge red">Disabled</span>' : '<span class="badge blue">Active</span>'}</td></tr>`;
+          })
+          .join("")
+      : '<tr><td colspan="5" class="hint">No logic steps yet.</td></tr>';
+  };
+
+/* JOIN Venn overlap refinement - accurate two-circle overlap shading */
+function atlasJoinIncludedRegionMarkup(joinType, clipId) {
+  const normalized = atlasJoinTypeDisplayName(joinType);
+  if (normalized === "INNER JOIN") {
+    return `<circle class="join-venn-included join-venn-included-intersection" cx="155" cy="142" r="112" clip-path="url(#${clipId}-right)"></circle>`;
+  }
+  if (normalized === "LEFT JOIN") {
+    return `<circle class="join-venn-included join-venn-included-left" cx="155" cy="142" r="112"></circle>`;
+  }
+  if (normalized === "RIGHT JOIN") {
+    return `<circle class="join-venn-included join-venn-included-right" cx="245" cy="142" r="112"></circle>`;
+  }
+  return `<circle class="join-venn-included join-venn-included-left" cx="155" cy="142" r="112"></circle><circle class="join-venn-included join-venn-included-right" cx="245" cy="142" r="112"></circle>`;
+}
+
+function atlasJoinVennSvg(
+  joinType,
+  leftLabel,
+  rightLabel,
+  leftColor,
+  rightColor,
+) {
+  const normalized = atlasJoinTypeDisplayName(joinType);
+  const clipId = `joinClip${String(normalized).replace(/[^A-Z]/g, "")}`;
+  return `<svg class="join-venn-svg" viewBox="0 0 400 300" role="img" aria-label="${esc(normalized)} Venn diagram showing included join output rows">
+    <defs>
+      <clipPath id="${clipId}-left"><circle cx="155" cy="142" r="112"></circle></clipPath>
+      <clipPath id="${clipId}-right"><circle cx="245" cy="142" r="112"></circle></clipPath>
+    </defs>
+    <rect class="join-venn-canvas" x="0" y="0" width="400" height="300" rx="18"></rect>
+    <circle class="join-venn-base join-venn-base-left" cx="155" cy="142" r="112" style="--join-source-color:${esc(leftColor)}"></circle>
+    <circle class="join-venn-base join-venn-base-right" cx="245" cy="142" r="112" style="--join-source-color:${esc(rightColor)}"></circle>
+    <circle class="join-venn-overlap-outline" cx="155" cy="142" r="112" clip-path="url(#${clipId}-right)"></circle>
+    ${atlasJoinIncludedRegionMarkup(normalized, clipId)}
+    <circle class="join-venn-stroke" cx="155" cy="142" r="112" style="--join-source-color:${esc(leftColor)}"></circle>
+    <circle class="join-venn-stroke" cx="245" cy="142" r="112" style="--join-source-color:${esc(rightColor)}"></circle>
+    <text class="join-venn-area-label join-venn-left-only-label" x="106" y="145">Left only</text>
+    <text class="join-venn-area-label join-venn-match-label" x="200" y="145">Matched</text>
+    <text class="join-venn-area-label join-venn-right-only-label" x="294" y="145">Right only</text>
+    <foreignObject x="20" y="246" width="166" height="42"><div class="join-venn-source-label join-venn-source-left">${esc(leftLabel)}</div></foreignObject>
+    <foreignObject x="214" y="246" width="166" height="42"><div class="join-venn-source-label join-venn-source-right">${esc(rightLabel)}</div></foreignObject>
+  </svg>`;
+}
+
+function atlasJoinUnionSvg(
+  leftLabel,
+  rightLabel,
+  leftColor,
+  rightColor,
+  outputLabel,
+) {
+  return `<svg class="join-union-visual" viewBox="0 0 520 300" role="img" aria-label="UNION appends rows from ${esc(leftLabel)} and ${esc(rightLabel)} into ${esc(outputLabel)}. Matching keys are not required.">
+    <rect class="join-venn-canvas" x="0" y="0" width="520" height="300" rx="18"></rect>
+    <rect class="join-union-source-box" x="70" y="38" width="380" height="64" rx="14" style="--join-source-color:${esc(leftColor)}"></rect>
+    <text class="join-union-label" x="260" y="76" text-anchor="middle">${esc(leftLabel)}</text>
+    <text class="join-union-plus" x="260" y="140" text-anchor="middle">+</text>
+    <rect class="join-union-source-box" x="70" y="158" width="380" height="64" rx="14" style="--join-source-color:${esc(rightColor)}"></rect>
+    <text class="join-union-label" x="260" y="196" text-anchor="middle">${esc(rightLabel)}</text>
+    <path class="join-union-arrow" d="M260 228 V252 M248 242 L260 254 L272 242"></path>
+    <rect class="join-union-output" x="120" y="258" width="280" height="34" rx="12"></rect>
+    <text class="join-union-output-title" x="260" y="280" text-anchor="middle">${esc(outputLabel)}</text>
+  </svg>`;
+}
+atlasRenderJoinPickerModal =
+  function atlasRenderJoinPickerModalOverlapRefinement() {
+    document.querySelector(".join-picker-backdrop")?.remove();
+    if (!atlasJoinPickerState) return;
+    const step = state.audit.steps.find(
+      (item) => item.id === atlasJoinPickerState.stepId,
+    );
+    if (!step || step.step_type !== "join") {
+      atlasCloseJoinPicker({ restoreFocus: false });
+      return;
+    }
+    const p = step.params || {};
+    const joinType = atlasJoinTypeDisplayName(
+      atlasJoinPickerState.pendingJoinType,
+    );
+    const union = joinType === "UNION";
+    const leftColor =
+      atlasSelectedSourceColor?.(p.left_source) ||
+      SOURCE_COLOR_PALETTE?.[0] ||
+      "#4d6fa9";
+    const rightColor =
+      atlasSelectedSourceColor?.(p.right_source) ||
+      SOURCE_COLOR_PALETTE?.[1] ||
+      "#b54b4b";
+    const leftLabel = atlasJoinSourceLabel(p.left_source);
+    const rightLabel = atlasJoinSourceLabel(p.right_source);
+    const outputLabel =
+      p.output_source || step.source || "[combined output not selected]";
+    const description = atlasJoinKeptRowsDescription(joinType);
+    const visual = union
+      ? atlasJoinUnionSvg(
+          leftLabel,
+          rightLabel,
+          leftColor,
+          rightColor,
+          outputLabel,
+        )
+      : atlasJoinVennSvg(
+          joinType,
+          leftLabel,
+          rightLabel,
+          leftColor,
+          rightColor,
+        );
+    const options = [
+      ["INNER JOIN", "Matched rows only"],
+      ["LEFT JOIN", "All left rows plus matches"],
+      ["RIGHT JOIN", "All right rows plus matches"],
+      ["FULL OUTER JOIN", "All rows from both sources"],
+      ["UNION", "Append rows from both sources"],
+    ];
+    const root = document.createElement("div");
+    root.className = "join-picker-backdrop";
+    root.innerHTML = `<div class="join-picker-modal join-picker-modal-refined" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-labelledby="join-picker-title" aria-describedby="join-picker-description"><div class="join-picker-header"><div><h2 class="join-picker-title" id="join-picker-title">Choose Join Type</h2><p class="hint" id="join-picker-description">Choose how the two sources are combined. Preview changes are temporary until Confirm is selected.</p></div><button type="button" class="btn ghost small" data-close-join-picker aria-label="Close Join Type picker">Cancel</button></div><div class="join-picker-refined-body"><section class="join-picker-visual-column"><div class="join-picker-visual-card" data-join-type="${esc(joinType)}" style="--join-left-color:${esc(leftColor)};--join-right-color:${esc(rightColor)}">${visual}</div><div class="join-type-options-under-venn">${options.map(([type, subtitle]) => `<button type="button" class="join-type-rectangle ${type === joinType ? "active" : ""} ${type === "UNION" ? "union" : ""}" data-join-type-option="${esc(type)}" aria-pressed="${type === joinType ? "true" : "false"}" style="--join-option-accent:${esc(type === "RIGHT JOIN" ? rightColor : leftColor)}"><span class="join-type-rectangle-label">${esc(type === "UNION" ? "UNION / APPEND ROWS" : type)}</span><span class="join-type-rectangle-subtitle">${esc(subtitle)}</span></button>`).join("")}</div></section><section class="join-picker-details-card" aria-live="polite"><div class="join-picker-summary-card"><strong>${esc(union ? "UNION / APPEND ROWS" : joinType)}</strong><p>${esc(description)}</p></div><div class="join-picker-table-wrap"><table><thead><tr><th>Input</th><th>Source</th><th>${union ? "Join Key" : "Join Key"}</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td>Left Source</td><td>${esc(leftLabel)}</td><td>${esc(union ? "Not applicable" : p.left_field || "[left key not selected]")}</td></tr><tr><td>Right Source</td><td>${esc(rightLabel)}</td><td>${esc(union ? "Not applicable" : p.right_field || "[right key not selected]")}</td></tr>${union ? `<tr><td>Output Source</td><td colspan="2">${esc(outputLabel)}</td></tr>` : ""}<tr><td>Developer Logic</td><td colspan="2">${esc(atlasJoinDeveloperLogic({ ...step, params: { ...p, join_type: joinType } }))}</td></tr></tbody></table></div></section></div><div class="join-picker-footer"><p class="hint">Confirm is required to preserve the selected relationship type.</p><div class="toolbar compact-toolbar"><button type="button" class="btn ghost" data-close-join-picker>Cancel</button><button type="button" class="btn primary" data-confirm-join-picker>Confirm</button></div></div></div>`;
+    document.body.append(root);
+    root.querySelector(`[data-join-type-option][aria-pressed="true"]`)?.focus();
+  };
+document.addEventListener(
+  "keydown",
+  (event) => {
+    if (event.key !== "Escape" || !atlasJoinPickerState) return;
+    event.preventDefault();
+    event.stopImmediatePropagation();
+    atlasCloseJoinPicker();
+  },
+  true,
+);

+================================================================================
+BEGIN FILE: js/15g-visual-ui-events.js
