+// ATLAS-STRING-TRANSFORM-UPDATE:BEGIN
+const atlasRenderThenActionBuilderBeforeStringTransform =
+  atlasRenderThenActionBuilder;
+atlasRenderThenActionBuilder =
+  function atlasRenderThenActionBuilderWithStringTransform(p, prefix = "THEN") {
+    const actionType = p.then_action_type || "freeform";
+    if (actionType !== "set_column_string_transform") {
+      const html = atlasRenderThenActionBuilderBeforeStringTransform(p, prefix);
+      return html.replace(
+        /(<option[^>]+value="set_column_value"[^>]*>[^<]*<\/option>)/,
+        `$1<option value="set_column_string_transform">Set Column to String Transformation</option>`,
+      );
+    }
+    const c = atlasStringTransformConfig(p, "then_");
+    return `<div class="then-action-builder string-transform-builder"><h4>${prefix} Action</h4>
+<label>${prefix} Action Type<select data-param="then_action_type"><option value="freeform">Freeform Text</option><option value="set_column_value">Set Column Value from Field</option><option value="set_column_string_transform" selected>Set Column to String Transformation</option><option value="set_column_calculation">Set Column to Calculated Value</option><option value="display_text">Display Text Response in Column</option><option value="do_not_display">Do Not Display</option></select></label>
+<label>Target Column${fieldInput("then_target_column", c.destinationColumn, "Select or create target column")}</label>
+<label>Operation<select data-param="then_string_operation"><option value="substring" ${c.operation === "substring" ? "selected" : ""}>First / Last Characters</option><option value="string_length" ${c.operation === "string_length" ? "selected" : ""}>Character Count</option></select></label>
+<input type="hidden" data-param="then_string_source_file" value="${esc(c.sourceFile)}">
+<label>Value Field${fieldInput("then_string_source_field", c.sourceField, "Select source field", c.sourceFile)}</label>
+${c.operation === "substring" ? `<label>Characters<select data-param="then_string_position"><option value="first" ${c.position === "first" ? "selected" : ""}>First</option><option value="last" ${c.position === "last" ? "selected" : ""}>Last</option></select></label><label>Number of Characters<input data-param="then_string_character_count" inputmode="numeric" pattern="[1-9][0-9]*" min="1" step="1" value="${esc(c.count)}"></label>` : `<p class="hint">Returns a numeric whole-number result. Blank text returns 0; null remains blank/NULL.</p>`}
+</div>`;
+  };
+
+document.addEventListener(
+  "change",
+  (event) => {
+    if (
+      event.target.matches(
+        '[data-param="value_mode"], [data-param="string_operation"], [data-param="then_string_operation"]',
+      )
+    )
+      atlasCommitLiveEditor?.({ rerender: true });
+  },
+  true,
+);
+
+document.addEventListener(
+  "input",
+  (event) => {
+    if (
+      !event.target.matches(
+        '[data-param="string_character_count"], [data-param="then_string_character_count"]',
+      )
+    )
+      return;
+    const valid =
+      !event.target.value || !!atlasPositiveWholeNumber(event.target.value);
+    event.target.setAttribute("aria-invalid", valid ? "false" : "true");
+  },
+  true,
+);
+
+function atlasStringTransformIssuesForAudit() {
+  return (state.audit.steps || []).flatMap((step) => {
+    const p = step.params || {};
+    if (
+      step.step_type === "create_column" &&
+      p.value_mode === ATLAS_STRING_TRANSFORM_VALUE_MODE
+    )
+      return atlasValidateStringTransform(p).map((issue) => ({
+        ...issue,
+        stepId: step.id,
+      }));
+    if (
+      step.step_type === "if_then" &&
+      p.then_action_type === "set_column_string_transform"
+    )
+      return atlasValidateStringTransform(p, "then_").map((issue) => ({
+        ...issue,
+        stepId: step.id,
+      }));
+    return [];
+  });
+}
+// ATLAS-STRING-TRANSFORM-UPDATE:END
+
+/* ATLAS-CREATED-FIELD-PICKER:BEGIN */
+/* ATLAS Visual Field Picker created-column update. Loaded at end of 15g. */
+const ATLAS_CREATED_SOURCE = "__created_columns__",
+  ATLAS_CREATED_LABEL = "Created Columns";
+function atlasPickerEntries() {
+  return typeof hierarchicalStepEntries === "function"
+    ? hierarchicalStepEntries({ includeDisabled: true })
+    : (state.audit.steps || []).map((step, i) => ({
+        step,
+        number: String(i + 1),
+      }));
+}
+function atlasPickerReferenceSource(value) {
+  let step = typeof value === "string" ? stepById?.(value) : value,
+    seen = new Set();
+  while (step && !seen.has(step.id)) {
+    seen.add(step.id);
+    if (step.step_type === "reference_source" || step.autoGeneratedReference)
+      return step.params?.source_file || step.source || "";
+    step = step.parentId ? stepById?.(step.parentId) : null;
+  }
+  return "";
+}
+function atlasPickerCreatedSource(step) {
+  return String(
+    step?.params?.source_file ||
+      step?.source ||
+      atlasPickerReferenceSource(step) ||
+      ATLAS_CREATED_SOURCE,
+  ).trim();
+}
+
+function commitPickerSelection(value, sourceFile = "") {
+  if (!visualPickerState?.targetStepId) return false;
+
+  setPickerTargetValue(value, sourceFile);
+  return true;
+}
+
+function atlasCreatedFieldEntries({
+  targetStepId = "",
+  sourceFile = "",
+  includeUnavailable = false,
+} = {}) {
+  const entries = atlasPickerEntries(),
+    found = targetStepId
+      ? entries.findIndex((e) => e.step.id === targetStepId)
+      : entries.length,
+    target = found < 0 ? entries.length : found;
+  return entries.flatMap((entry, index) => {
+    const step = entry.step,
+      name = String(step.params?.column_name || step.field || "").trim(),
+      source = atlasPickerCreatedSource(step),
+      available = index < target;
+    if (
+      step.step_type !== "create_column" ||
+      !name ||
+      (!includeUnavailable && !available) ||
+      (sourceFile && sourceFile !== source)
+    )
+      return [];
+    const selected = (state.audit.sources || []).find((s) =>
+      [s.name, s.sourceFile, s.sourceKey, s.alias].includes(source),
+    );
+    return [
+      {
+        fieldName: name,
+        rawFieldName: name,
+        vanityLabel: name,
+        label: name,
+        detail: `Created by Step ${entry.number || index + 1}`,
+        sourceFile: source,
+        sourceAlias:
+          source === ATLAS_CREATED_SOURCE
+            ? ATLAS_CREATED_LABEL
+            : sourceAliasFor?.(source) || source,
+        sourceColor: selected?.color || "#7da2d8",
+        origin: "created",
+        isCreatedField: true,
+        creatorStepId: step.id,
+        available,
+      },
+    ];
+  });
+}
+function atlasUnifiedFieldEntries({ sourceFile = "", targetStepId = "" } = {}) {
+  const selected = new Set();
+  (state.audit.sources || []).forEach((s) =>
+    [s.name, s.sourceFile, s.sourceKey, s.alias]
+      .filter(Boolean)
+      .forEach((v) => selected.add(v)),
+  );
+  const sources = sourceCatalogEntries()
+    .filter(
+      (s) =>
+        selected.has(s.sourceFile) ||
+        selected.has(s.sourceKey) ||
+        selected.has(s.alias),
+    )
+    .filter(
+      (s) =>
+        !sourceFile ||
+        [s.sourceFile, s.sourceKey, s.alias].includes(sourceFile),
+    );
+  const base = sources.flatMap((source) =>
+    (source.fields || []).map((field) => {
+      const parts = fieldDisplayParts(field, source.sourceFile),
+        picked = (state.audit.sources || []).find((s) =>
+          [s.name, s.sourceFile, s.sourceKey, s.alias].includes(
+            source.sourceFile,
+          ),
+        );
+      return {
+        ...field,
+        sourceFile: source.sourceFile,
+        sourceAlias: source.alias || source.sourceFile,
+        fieldName: parts.raw,
+        rawFieldName: parts.raw,
+        vanityLabel: parts.vanity,
+        label: parts.display,
+        detail: parts.alternate,
+        sourceColor: picked?.color || "",
+        origin: "source",
+        isCreatedField: false,
+      };
+    }),
+  );
+  const seen = new Set();
+  return [
+    ...base,
+    ...atlasCreatedFieldEntries({ targetStepId, sourceFile }),
+  ].filter((f) => {
+    const key = `${f.origin}:${f.sourceFile}:${f.fieldName}:${f.creatorStepId || ""}`;
+    if (!f.fieldName || seen.has(key)) return false;
+    seen.add(key);
+    return true;
+  });
+}
+const atlasFieldsForSourceOriginal = fieldsForSource;
+fieldsForSource = function (sourceFile = "") {
+  const rows = atlasUnifiedFieldEntries({
+    sourceFile,
+    targetStepId: state.selectedStepId || "",
+  });
+  return rows.length ? rows : atlasFieldsForSourceOriginal(sourceFile);
+};
+pickerFieldOptions = function (sourceFile = "") {
+  return atlasUnifiedFieldEntries({
+    sourceFile,
+    targetStepId: visualPickerState?.targetStepId || state.selectedStepId || "",
+  });
+};
+function atlasFieldPickerEligibilityPolicy(picker, step) {
+  const name = picker?.dataset?.paramName || picker?.dataset?.conditionName || "";
+  const multiSourceNames = new Set([
+    "fieldName",
+    "compareFieldName",
+    "then_target_column",
+    "then_value_field",
+  ]);
+  if (
+    picker?.dataset?.openMiniPicker === "field" &&
+    step?.step_type === "if_then" &&
+    multiSourceNames.has(name)
+  )
+    return { mode: "all-selected", sourceFile: "" };
+  return {
+    mode: "single-source",
+    sourceFile:
+      picker?.dataset?.pickerSource || atlasPickerReferenceSource(step) || "",
+  };
+}
+function atlasPickerScope(picker, step) {
+  return atlasFieldPickerEligibilityPolicy(picker, step).sourceFile;
+}
+const atlasSetPickerTargetValueOriginal = setPickerTargetValue;
+setPickerTargetValue = function atlasSetPickerTargetValueCommitBeforeRender(
+  value,
+  sourceFile = "",
+) {
+  const context = visualPickerState;
+  if (!context) return;
+  const step = stepById?.(context.targetStepId || state.selectedStepId);
+  const target = context.target;
+  const editor = target?.closest("[data-live-editor-step-id]");
+  const conditionRow = target?.closest(".guided-condition-row");
+  const option =
+    context.kind === "field"
+      ? pickerFieldOptions(context.sourceFile).find(
+          (x) => x.fieldName === value && x.sourceFile === sourceFile,
+        )
+      : null;
+  const ref = option?.isCreatedField
+    ? { kind: "created", creatorStepId: option.creatorStepId, sourceFile }
+    : null;
+
+  const parameterSourcePairs = {
+    field_name: "source_file",
+    left_field: "left_source",
+    right_field: "right_source",
+    then_value_field: "then_value_source_file",
+    concat_field_1: "concat_source_1",
+    concat_field_2: "concat_source_2",
+    string_source_field: "string_source_file",
+    then_string_source_field: "then_string_source_file",
+  };
+  const parameterFieldPairs = {
+    source_file: "field_name",
+    left_source: "left_field",
+    right_source: "right_field",
+    then_value_source_file: "then_value_field",
+    concat_source_1: "concat_field_1",
+    concat_source_2: "concat_field_2",
+    string_source_file: "string_source_field",
+    then_string_source_file: "then_string_source_field",
+  };
+
+  if (context.targetType === "condition") {
+    const control = conditionRow?.querySelector(
+      `[data-condition-field="${CSS.escape(context.name)}"]`,
+    );
+    if (control) control.value = value;
+    if (context.kind === "field") {
+      const sourceName =
+        context.name === "compareFieldName"
+          ? "compareSourceFile"
+          : "sourceFile";
+      const sourceControl = conditionRow?.querySelector(
+        `[data-condition-field="${sourceName}"]`,
+      );
+      if (sourceControl && sourceFile) sourceControl.value = sourceFile;
+    }
+  } else {
+    const control = editor?.querySelector(
+      `[data-param="${CSS.escape(context.name)}"]`,
+    );
+    if (control) control.value = value;
+    if (context.kind === "field") {
+      const sourceName = parameterSourcePairs[context.name];
+      const sourceControl = sourceName
+        ? editor?.querySelector(`[data-param="${sourceName}"]`)
+        : null;
+      if (sourceControl && sourceFile) sourceControl.value = sourceFile;
+    } else if (context.kind === "source") {
+      const fieldName = parameterFieldPairs[context.name];
+      const fieldControl = fieldName
+        ? editor?.querySelector(`[data-param="${fieldName}"]`)
+        : null;
+      if (fieldControl?.value) {
+        const valid = fieldsForSource(value).some((field) => {
+          const parts = fieldDisplayParts(field, value);
+          return (
+            fieldControl.value === parts.raw ||
+            fieldControl.value === parts.vanity
+          );
+        });
+        if (!valid) fieldControl.value = "";
+      }
+    }
+  }
+
+  // Capture the updated hidden controls before any picker state or editor DOM is removed.
+  atlasCommitLiveEditor?.({ rerender: false });
+
+  // Preserve created-field reference metadata after the standard editor capture.
+  if (step && (context.kind === "field" || context.kind === "source")) {
+    step.params = step.params || {};
+    if (context.kind === "source" && context.targetType !== "condition") {
+      step.params[context.name] = value;
+    } else if (context.targetType === "condition") {
+      const conditions = parseStepConditions?.(step) || [];
+      const condition = conditions.find((c) => c.id === context.conditionId);
+      if (condition) {
+        const compare = context.name === "compareFieldName";
+        const refKey = compare ? "compareFieldRef" : "fieldRef";
+        condition[context.name] = value;
+        condition[compare ? "compareSourceFile" : "sourceFile"] = sourceFile;
+        if (ref) condition[refKey] = ref;
+        else delete condition[refKey];
+        atlasWriteLiveEditorConditions?.(step, conditions);
+      }
+    } else {
+      step.params[context.name] = value;
+      const key = `${context.name}_field_ref`;
+      if (ref) step.params[key] = ref;
+      else delete step.params[key];
+      const sourceName = parameterSourcePairs[context.name];
+      if (sourceName && sourceFile) step.params[sourceName] = sourceFile;
+    }
+  }
+
+  visualPickerState = null;
+  document
+    .querySelector(".visual-picker-modal-backdrop.dynamic-mini-picker")
+    ?.remove();
+  atlasMarkLogicIssuesDirty?.();
+  renderAll?.();
+  scheduleAutosave?.();
+};
+function atlasCreatedPickerHtml() {
+  if (!visualPickerState) return "";
+  const values =
+      visualPickerState.kind === "source"
+        ? pickerSourceOptions()
+        : pickerFieldOptions(visualPickerState.sourceFile),
+    query = String(visualPickerState.query || "").toLowerCase(),
+    groups = new Map();
+  values
+    .filter((x) =>
+      `${x.sourceFile} ${x.sourceAlias} ${x.fieldName} ${x.label}`
+        .toLowerCase()
+        .includes(query),
+    )
+    .forEach((x) => {
+      const group =
+        visualPickerState.kind === "source"
+          ? "Selected Sources"
+          : x.sourceFile === ATLAS_CREATED_SOURCE
+            ? ATLAS_CREATED_LABEL
+            : x.sourceFile;
+      if (!groups.has(group)) groups.set(group, []);
+      groups.get(group).push(x);
+    });
+  return (
+    [...groups]
+      .map(
+        ([group, items]) =>
+          `<section class="visual-picker-group"><div class="visual-picker-group-title"><strong>${esc(group)}</strong><span>${items.length}</span></div><div class="visual-picker-option-list">${items.map((item) => `<button type="button" class="visual-picker-option ${item.isCreatedField ? "custom-field-pill" : ""}" data-mini-picker-value="${esc(visualPickerState.kind === "source" ? item.sourceFile : item.fieldName)}" data-mini-picker-source="${esc(item.sourceFile)}" style="--source-color:${esc(item.sourceColor || "#7da2d8")}"><span class="source-color-dot"></span><span class="visual-picker-option-copy"><strong>${esc(visualPickerState.kind === "source" ? item.sourceFile : item.label)}</strong><small>${esc(visualPickerState.kind === "source" ? item.sourceAlias : `${item.sourceAlias}.${item.fieldName}`)}</small></span>${item.isCreatedField ? '<span class="created-column-badge">fx Created</span>' : ""}</button>`).join("")}</div></section>`,
+      )
+      .join("") ||
+    '<p class="hint">No selected source fields match the current search.</p>'
+  );
+}
+renderMiniPicker = function renderMiniPickerSharedAdapter() {
+  document
+    .querySelector(".visual-picker-modal-backdrop.dynamic-mini-picker")
+    ?.remove();
+  if (!visualPickerState) return;
+  if (!window.ATLASVisualPicker?.open) {
+    console.error("ATLAS Visual Picker: shared component is unavailable.");
+    visualPickerState = null;
+    return;
+  }
+
+  const pickerKind = visualPickerState.kind;
+  const values =
+    pickerKind === "source"
+      ? pickerSourceOptions()
+      : pickerFieldOptions(visualPickerState.sourceFile);
+  const entries = values.map((item) => {
+    const sourceFile = item.sourceFile || "";
+    const sourceAlias = item.sourceAlias || sourceFile || "Other";
+    const fieldName = item.fieldName || "";
+    return {
+      key:
+        pickerKind === "source"
+          ? sourceFile
+          : `${item.sourceKey || atlasSourceStableKey(sourceFile)}::${fieldName}`,
+      label: pickerKind === "source" ? sourceFile : item.label || fieldName,
+      rawName: pickerKind === "source" ? sourceFile : fieldName,
+      sourceKey: item.sourceKey || atlasSourceStableKey(sourceFile),
+      sourceLabel:
+        pickerKind === "source"
+          ? "Selected Sources"
+          : sourceFile === ATLAS_CREATED_SOURCE
+            ? ATLAS_CREATED_LABEL
+            : sourceAlias,
+      sourceColor:
+        item.sourceColor || atlasSelectedSourceColor(sourceFile) || "#7da2d8",
+      custom: Boolean(item.isCreatedField),
+      badge: item.isCreatedField ? "Custom" : "",
+      pickerValue: pickerKind === "source" ? sourceFile : fieldName,
+      pickerSource: sourceFile,
+    };
+  });
+  const selectedValue = String(
+    visualPickerState.target
+      ?.closest(".guided-condition-row")
+      ?.querySelector(
+        `[data-condition-field="${CSS.escape(visualPickerState.name || "")}"]`,
+      )?.value || visualPickerState.target?.value || "",
+  );
+  const selectedSource =
+    visualPickerState.selectedSourceFile || visualPickerState.sourceFile || "";
+  const selectedEntry = entries.find(
+    (entry) =>
+      entry.pickerValue === selectedValue &&
+      (pickerKind === "source" ||
+        !selectedSource ||
+        entry.pickerSource === selectedSource),
+  );
+
+  window.ATLASVisualPicker.open({
+    mode: pickerKind,
+    title:
+      pickerKind === "source" ? "Choose Selected Source File" : "Choose Field",
+    guidance:
+      pickerKind === "source"
+        ? "Only sources selected for this audit are available here."
+        : "Fields are grouped by source. Created columns are available only after their creation step.",
+    searchPlaceholder: "Search selected sources and fields...",
+    entries,
+    query: visualPickerState.query || "",
+    sort:
+      typeof atlasFieldPickerSortMode === "string"
+        ? atlasFieldPickerSortMode
+        : "source",
+    selectedKey: selectedEntry?.key || null,
+    onSelect(entry) {
+      if (!visualPickerState || !entry) return;
+      commitPickerSelection(entry.pickerValue, entry.pickerSource);
+    },
+    onClose(reason) {
+      if (reason !== "select") visualPickerState = null;
+    },
+  });
+};
+function atlasRemoveSourceControl(html, label, field, value = "") {
+  const template = document.createElement("template");
+  template.innerHTML = html;
+  const sourceTrigger = [...template.content.querySelectorAll(
+    'button[data-open-mini-picker="source"][data-condition-name]',
+  )].find((button) => button.dataset.conditionName === field);
+  const sourceLabel = sourceTrigger?.closest("label");
+  if (!sourceLabel) return html;
+  const hidden = document.createElement("input");
+  hidden.type = "hidden";
+  hidden.dataset.conditionField = field;
+  hidden.value = value || "";
+  sourceLabel.replaceWith(hidden);
+  return template.innerHTML;
+}
+const atlasRenderConditionRowOriginal = renderConditionRow;
+renderConditionRow = function (condition, index) {
+  let html = atlasRenderConditionRowOriginal(condition, index);
+  html = atlasRemoveSourceControl(
+    html,
+    "Source File",
+    "sourceFile",
+    condition.fieldName ? condition.sourceFile || "" : "",
+  );
+  return atlasRemoveSourceControl(
+    html,
+    "Compare Source",
+    "compareSourceFile",
+    condition.compareSourceFile || condition.sourceFile || "",
+  );
+};
+function atlasCreatedRefs(step) {
+  const refs = [],
+    p = step.params || {};
+  Object.entries(p).forEach(([key, value]) => {
+    if (key.endsWith("_field_ref") && value?.kind === "created")
+      refs.push({ ...value, location: key });
+  });
+  try {
+    (JSON.parse(p.conditions_json || "[]") || []).forEach((c) => {
+      if (c.fieldRef?.kind === "created")
+        refs.push({ ...c.fieldRef, location: "condition" });
+      if (c.compareFieldRef?.kind === "created")
+        refs.push({ ...c.compareFieldRef, location: "compare condition" });
+    });
+  } catch (_e) {}
+  return refs;
+}
+function atlasCreatedFieldIssuesForAudit() {
+  const entries = atlasPickerEntries(),
+    positions = new Map(entries.map((e, i) => [e.step.id, i])),
+    creators = new Map(
+      entries
+        .filter((e) => e.step.step_type === "create_column")
+        .map((e) => [e.step.id, e.step]),
+    );
+  return entries.flatMap((entry) =>
+    atlasCreatedRefs(entry.step).flatMap((ref) => {
+      const creator = creators.get(ref.creatorStepId);
+      if (!creator)
+        return [
+          {
+            level: "error",
+            code: "created_field_removed",
+            stepId: entry.step.id,
+            message: `Created-column reference in ${ref.location} is invalid because its Create Column rule was removed.`,
+          },
+        ];
+      if (
+        (positions.get(ref.creatorStepId) ?? Infinity) >=
+        (positions.get(entry.step.id) ?? -1)
+      )
+        return [
+          {
+            level: "error",
+            code: "created_field_forward_reference",
+            stepId: entry.step.id,
+            message: `Created column "${creator.params?.column_name || creator.field || ""}" is referenced before its Create Column rule.`,
+          },
+        ];
+      return [];
+    }),
+  );
+}
+const atlasColumnNameFocus = new WeakMap();
+document.addEventListener(
+  "focusin",
+  (event) => {
+    const input = event.target.closest?.('[data-param="column_name"]');
+    if (input) atlasColumnNameFocus.set(input, input.value || "");
+  },
+  true,
+);
+document.addEventListener(
+  "focusout",
+  (event) => {
+    const input = event.target.closest?.('[data-param="column_name"]');
+    if (!input) return;
+    const oldName = atlasColumnNameFocus.get(input) || "",
+      newName = String(input.value || "").trim(),
+      creator = stepById?.(
+        input.closest("[data-live-editor-step-id]")?.dataset.liveEditorStepId ||
+          state.selectedStepId,
+      );
+    if (!creator || !oldName || !newName || oldName === newName) return;
+    (state.audit.steps || []).forEach((step) => {
+      const p = step.params || {};
+      Object.entries(p).forEach(([key, ref]) => {
+        if (key.endsWith("_field_ref") && ref?.creatorStepId === creator.id) {
+          const valueKey = key.slice(0, -10);
+          if (p[valueKey] === oldName) p[valueKey] = newName;
+        }
+      });
+      try {
+        const conditions = JSON.parse(p.conditions_json || "[]");
+        let changed = false;
+        conditions.forEach((c) => {
+          if (
+            c.fieldRef?.creatorStepId === creator.id &&
+            c.fieldName === oldName
+          ) {
+            c.fieldName = newName;
+            changed = true;
+          }
+          if (
+            c.compareFieldRef?.creatorStepId === creator.id &&
+            c.compareFieldName === oldName
+          ) {
+            c.compareFieldName = newName;
+            changed = true;
+          }
+        });
+        if (changed) atlasWriteLiveEditorConditions?.(step, conditions);
+      } catch (_e) {}
+    });
+    atlasMarkLogicIssuesDirty?.();
+    renderAll?.();
+    scheduleAutosave?.();
+  },
+  true,
+);
+document.addEventListener(
+  "click",
+  (event) => {
+    const picker = event.target.closest?.('[data-open-mini-picker="field"]');
+    if (!picker) return;
+    queueMicrotask(() => {
+      if (!visualPickerState) return;
+      const step = stepById?.(
+        picker.closest("[data-live-editor-step-id]")?.dataset
+          .liveEditorStepId || state.selectedStepId,
+      );
+      visualPickerState.targetStepId = step?.id || state.selectedStepId || "";
+      visualPickerState.conditionId =
+        picker.closest(".guided-condition-row")?.dataset.conditionId || "";
+      visualPickerState.selectedSourceFile = picker.dataset.pickerSource || "";
+      visualPickerState.sourceFile = atlasPickerScope(picker, step);
+      renderMiniPicker();
+    });
+  },
+  true,
+);
+
+/* ATLAS-CREATED-FIELD-PICKER:END */
+
+/* ATLAS-LOGIC-RULE-VIRTUALIZATION:BEGIN */
+const ATLAS_LOGIC_VIRTUAL_OVERSCAN_PX = 700;
+const ATLAS_LOGIC_VIRTUAL_ESTIMATED_HEIGHT = 118;
+const atlasLogicVirtualHeights = new Map();
+let atlasLogicVirtualFrameId = 0;
+let atlasLogicVirtualDragMode = false;
+let atlasLogicVirtualLastWidth = 0;
+
+function atlasLogicVirtualEntries() {
+  return typeof atlasVisibleStepEntries === "function"
+    ? atlasVisibleStepEntries()
+    : hierarchicalStepEntries({ includeDisabled: true });
+}
+
+function atlasLogicVirtualHeight(entry) {
+  return (
+    atlasLogicVirtualHeights.get(entry.step.id) ||
+    ATLAS_LOGIC_VIRTUAL_ESTIMATED_HEIGHT
+  );
+}
+
+function atlasLogicVirtualOffsets(entries) {
+  const offsets = [0];
+  entries.forEach((entry) => {
+    offsets.push(offsets[offsets.length - 1] + atlasLogicVirtualHeight(entry));
+  });
+  return offsets;
+}
+
+function atlasLogicVirtualIndexAtOffset(offsets, value) {
+  let low = 0;
+  let high = Math.max(0, offsets.length - 2);
+  while (low < high) {
+    const middle = Math.floor((low + high) / 2);
+    if (offsets[middle + 1] <= value) low = middle + 1;
+    else high = middle;
+  }
+  return low;
+}
+
+function atlasLogicVirtualRange(entries, offsets, list) {
+  if (!entries.length) return { start: 0, end: 0 };
+  if (atlasLogicVirtualDragMode) return { start: 0, end: entries.length };
+  const top = Math.max(0, list.scrollTop - ATLAS_LOGIC_VIRTUAL_OVERSCAN_PX);
+  const bottom =
+    list.scrollTop + list.clientHeight + ATLAS_LOGIC_VIRTUAL_OVERSCAN_PX;
+  const start = atlasLogicVirtualIndexAtOffset(offsets, top);
+  const end = Math.min(
+    entries.length,
+    atlasLogicVirtualIndexAtOffset(offsets, bottom) + 1,
+  );
+  return { start, end: Math.max(start + 1, end) };
+}
+
+function atlasLogicVirtualSpacer(position, height) {
+  return `<div class="logic-virtual-spacer logic-virtual-spacer-${position}" data-logic-virtual-spacer="${position}" aria-hidden="true" style="height:${Math.max(0, Math.round(height))}px;min-height:${Math.max(0, Math.round(height))}px;flex:0 0 auto;pointer-events:none"></div>`;
+}
+
+function atlasMeasureLogicVirtualCards(list, anchorId, anchorOffset) {
+  let changedAboveAnchor = 0;
+  list.querySelectorAll(".logic-rule-card[data-id]").forEach((card) => {
+    const style = getComputedStyle(card);
+    const measured = Math.ceil(
+      card.getBoundingClientRect().height +
+        (parseFloat(style.marginTop) || 0) +
+        (parseFloat(style.marginBottom) || 0),
+    );
+    const id = card.dataset.id;
+    const previous =
+      atlasLogicVirtualHeights.get(id) || ATLAS_LOGIC_VIRTUAL_ESTIMATED_HEIGHT;
+    if (measured > 0 && Math.abs(measured - previous) > 1) {
+      atlasLogicVirtualHeights.set(id, measured);
+      if (anchorId && card.offsetTop < anchorOffset)
+        changedAboveAnchor += measured - previous;
+    }
+  });
+  if (changedAboveAnchor) list.scrollTop += changedAboveAnchor;
+}
+
+function atlasRenderVirtualLogicRules(options = {}) {
+  const list = e.stepsList || $("stepsList");
+  if (!list) return;
+  (state.audit.steps || []).forEach((step, index) =>
+    normalizeCatalogStep?.(step, index),
+  );
+  (state.audit.steps || []).forEach((step) => {
+    if (step.step_type === "if_then" && step.params?.inverse_else !== "Yes")
+      syncInverseElseStepsForSource?.(step.id);
+  });
+
+  const entries = atlasLogicVirtualEntries();
+  const width = Math.round(list.getBoundingClientRect().width);
+  if (atlasLogicVirtualLastWidth && width !== atlasLogicVirtualLastWidth)
+    atlasLogicVirtualHeights.clear();
+  atlasLogicVirtualLastWidth = width;
+
+  let offsets = atlasLogicVirtualOffsets(entries);
+  const selectedIndex = entries.findIndex(
+    (entry) => entry.step.id === state.selectedStepId,
+  );
+  if (
+    selectedIndex >= 0 &&
+    !atlasLogicVirtualDragMode &&
+    (offsets[selectedIndex + 1] < list.scrollTop ||
+      offsets[selectedIndex] > list.scrollTop + list.clientHeight)
+  ) {
+    list.scrollTop = Math.max(
+      0,
+      offsets[selectedIndex] - Math.max(24, Math.floor(list.clientHeight / 3)),
+    );
+  }
+
+  const range = atlasLogicVirtualRange(entries, offsets, list);
+  const anchor = entries[range.start]?.step.id || "";
+  const anchorOffset = offsets[range.start] || 0;
+  const cards = entries
+    .slice(range.start, range.end)
+    .map((entry) => renderLogicRuleCard(entry))
+    .join("");
+  list.style.gap = "0";
+  list.innerHTML = entries.length
+    ? `${atlasLogicVirtualSpacer("before", offsets[range.start])}${cards}${atlasLogicVirtualSpacer("after", offsets[offsets.length - 1] - offsets[range.end])}`
+    : "";
+
+  atlasMeasureLogicVirtualCards(list, anchor, anchorOffset);
+  offsets = atlasLogicVirtualOffsets(entries);
+  const before = list.querySelector('[data-logic-virtual-spacer="before"]');
+  const after = list.querySelector('[data-logic-virtual-spacer="after"]');
+  if (before) {
+    before.style.height = `${Math.max(0, Math.round(offsets[range.start]))}px`;
+    before.style.minHeight = before.style.height;
+  }
+  if (after) {
+    after.style.height = `${Math.max(0, Math.round(offsets[offsets.length - 1] - offsets[range.end]))}px`;
+    after.style.minHeight = after.style.height;
+  }
+
+  if (options.includeSelectedStep !== false) renderSelectedStep?.();
+  if (options.includeLogicTable !== false) renderLogicTablePreviewRows?.();
+  requestAnimationFrame(atlasSyncRulesHeightToEditor);
+}
+
+function atlasScheduleVirtualLogicRules() {
+  if (atlasLogicVirtualFrameId) return;
+  atlasLogicVirtualFrameId = requestAnimationFrame(() => {
+    atlasLogicVirtualFrameId = 0;
+    atlasRenderVirtualLogicRules({
+      includeSelectedStep: false,
+      includeLogicTable: false,
+    });
+  });
+}
+
+function atlasRevealLogicStep(stepId, options = {}) {
+  const entries = atlasLogicVirtualEntries();
+  const index = entries.findIndex((entry) => entry.step.id === stepId);
+  const list = e.stepsList || $("stepsList");
+  if (!list || index < 0) return false;
+  state.selectedStepId = stepId;
+  const offsets = atlasLogicVirtualOffsets(entries);
+  const align = options.align || "center";
+  const targetTop = offsets[index];
+  const targetHeight = atlasLogicVirtualHeight(entries[index]);
+  list.scrollTop =
+    align === "start"
+      ? targetTop
+      : Math.max(0, targetTop - (list.clientHeight - targetHeight) / 2);
+  atlasRenderVirtualLogicRules({
+    includeSelectedStep: options.includeSelectedStep !== false,
+    includeLogicTable: options.includeLogicTable !== false,
+  });
+  requestAnimationFrame(() => {
+    const card = Array.from(
+      list.querySelectorAll(".logic-rule-card[data-id]"),
+    ).find((item) => item.dataset.id === stepId);
+    if (!card) return;
+    if (options.highlight) card.classList.add("logic-issue-target-highlight");
+    if (options.focus !== false) {
+      const focusTarget =
+        card.querySelector("[data-select-step], button, [tabindex]") || card;
+      if (!focusTarget.hasAttribute("tabindex") && focusTarget === card)
+        focusTarget.tabIndex = -1;
+      focusTarget.focus({ preventScroll: true });
+    }
+  });
+  return true;
+}
+
+function atlasInvalidateLogicVirtualMeasurements(stepId = "") {
+  if (stepId) atlasLogicVirtualHeights.delete(stepId);
+  else atlasLogicVirtualHeights.clear();
+}
+
+renderSteps = function renderStepsVirtualized(options = {}) {
+  atlasRenderVirtualLogicRules(options);
+};
+
+document.addEventListener(
+  "scroll",
+  (event) => {
+    const list = e.stepsList || $("stepsList");
+    if (event.target === list && !atlasLogicVirtualDragMode)
+      atlasScheduleVirtualLogicRules();
+  },
+  true,
+);
+
+window.addEventListener("resize", () => {
+  atlasInvalidateLogicVirtualMeasurements();
+  atlasScheduleVirtualLogicRules();
+});
+
+function atlasExpandLogicVirtualWindowForDrag() {
+  const list = e.stepsList || $("stepsList");
+  if (!list) return;
+  const entries = atlasLogicVirtualEntries();
+  const mounted = Array.from(
+    list.querySelectorAll(".logic-rule-card[data-id]"),
+  );
+  if (!mounted.length) {
+    atlasRenderVirtualLogicRules({
+      includeSelectedStep: false,
+      includeLogicTable: false,
+    });
+    return;
+  }
+  const firstIndex = entries.findIndex(
+    (entry) => entry.step.id === mounted[0].dataset.id,
+  );
+  const lastIndex = entries.findIndex(
+    (entry) => entry.step.id === mounted[mounted.length - 1].dataset.id,
+  );
+  list
+    .querySelectorAll("[data-logic-virtual-spacer]")
+    .forEach((node) => node.remove());
+  mounted[0].insertAdjacentHTML(
+    "beforebegin",
+    entries
+      .slice(0, Math.max(0, firstIndex))
+      .map((entry) => renderLogicRuleCard(entry))
+      .join(""),
+  );
+  mounted[mounted.length - 1].insertAdjacentHTML(
+    "afterend",
+    entries
+      .slice(Math.max(0, lastIndex + 1))
+      .map((entry) => renderLogicRuleCard(entry))
+      .join(""),
+  );
+}
+
+document.addEventListener(
+  "pointerdown",
+  (event) => {
+    const dragHandle = event.target.closest?.(".compact-dragger");
+    if (!dragHandle?.closest(".logic-rule-card[draggable='true']")) return;
+    atlasLogicVirtualDragMode = true;
+    atlasExpandLogicVirtualWindowForDrag();
+  },
+  true,
+);
+
+function atlasEndLogicVirtualDrag() {
+  if (!atlasLogicVirtualDragMode) return;
+  atlasLogicVirtualDragMode = false;
+  atlasScheduleVirtualLogicRules();
+}
+document.addEventListener("pointerup", atlasEndLogicVirtualDrag, true);
+document.addEventListener("dragend", atlasEndLogicVirtualDrag, true);
+document.addEventListener("drop", atlasEndLogicVirtualDrag, true);
+/* ATLAS-LOGIC-RULE-VIRTUALIZATION:END */
+

+END FILE: js/15g-visual-ui-events.js

+/**
+ * 16-page-shell.js
+ *
+ * Page shell enhancements for dashboard metrics, topbar audit switching, navigation lock state, flow navigation, and field display toggle rendering.
+ * Auto-generated documentation comments explain function purpose, expected inputs, and module sections without changing runtime behavior.
+ */
+
+// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
+// Dashboard metric helpers
+// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
+/**
+ * Sets textContent for an element by id.
+ * @param {any} id - Element id or record id to look up.
+ * @param {any} value - Value to process or assign.
+ */
+function setText(id, value) {
+  const el = $(id);
+  if (el) el.textContent = value;
+}
+
+/**
+ * Updates dashboard, overview, and group snapshot metrics.
+ */
+function updateDashboardMetrics() {
+  const repoAudits = repoLibraryState?.audits?.length || 0;
+  const savedAudits = state.savedFlows?.length || 0;
+  const selectedSources = state.audit.sources?.length || 0;
+  const logicSteps = state.audit.steps?.length || 0;
+  const sourceFiles = repoLibraryState?.sourceFiles?.length || 0;
+  const vanityLabels = repoLibraryState?.vanityLabels?.length || 0;
+  const sourceAliases = repoLibraryState?.sourceAliases?.length || 0;
+  const fieldCount =
+    (state.audit.fields || []).length ||
+    (state.audit.sources || []).reduce(
+      (total, source) =>
+        total +
+        (typeof fieldsForSource === "function"
+          ? fieldsForSource(source.name).length
+          : 0),
+      0,
+    );
+
+  setText("metricRepoAudits", repoAudits.toLocaleString());
+  setText("metricSavedAudits", savedAudits.toLocaleString());
+  setText("metricSelectedSources", selectedSources.toLocaleString());
+  setText("metricLogicSteps", logicSteps.toLocaleString());
+  setText("metricSourceFiles", sourceFiles.toLocaleString());
+  setText("metricVanityLabels", vanityLabels.toLocaleString());
+  setText("metricSourceAliases", sourceAliases.toLocaleString());
+  setText("overviewSourceCount", selectedSources.toLocaleString());
+  setText("overviewFieldCount", fieldCount.toLocaleString());
+  setText("overviewStepCount", logicSteps.toLocaleString());
+  setText("selectedSourceBadge", selectedSources.toLocaleString());
+
+  const progress = Math.min(
+    100,
+    Math.round(
+      ([
+        state.audit.name && state.audit.name !== "New Audit Logic Flow",
+        !!state.audit.purpose,
+        selectedSources > 0,
+        fieldCount > 0,
+        logicSteps > 0,
+      ].filter(Boolean).length /
+        5) *
+        100,
+    ),
+  );
+  setText("overviewProgressBadge", `${progress}% ready`);
+
+  const snapshot = $("groupAuditSnapshot");
+  if (snapshot) {
+    snapshot.innerHTML = `
+      <div class="package"><span>Audit</span><strong>${esc(state.audit.name || "Untitled Audit")}</strong></div>
+      <div class="package"><span>Purpose</span><strong>${state.audit.purpose ? "Entered" : "Missing"}</strong></div>
+      <div class="package"><span>Sources</span><strong>${selectedSources.toLocaleString()}</strong></div>
+      <div class="package"><span>Logic steps</span><strong>${logicSteps.toLocaleString()}</strong></div>
+    `;
+  }
+  const savedList = $("groupSavedAuditList");
+  if (savedList) {
+    const flows = (state.savedFlows || []).slice(0, 6);
+    savedList.innerHTML = flows.length
+      ? flows
+          .map(
+            (flow) =>
+              `<div class="package"><span>${esc(flow.name || "Saved Audit")}</span><small>${esc(flow.updatedAt ? new Date(flow.updatedAt).toLocaleString() : "")}</small></div>`,
+          )
+          .join("")
+      : '<p class="hint">Saved audits will appear here after local saves are loaded.</p>';
+  }
+}
+
+// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
+// Render wrappers for page shell
+// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
+function renderPageShellStatus() {
+  updateDashboardMetrics();
+  updateNavigationLockState?.();
+  renderFlowNavControls?.();
+  renderActiveAuditSelect?.();
+  renderFieldDisplayModeButton?.();
+}
+
+const pageShellRenderAllBase =
+  typeof renderAll === "function" ? renderAll : null;
+if (pageShellRenderAllBase) {
+  /**
+   * Central render coordinator for the current audit UI.
+   */
+  renderAll = function renderAllWithPageShell(options = {}) {
+    pageShellRenderAllBase();
+    if (options.shell !== false) renderPageShellStatus();
+  };
+}
+
+const pageShellRefreshSavedBase =
+  typeof refreshSaved === "function" ? refreshSaved : null;
+if (pageShellRefreshSavedBase) {
+  /**
+   * Loads saved flows into state and updates the saved-flow selector.
+   */
+  refreshSaved = async function refreshSavedWithPageShell() {
+    await pageShellRefreshSavedBase();
+    if (typeof renderAuditLibrarySelect === "function")
+      renderAuditLibrarySelect();
+    updateDashboardMetrics();
+    renderActiveAuditSelect?.();
+    renderFieldDisplayModeButton?.();
+  };
+}
+
+// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
+// Topbar audit switcher
+// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
+/**
+ * Builds audit options for the topbar selector.
+ */
+function topbarAuditOptions() {
+  const options = [];
+  (repoLibraryState?.audits || []).forEach((audit) =>
+    options.push({
+      group: "Repo Audits",
+      value: `repo:${audit.id}`,
+      label: audit.name || audit.id,
+    }),
+  );
+  (state.savedFlows || []).forEach((flow) =>
+    options.push({
+      group: "Local Saved Audits",
+      value: `local:${flow.id}`,
+      label: flow.name || "Saved audit",
+    }),
+  );
+  if (typeof workbookState !== "undefined" && workbookState.loaded) {
+    (workbookState.auditSheets || []).forEach((name) =>
+      options.push({
+        group: "Workbook Imports",
+        value: `workbook:${name}`,
+        label: name,
+      }),
+    );
+  }
+  if (
+    hasSelectedAudit() &&
+    String(state.activeAuditValue || "").startsWith("draft:") &&
+    !options.some((option) => option.value === state.activeAuditValue)
+  ) {
+    options.push({
+      group: "Current Draft",
+      value: state.activeAuditValue,
+      label: state.audit.name || "Unsaved Draft",
+    });
+  }
+  if (
+    hasSelectedAudit() &&
+    state.activeAuditValue &&
+    !options.some((option) => option.value === state.activeAuditValue) &&
+    state.audit.name
+  ) {
+    options.push({
+      group: "Current Audit",
+      value: state.activeAuditValue,
+      label: state.audit.name,
+    });
+  }
+  return options;
+}
+
+/**
+ * Returns the active page name.
+ */
+function activePageName() {
+  return (
+    document.querySelector(".page.active")?.dataset.page ||
+    localStorage.getItem("atlasAlignActiveSection") ||
+    "my-audits"
+  );
+}
+
+/**
+ * Shows or hides the topbar audit switcher.
+ */
+function renderTopbarAuditSwitcherVisibility() {
+  const wrapper = document.querySelector(".topbar-audit-switcher");
+  if (!wrapper) return;
+  wrapper.hidden = activePageName() === "my-audits";
+}
+
+/**
+ * Returns the audit value staged in the topbar selector.
+ */
+function pendingActiveAuditValue() {
+  return state.pendingActiveAuditValue || $("activeAuditSelect")?.value || "";
+}
+
+/**
+ * Renders the topbar active-audit selector.
+ */
+function renderActiveAuditSelect() {
+  const select = $("activeAuditSelect");
+  const confirmButton = $("confirmActiveAuditBtn");
+  renderTopbarAuditSwitcherVisibility();
+  if (!select) return;
+  const options = topbarAuditOptions();
+  const stagedValue = state.pendingActiveAuditValue || "";
+  if (!hasSelectedAudit()) {
+    const placeholder = options.length
+      ? "Select an audit..."
+      : "No audit selected";
+    const groups = [...new Set(options.map((option) => option.group))];
+    select.innerHTML =
+      `<option value="">${esc(placeholder)}</option>` +
+      groups
+        .map(
+          (group) =>
+            `<optgroup label="${esc(group)}">${options
+              .filter((option) => option.group === group)
+              .map(
+                (option) =>
+                  `<option value="${esc(option.value)}">${esc(option.label)}</option>`,
+              )
+              .join("")}</optgroup>`,
+        )
+        .join("");
+    select.disabled = !options.length;
+    select.value =
+      stagedValue && options.some((option) => option.value === stagedValue)
+        ? stagedValue
+        : "";
+    if (confirmButton) confirmButton.disabled = !select.value;
+    return;
+  }
+  let renderOptions = options;
+  if (
+    state.activeAuditValue &&
+    state.audit.name &&
+    !renderOptions.some((option) => option.value === state.activeAuditValue)
+  ) {
+    renderOptions = [
+      {
+        group: "Current Audit",
+        value: state.activeAuditValue,
+        label: state.audit.name,
+      },
+      ...renderOptions,
+    ];
+  }
+  const groups = [...new Set(renderOptions.map((option) => option.group))];
+  select.innerHTML = groups
+    .map(
+      (group) =>
+        `<optgroup label="${esc(group)}">${renderOptions
+          .filter((option) => option.group === group)
+          .map(
+            (option) =>
+              `<option value="${esc(option.value)}">${esc(option.label)}</option>`,
+          )
+          .join("")}</optgroup>`,
+    )
+    .join("");
+  select.disabled = !renderOptions.length;
+  const valueToShow =
+    stagedValue && renderOptions.some((option) => option.value === stagedValue)
+      ? stagedValue
+      : state.activeAuditValue;
+  if (
+    valueToShow &&
+    renderOptions.some((option) => option.value === valueToShow)
+  )
+    select.value = valueToShow;
+  if (confirmButton)
+    confirmButton.disabled =
+      !select.value || select.value === state.activeAuditValue;
+}
+
+// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
+// Display-mode button
+// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
+/**
+ * Renders the vanity/raw display toggle button.
+ */
+function renderFieldDisplayModeButton() {
+  const button = $("fieldDisplayModeBtn");
+  if (!button) return;
+
+  const mode = fieldDisplayMode?.() || "source";
+  const nextMode = mode === "vanity" ? "source field names" : "vanity labels";
+
+  button.innerHTML =
+    mode === "vanity"
+      ? typeof atlasVanityLabelIconSvg === "function"
+        ? atlasVanityLabelIconSvg()
+        : ""
+      : "Aa";
+
+  button.title = `Switch to ${nextMode}`;
+  button.setAttribute("aria-label", `Switch to ${nextMode}`);
+  button.classList.toggle("active", mode === "vanity");
+}

+// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
+// Workflow navigation
+// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
+const AUDIT_FLOW_PAGES = [
+  { page: "overview", label: "Audit Overview", nextLabel: "Select Sources" },
+  { page: "fields", label: "Select Sources", nextLabel: "Build Logic" },
+  { page: "steps", label: "Logic Builder", nextLabel: "Requirements Preview" },
+  {
+    page: "preview",
+    label: "Requirements Preview",
+    nextLabel: "Readiness & Review",
+  },
+  {
+    page: "review",
+    label: "Readiness & Review",
+    nextLabel: "Publication Preview",
+  },
+  { page: "save", label: "Publication Preview" },
+];
+
+/**
+ * Locks or unlocks navigation based on active audit state.
+ */
+function updateNavigationLockState() {
+  const unlockedPages = new Set(["my-audits", "group"]);
+  const locked = !hasSelectedAudit();
+  document.body.classList.toggle("audit-selected", !locked);
+  document.body.classList.toggle("audit-not-selected", locked);
+  document.querySelectorAll(".navitem, .rail-item").forEach((button) => {
+    const targetPage = pageForSection(
+      button.dataset.section ||
+        button.dataset.page ||
+        button.dataset.panel ||
+        "",
+    );
+    const shouldLock = locked && !unlockedPages.has(targetPage);
+    button.classList.toggle("is-locked", shouldLock);
+    if (shouldLock) {
+      button.setAttribute("aria-disabled", "true");
+      button.setAttribute("title", "Select or create an audit first.");
+    } else {
+      button.removeAttribute("aria-disabled");
+      button.removeAttribute("title");
+    }
+  });
+  renderFlowNavControls?.();
+}
+
+/**
+ * Returns the active page for flow navigation.
+ */
+function activeAuditFlowPage() {
+  return (
+    document.querySelector(".page.active")?.dataset.page ||
+    localStorage.getItem("atlasAlignActiveSection") ||
+    "my-audits"
+  );
+}
+
+/**
+ * Renders Back/Next controls for the audit workflow.
+ */
+function renderFlowNavControls() {
+  const controls = $("flowNavControls");
+  const back = $("flowBackBtn");
+  const next = $("flowNextBtn");
+  if (!controls || !back || !next) return;
+  const currentPage = activeAuditFlowPage();
+  const currentIndex = AUDIT_FLOW_PAGES.findIndex(
+    (item) => item.page === currentPage,
+  );
+  const showControls = hasSelectedAudit() && currentIndex >= 0;
+  controls.hidden = !showControls;
+  if (!showControls) {
+    back.hidden = true;
+    next.hidden = true;
+    back.removeAttribute("data-flow-target");
+    next.removeAttribute("data-flow-target");
+    return;
+  }
+  const previous = AUDIT_FLOW_PAGES[currentIndex - 1];
+  const current = AUDIT_FLOW_PAGES[currentIndex];
+  const nextPage = AUDIT_FLOW_PAGES[currentIndex + 1];
+  back.hidden = !previous;
+  if (previous) {
+    back.textContent = `Back: ${previous.label}`;
+    back.dataset.flowTarget = previous.page;
+  } else {
+    back.removeAttribute("data-flow-target");
+  }
+  next.hidden = !nextPage;
+  if (nextPage) {
+    next.textContent = `Next: ${current.nextLabel || nextPage.label}`;
+    next.dataset.flowTarget = nextPage.page;
+  } else {
+    next.removeAttribute("data-flow-target");
+  }
+}
+
+// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
+// Page shell event handlers
+// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
+document.addEventListener("click", (event) => {
+  const flowNav = event.target.closest("[data-flow-target]");
+  if (flowNav) {
+    event.preventDefault();
+    atlasNavigateToSection(flowNav.dataset.flowTarget).catch((err) =>
+      status(`Navigation save failed: ${err.message}`, "bad"),
+    );
+    renderActiveAuditSelect?.();
+    renderFieldDisplayModeButton?.();
+    return;
+  }
+  const nav = event.target.closest(".navitem");
+  if (nav) {
+    if (nav.classList.contains("is-locked")) {
+      event.preventDefault();
+      event.stopPropagation();
+      return;
+    }
+    event.preventDefault();
+    atlasNavigateToSection(nav.dataset.section).catch((err) =>
+      status(`Navigation save failed: ${err.message}`, "bad"),
+    );
+    renderActiveAuditSelect?.();
+    renderFieldDisplayModeButton?.();
+    return;
+  }
+  if (event.target.closest("#collapseNavBtn")) {
+    $("sidebar")?.classList.toggle("collapsed");
+    $("main")?.classList.toggle("expanded");
+    localStorage.setItem(
+      "atlasAlignNavCollapsed",
+      $("sidebar")?.classList.contains("collapsed") ? "Yes" : "No",
+    );
+    return;
+  }
+  if (event.target.closest("#myAuditsNewAuditBtn")) {
+    $("newAuditBtn")?.click();
+    return;
+  }
+  const previewTab = event.target.closest("[data-preview-tab]");
+  if (previewTab) {
+    document
+      .querySelectorAll("[data-preview-tab]")
+      .forEach((tab) => tab.classList.toggle("active", tab === previewTab));
+    if (e.previewStyle) e.previewStyle.value = previewTab.dataset.previewTab;
+    renderPreview?.();
+  }
+});
+
+globalThis.ATLASAssignmentEditor = Object.freeze({
+  add: atlasAddAuditAssignment,
+  searchRecent: atlasSearchCachedUsers,
+  searchDirectory: atlasSearchDirectoryProfiles,
+  displayName: atlasDisplayUser,
+});
+
+document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", () => {
+  if (localStorage.getItem("atlasAlignNavCollapsed") === "Yes") {
+    $("sidebar")?.classList.add("collapsed");
+    $("main")?.classList.add("expanded");
+  }
+  updateNavigationLockState?.();
+  renderFlowNavControls?.();
+  renderActiveAuditSelect?.();
+  renderFieldDisplayModeButton?.();
+});
+
+// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
+// Final audit switcher override
+// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
+/* Final audit switcher rendering and Switch button refinements */
+function atlasOptionHtml(option) {
+  return `<option value="${esc(option.value)}">${esc(option.label || option.value)}</option>`;
+}
+/**
+ * Groups audit options for the topbar selector.
+ * @param {any} options - Configuration/options object.
+ * @param {any} includePlaceholder - Input used by atlasGroupedAuditOptionsHtml.
+ * @param {any} placeholder - Input used by atlasGroupedAuditOptionsHtml.
+ */
+function atlasGroupedAuditOptionsHtml(
+  options,
+  includePlaceholder = false,
+  placeholder = "Select an audit...",
+) {
+  const groups = [
+    ...new Set((options || []).map((option) => option.group || "Audits")),
+  ];
+  const placeholderHtml = includePlaceholder
+    ? `<option value="">${esc(placeholder)}</option>`
+    : "";
+  return (
+    placeholderHtml +
+    groups
+      .map(
+        (group) =>
+          `<optgroup label="${esc(group)}">${options
+            .filter((option) => (option.group || "Audits") === group)
+            .map(atlasOptionHtml)
+            .join("")}</optgroup>`,
+      )
+      .join("")
+  );
+}
+/**
+ * Renders the topbar active-audit selector.
+ */
+renderActiveAuditSelect = function renderActiveAuditSelectFinal() {
+  const select = $("activeAuditSelect");
+  const confirmButton = $("confirmActiveAuditBtn");
+  renderTopbarAuditSwitcherVisibility?.();
+  if (!select) return;
+  const options = topbarAuditOptions?.() || [];
+  const hasOptions = options.length > 0;
+  let renderOptions = options;
+  if (
+    hasSelectedAudit?.() &&
+    state.activeAuditValue &&
+    state.audit?.name &&
+    !renderOptions.some((option) => option.value === state.activeAuditValue)
+  ) {
+    renderOptions = [
+      {
+        group: "Current Audit",
+        value: state.activeAuditValue,
+        label: state.audit.name,
+      },
+      ...renderOptions,
+    ];
+  }
+  const stagedValue = state.pendingActiveAuditValue || "";
+  const includePlaceholder = !hasSelectedAudit?.();
+  const placeholder = hasOptions ? "Select an audit..." : "No audit selected";
+  select.innerHTML = atlasGroupedAuditOptionsHtml(
+    renderOptions,
+    includePlaceholder,
+    placeholder,
+  );
+  select.disabled = !renderOptions.length;
+  const valueToShow =
+    stagedValue && renderOptions.some((option) => option.value === stagedValue)
+      ? stagedValue
+      : state.activeAuditValue || "";
+  if (
+    valueToShow &&
+    renderOptions.some((option) => option.value === valueToShow)
+  )
+    select.value = valueToShow;
+  else select.value = includePlaceholder ? "" : renderOptions[0]?.value || "";
+  if (confirmButton) {
+    confirmButton.textContent = "Switch";
+    confirmButton.title = "Switch audit";
+    confirmButton.setAttribute("aria-label", "Switch audit");
+    confirmButton.classList.remove("secondary", "icon-button");
+    confirmButton.classList.add("primary");
+    confirmButton.disabled =
+      !select.value || select.value === state.activeAuditValue;
+  }
+};
+
+// Database-only audit selectors and metrics.
+topbarAuditOptions = function topbarDatabaseAuditOptions() {
+  const options = (state.databaseAudits || []).map((audit) => ({
+    group: "Database Audits",
+    value: `database:${audit.id}`,
+    label: `${audit.name || audit.id} · r${audit.currentRevisionNumber || 0}`,
+  }));
+  if (typeof workbookState !== "undefined" && workbookState.loaded)
+    (workbookState.auditSheets || []).forEach((name) =>
+      options.push({
+        group: "Workbook Imports",
+        value: `workbook:${name}`,
+        label: name,
+      }),
+    );
+  if (
+    hasSelectedAudit() &&
+    String(state.activeAuditValue || "").startsWith("draft:")
+  )
+    options.push({
+      group: "Current Draft",
+      value: state.activeAuditValue,
+      label: state.audit.name || "Unsaved Draft",
+    });
+  return options;
+};
+const atlasDatabaseMetricsBase = updateDashboardMetrics;
+updateDashboardMetrics = function updateDatabaseOnlyMetrics() {
+  atlasDatabaseMetricsBase();
+  setText(
+    "metricSavedAudits",
+    (state.databaseAudits || []).length.toLocaleString(),
+  );
+  setText(
+    "auditRevisionBadge",
+    state.auditPersistence?.revisionNumber
+      ? `Revision ${state.auditPersistence.revisionNumber}`
+      : "Unsaved",
+  );
+  const savedList = $("groupSavedAuditList");
+  if (savedList) {
+    const audits = (state.databaseAudits || []).slice(0, 6);
+    savedList.innerHTML = audits.length
+      ? audits
+          .map(
+            (audit) =>
+              `<div class="package"><span>${esc(audit.name || audit.id)}</span><small>${esc(audit.updatedAt ? new Date(audit.updatedAt).toLocaleString() : "")}</small></div>`,
+          )
+          .join("")
+      : '<p class="hint">No database audits yet.</p>';
+  }
+};
+
+const ATLAS_ASSIGNMENT_ROLES = ["assigned", "backup", "developer"];
+
+// Assignment search state is required by the initial page-shell render and by
+// the input handlers registered later during bootstrap.
+state.assignmentUserSearch = state.assignmentUserSearch || {};
+ATLAS_ASSIGNMENT_ROLES.forEach((role) => {
+  state.assignmentUserSearch[role] = state.assignmentUserSearch[role] || {
+    query: "",
+    results: [],
+    loading: false,
+    error: "",
+    source: "recent",
+    controller: null,
+  };
+});
+// Signed-in profile, personal audit work queue, and assignment editor.
+function atlasUserInitials(user) {
+  const source = String(user?.fullName || user?.username || "").trim();
+  if (!source) return "?";
+  const parts = source.split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);
+  return (
+    parts.length > 1 ? parts[0][0] + parts.at(-1)[0] : source.slice(0, 2)
+  ).toUpperCase();
+}
+function atlasDisplayUser(user) {
+  return String(user?.fullName || user?.username || "Unknown user");
+}
+function atlasUserMatches(left, right) {
+  const leftId = String(left?.id || "").trim(),
+    rightId = String(right?.id || "").trim();
+  if (leftId && rightId) return leftId === rightId;
+  const a = String(left?.username || "")
+      .trim()
+      .toLowerCase(),
+    b = String(right?.username || "")
+      .trim()
+      .toLowerCase();
+  return !!a && a === b;
+}
+function atlasAssignmentRole(role) {
+  return ATLAS_ASSIGNMENT_ROLES.includes(role) ? role : "";
+}
+function atlasEnsureAuditAssignments() {
+  const assignments = normalizeAuditAssignments?.(state.audit?.assignments) || {
+    assigned: [],
+    backup: [],
+    developer: [],
+  };
+  state.audit.assignments = assignments;
+  return assignments;
+}
+function atlasCommitAssignmentMutation(role) {
+  renderAssignmentEditor();
+  renderMyAudits();
+  atlasInvalidatePages?.(["my-audits", "overview"]);
+  scheduleAutosave?.();
+  const roleLabel =
+    {
+      assigned: "Assigned Analysts",
+      backup: "Backup Analysts",
+      developer: "Developers",
+    }[role] || "Audit Assignments";
+  status?.(`${roleLabel} updated.`, "ok");
+}
+function atlasAddAuditAssignment(role, user) {
+  const validRole = atlasAssignmentRole(role);
+  const normalizedUser = normalizeAssignmentUser?.(user);
+  if (!validRole || !normalizedUser || user?.disabled === true) return false;
+  const assignments = atlasEnsureAuditAssignments();
+  if (
+    assignments[validRole].some((value) =>
+      atlasUserMatches(value, normalizedUser),
+    )
+  )
+    return false;
+  assignments[validRole].push(normalizedUser);
+  atlasCommitAssignmentMutation(validRole);
+  return true;
+}
+function atlasRemoveAuditAssignment(role, identity) {
+  const validRole = atlasAssignmentRole(role);
+  if (!validRole) return false;
+  const assignments = atlasEnsureAuditAssignments();
+  const current = assignments[validRole];
+  const next = current.filter((user) => !atlasUserMatches(user, identity));
+  if (next.length === current.length) return false;
+  assignments[validRole] = next;
+  atlasCommitAssignmentMutation(validRole);
+  return true;
+}
+function atlasAuditMembership(audit) {
+  const current = state.currentUser,
+    assignments = normalizeAuditAssignments?.(audit?.assignments) || {
+      assigned: [],
+      backup: [],
+      developer: [],
+    };
+  if (!current || state.currentUserStatus !== "ready")
+    return { assigned: false, backup: false, developer: false, role: "Other" };
+  const assigned = assignments.assigned.some((user) =>
+    atlasUserMatches(user, current),
+  );
+  const backup = assignments.backup.some((user) =>
+    atlasUserMatches(user, current),
+  );
+  const developer = assignments.developer.some((user) =>
+    atlasUserMatches(user, current),
+  );
+
+  return {
+    assigned,
+    backup,
+    developer,
+    role: assigned
+      ? "Assigned Analyst"
+      : backup
+        ? "Backup Analyst"
+        : developer
+          ? "Developer"
+          : "Other",
+  };
+}
+
+function renderCurrentUserProfile() {
+  const root = $("currentUserProfile");
+  if (!root) return;
+  root.replaceChildren();
+  if (
+    state.currentUserStatus === "loading" ||
+    state.currentUserStatus === "idle"
+  ) {
+    root.textContent = "Loading profile…";
+    root.setAttribute("aria-label", "Loading signed-in user profile");
+    return;
+  }
+  if (state.currentUserStatus !== "ready" || !state.currentUser) {
+    root.textContent = "Profile unavailable";
+    root.setAttribute("aria-label", "Signed-in user profile unavailable");
+    return;
+  }
+
+  const user = state.currentUser,
+    avatar = document.createElement("span");
+  avatar.className = "avatar profile-avatar";
+  avatar.textContent = atlasUserInitials(user);
+  avatar.setAttribute("aria-hidden", "true");
+
+  if (user.avatar) {
+    const img = document.createElement("img");
+    img.src = user.avatar;
+    img.alt = `${atlasDisplayUser(user)} profile photo`;
+    img.addEventListener("error", () => img.remove(), { once: true });
+    avatar.append(img);
+  }
+
+  const text = document.createElement("span");
+  text.className = "profile-text";
+  const primary = document.createElement("strong");
+  primary.textContent = atlasDisplayUser(user);
+  const secondary = document.createElement("span");
+  secondary.className = "profile-secondary";
+  secondary.textContent = user.username || user.email || "";
+  text.append(primary, secondary);
+  root.append(avatar, text);
+  root.setAttribute("aria-label", `Signed in as ${atlasDisplayUser(user)}`);
+}
+function atlasAssignmentNames(users) {
+  return (users || []).map(atlasDisplayUser).join(", ") || "None";
+}
+function atlasAuditSearchText(audit) {
+  const a = normalizeAuditAssignments?.(audit.assignments) || {
+    assigned: [],
+    backup: [],
+    developer: [],
+  };
+
+  return [
+    audit.name,
+    audit.id,
+    audit.purpose,
+    audit.description,
+    ...a.assigned.flatMap((u) => [u.fullName, u.username]),
+    ...a.backup.flatMap((u) => [u.fullName, u.username]),
+    ...a.developer.flatMap((u) => [u.fullName, u.username]),
+  ]
+    .join(" ")
+    .toLowerCase();
+}
+
+function renderMyAudits() {
+  const body = $("myAuditsTableBody"),
+    stateHost = $("myAuditsState");
+  if (!body || !stateHost) return;
+
+  const activeAudits = state.databaseAudits || [],
+    deletedAudits = state.deletedAudits || [],
+    identityReady = state.currentUserStatus === "ready",
+    canManageDeletedAudits = Boolean(
+      identityReady && state.currentUser?.access?.canManageDeletedAudits,
+    ),
+    requestedDeletedMode = state.myAuditsRoleFilter === "deleted",
+    deletedMode = requestedDeletedMode && canManageDeletedAudits,
+    effectiveFilter =
+      requestedDeletedMode && !canManageDeletedAudits
+        ? "all"
+        : state.myAuditsRoleFilter,
+    audits = deletedMode ? deletedAudits : activeAudits,
+    listStatus = deletedMode
+      ? state.deletedAuditsStatus
+      : state.databaseAuditsStatus,
+    listError = deletedMode
+      ? state.deletedAuditsError
+      : state.databaseAuditsError;
+
+  const metrics = {
+    assigned: 0,
+    backup: 0,
+    developer: 0,
+    other: 0,
+    all: activeAudits.length,
+  };
+
+  activeAudits.forEach((audit) => {
+    const membership = atlasAuditMembership(audit);
+    if (identityReady && membership.assigned) metrics.assigned++;
+    if (identityReady && membership.backup) metrics.backup++;
+    if (identityReady && membership.developer) metrics.developer++;
+    if (
+      !identityReady ||
+      (!membership.assigned && !membership.backup && !membership.developer)
+    )
+      metrics.other++;
+  });
+
+  Object.entries(metrics).forEach(([key, value]) =>
+    setText(`myAuditsMetric${key[0].toUpperCase() + key.slice(1)}`, value),
+  );
+
+  const deletedFilter = $("myAuditsDeletedFilter");
+  if (deletedFilter) deletedFilter.hidden = !canManageDeletedAudits;
+  setText("myAuditsDeletedCount", String(deletedAudits.length));
+
+  document.querySelectorAll("[data-my-audits-role]").forEach((button) => {
+    const filter = button.dataset.myAuditsRole;
+    const active = filter === effectiveFilter;
+    button.classList.toggle("active", active);
+    button.setAttribute("aria-pressed", String(active));
+    button.disabled =
+      (filter === "deleted" && !canManageDeletedAudits) ||
+      (!identityReady && filter !== "all");
+  });
+
+  if (listStatus === "loading") {
+    body.replaceChildren();
+    stateHost.textContent = deletedMode
+      ? "Loading Deleted Items…"
+      : "Loading database audits…";
+    stateHost.className = "my-audits-state loading";
+    return;
+  }
+
+  if (listStatus === "error") {
+    body.replaceChildren();
+    const label = deletedMode ? "Deleted Items" : "Audit list";
+    stateHost.textContent = `${label} could not be loaded. ${listError || ""}`;
+    stateHost.className = "my-audits-state error";
+    return;
+  }
+
+  if (!identityReady && effectiveFilter !== "all") {
+    body.replaceChildren();
+    stateHost.textContent =
+      "Personalized audit filters are unavailable because your profile could not be loaded. Choose All to browse audits.";
+    stateHost.className = "my-audits-state error";
+    return;
+  }
+
+  const query = String(state.myAuditsSearch ?? "")
+    .trim()
+    .toLowerCase();
+  const filtered = audits.filter((audit) => {
+    const searchMatches = !query || atlasAuditSearchText(audit).includes(query);
+    if (deletedMode) return searchMatches;
+
+    const membership = atlasAuditMembership(audit);
+    const filterMatches =
+      effectiveFilter === "all" ||
+      (effectiveFilter === "assigned" && membership.assigned) ||
+      (effectiveFilter === "backup" && membership.backup) ||
+      (effectiveFilter === "developer" && membership.developer) ||
+      (effectiveFilter === "other" &&
+        !membership.assigned &&
+        !membership.backup &&
+        !membership.developer);
+    return filterMatches && searchMatches;
+  });
+
+  body.replaceChildren();
+  stateHost.textContent = "";
+  stateHost.className = "my-audits-state";
+
+  filtered.forEach((audit) => {
+    const membership = atlasAuditMembership(audit),
+      assignments = normalizeAuditAssignments?.(audit.assignments) || {
+        assigned: [],
+        backup: [],
+        developer: [],
+      },
+      row = document.createElement("tr");
+    const cells = [
+      [audit.name || audit.id, audit.id],
+      [membership.role, ""],
+      [atlasAssignmentNames(assignments.assigned), ""],
+      [atlasAssignmentNames(assignments.backup), ""],
+      [atlasAssignmentNames(assignments.developer), ""],
+      [
+        audit.updatedAt
+          ? new Date(audit.updatedAt).toLocaleString()
+          : "Not set",
+        "",
+      ],
+    ];
+
+    cells.forEach(([main, sub], index) => {
+      const cell = document.createElement("td");
+      if (index === 1) {
+        const badge = document.createElement("span");
+        badge.className = "badge role-badge";
+        badge.textContent = main;
+        cell.append(badge);
+      } else {
+        const mainText = document.createElement(
+          index === 0 ? "strong" : "span",
+        );
+        mainText.textContent = main;
+        cell.append(mainText);
+        if (sub) {
+          const secondaryText = document.createElement("small");
+          secondaryText.textContent = sub;
+          cell.append(secondaryText);
+        }
+        cell.title = main;
+      }
+      row.append(cell);
+    });
+
+    const action = document.createElement("td");
+    if (deletedMode) {
+      const restore = document.createElement("button");
+      restore.type = "button";
+      restore.className = "btn secondary small";
+      restore.dataset.restoreDeletedAudit = audit.id;
+      restore.textContent = "Restore";
+
+      const remove = document.createElement("button");
+      remove.type = "button";
+      remove.className = "btn secondary small";
+      remove.dataset.permanentlyDeleteAudit = audit.id;
+      remove.textContent = "Delete permanently";
+      action.append(restore, document.createTextNode(" "), remove);
+    } else {
+      const open = document.createElement("button");
+      open.type = "button";
+      open.className = "btn primary small";
+      open.dataset.openDatabaseAudit = audit.id;
+      open.textContent = "Open";
+      action.append(open);
+    }
+    row.append(action);
+    body.append(row);
+  });
+
+  if (!filtered.length) {
+    const messages = {
+      assigned: "No audits are currently assigned to you.",
+      backup: "You are not currently listed as backup on any audits.",
+      developer: "You are not listed as Developer for any audits.",
+      other: "No other audits are available.",
+      all: "No database audits are available.",
+      deleted: "Deleted Items is empty.",
+    };
+    stateHost.textContent = messages[effectiveFilter] || messages.all;
+    stateHost.className = "my-audits-state empty";
+  }
+}
+function atlasRenderAssignmentAvatar(user) {
+  const resolved = atlasResolvedAssignmentUser?.(user) || user;
+  const span = document.createElement("span");
+  span.className = "avatar assignment-avatar";
+  span.textContent = atlasUserInitials(resolved);
+  span.setAttribute("aria-hidden", "true");
+  if (resolved?.avatar) {
+    const img = document.createElement("img");
+    img.src = resolved.avatar;
+    img.alt = "";
+    img.loading = "lazy";
+    img.referrerPolicy = "no-referrer";
+    img.addEventListener("error", () => img.remove(), { once: true });
+    span.append(img);
+  }
+  return span;
+}
+
+function renderAssignmentEditor() {
+  ["assigned", "backup", "developer"].forEach((role) => {
+    const root = $(
+      `assignment${role[0].toUpperCase() + role.slice(1)}Selected`,
+    );
+    if (!root) return;
+    root.replaceChildren();
+    const users =
+      normalizeAuditAssignments?.(state.audit.assignments)?.[role] || [];
+    users.forEach((user) => {
+      const chip = document.createElement("span");
+      chip.className = "assignment-chip";
+      chip.append(atlasRenderAssignmentAvatar(user));
+      const name = document.createElement("span");
+      name.textContent = atlasDisplayUser(
+        atlasResolvedAssignmentUser?.(user) || user,
+      );
+      const remove = document.createElement("button");
+      remove.type = "button";
+      remove.dataset.removeAssignmentRole = role;
+      remove.dataset.assignmentUserId = user.id || "";
+      remove.dataset.assignmentUsername = user.username || "";
+      remove.setAttribute(
+        "aria-label",
+        `Remove ${atlasDisplayUser(user)} from ${role}`,
+      );
+      remove.textContent = "×";
+      chip.append(name, remove);
+      root.append(chip);
+    });
+    if (!users.length)
+      root.textContent = `No ${role === "developer" ? "developers" : role + " analysts"} selected.`;
+    renderAssignmentSearchResults(role);
+  });
+}
+function renderAssignmentSearchResults(role) {
+  const host = $(`assignment${role[0].toUpperCase() + role.slice(1)}Results`);
+  const search = state.assignmentUserSearch[role];
+  if (!host) return;
+  host.replaceChildren();
+  if (search.loading) {
+    host.textContent = "Searching users…";
+    return;
+  }
+  if (search.error) {
+    host.textContent = search.error;
+    host.className = "assignment-results error";
+    return;
+  }
+  host.className = "assignment-results";
+  search.results.forEach((user, index) => {
+    const row = document.createElement("div");
+    row.className = "assignment-result";
+    const identity = document.createElement("span");
+    identity.className = "assignment-result-user";
+    identity.append(atlasRenderAssignmentAvatar(user));
+    const label = document.createElement("span");
+    label.className = "assignment-result-label";
+    const resolvedUser = atlasResolvedAssignmentUser?.(user) || user;
+    label.textContent = `${atlasDisplayUser(resolvedUser)}${resolvedUser.username ? ` · ${resolvedUser.username}` : ""}`;
+    identity.append(label);
+    const action = document.createElement("button");
+    action.type = "button";
+    action.className = "btn primary small assignment-result-action";
+    action.dataset.addAssignmentRole = role;
+    action.dataset.assignmentResultIndex = String(index);
+    const already = (state.audit.assignments?.[role] || []).some((value) =>
+      atlasUserMatches(value, user),
+    );
+    action.disabled = already || user.disabled;
+
+    const roleLabel =
+      {
+        assigned: "Assigned",
+        backup: "Backup",
+        developer: "Developer",
+      }[role] || "Unknown role";
+
+    action.textContent = already
+      ? "Already selected"
+      : `Assign as ${roleLabel}`;
+
+    const ATLAS_ASSIGNMENT_ROLE_LABELS = {
+      assigned: "Assigned",
+      backup: "Backup",
+      developer: "Developer",
+    };
+
+    action.textContent = already
+      ? "Already selected"
+      : `Assign as ${ATLAS_ASSIGNMENT_ROLE_LABELS[role] || "Unknown role"}`;
+
+    row.append(identity, action);
+    host.append(row);
+  });
+  if (
+    search.query.length >= 2 &&
+    !search.loading &&
+    !search.error &&
+    !search.results.length
+  )
+    host.textContent = "No users found.";
+}

+const assignmentRenderBase = renderPageShellStatus;
+renderPageShellStatus = function renderPageShellStatusWithAssignments() {
+  assignmentRenderBase();
+  renderCurrentUserProfile();
+  renderMyAudits();
+  renderAssignmentEditor();
+};
