+// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
+// Assignment user profile cache, recent suggestions, and directory fallback
+// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
+state.assignmentProfileCache = state.assignmentProfileCache || new Map();
+state.assignmentRecentUsers = state.assignmentRecentUsers || [];
+state.assignmentUserSearch = state.assignmentUserSearch || {};
+ATLAS_ASSIGNMENT_ROLES.forEach((role) => {
+  state.assignmentUserSearch[role] = state.assignmentUserSearch[role] || {
+    query: "",
+    results: [],
+    loading: false,
+    error: "",
+    source: "recent",
+    controller: null,
+  };
+});
+
+function atlasUserProfileKey(user) {
+  return String(user?.id || user?.username || "")
+    .trim()
+    .toLowerCase();
+}
+function atlasCacheAssignmentProfiles(users) {
+  (users || []).forEach((user) => {
+    const key = atlasUserProfileKey(user);
+    if (key) state.assignmentProfileCache.set(key, user);
+  });
+}
+function atlasResolvedAssignmentUser(user) {
+  const byId = String(user?.id || "")
+    .trim()
+    .toLowerCase();
+  const byUsername = String(user?.username || "")
+    .trim()
+    .toLowerCase();
+  return (
+    state.assignmentProfileCache.get(byId) ||
+    state.assignmentProfileCache.get(byUsername) ||
+    user
+  );
+}
+function atlasMergeAssignmentUsers(...lists) {
+  const result = [];
+  lists.flat().forEach((user) => {
+    if (!user || result.some((item) => atlasUserMatches(item, user))) return;
+    result.push(user);
+  });
+  return result;
+}
+async function atlasLoadRecentAssignmentUsers(role, query = "") {
+  const search = state.assignmentUserSearch[role];
+  if (!search) return;
+  search.controller?.abort?.();
+  search.controller = new AbortController();
+  search.query = String(query || "").trim();
+  search.loading = true;
+  search.error = "";
+  search.source = "recent";
+  renderAssignmentSearchResults(role);
+  try {
+    const payload = await atlasSearchCachedUsers(search.query, {
+      size: 20,
+      signal: search.controller.signal,
+    });
+    const items = Array.isArray(payload?.items) ? payload.items : [];
+    atlasCacheAssignmentProfiles(items);
+    state.assignmentRecentUsers = items;
+    search.results = items;
+  } catch (error) {
+    if (error.name !== "AbortError") search.error = error.message;
+  } finally {
+    search.loading = false;
+    renderAssignmentSearchResults(role);
+  }
+}
+async function atlasSearchAllDirectoryUsers(role) {
+  const search = state.assignmentUserSearch[role];
+  if (!search || search.query.length < 2) {
+    if (search)
+      search.error =
+        "Enter at least 2 characters before searching all Disney users.";
+    renderAssignmentSearchResults(role);
+    return;
+  }
+  search.controller?.abort?.();
+  search.controller = new AbortController();
+  search.loading = true;
+  search.error = "";
+  search.source = "directory";
+  renderAssignmentSearchResults(role);
+  try {
+    const payload = await atlasSearchDirectoryProfiles(search.query, {
+      size: 20,
+      signal: search.controller.signal,
+    });
+    const items = Array.isArray(payload?.items) ? payload.items : [];
+    atlasCacheAssignmentProfiles(items);
+    search.results = atlasMergeAssignmentUsers(search.results, items);
+  } catch (error) {
+    if (error.name !== "AbortError") search.error = error.message;
+  } finally {
+    search.loading = false;
+    renderAssignmentSearchResults(role);
+  }
+}
+function atlasAssignedUsersForHydration() {
+  const assignments =
+    normalizeAuditAssignments?.(state.audit?.assignments) || {};
+  return atlasMergeAssignmentUsers(
+    assignments.assigned || [],
+    assignments.backup || [],
+    assignments.developer || [],
+  );
+}
+const atlasProfileHydrationInFlight = new Map();
+async function atlasHydrateAssignedUserProfiles() {
+  const auditId = state.audit?.id || "";
+  const users = atlasAssignedUsersForHydration().filter(
+    (user) => !state.assignmentProfileCache.has(atlasUserProfileKey(user)),
+  );
+  if (!users.length) return [];
+  const key = users.map(atlasUserProfileKey).filter(Boolean).sort().join("|");
+  if (atlasProfileHydrationInFlight.has(key))
+    return atlasProfileHydrationInFlight.get(key);
+  const request = atlasMeasure?.("atlas.profile.hydrate", async () => {
+    try {
+      const payload = await atlasResolveUserProfiles(users);
+      const items = Array.isArray(payload?.items) ? payload.items : [];
+      atlasCacheAssignmentProfiles(items);
+      if (state.audit?.id === auditId) {
+        renderAssignmentEditor();
+        if (atlasActivePage?.() === "my-audits") renderMyAudits();
+      }
+      return items;
+    } catch (_error) {
+      return [];
+    } finally {
+      atlasProfileHydrationInFlight.delete(key);
+    }
+  });
+  atlasProfileHydrationInFlight.set(key, request);
+  return request;
+}
+ATLAS_ASSIGNMENT_ROLES.forEach((role) => {
+  const suffix = role[0].toUpperCase() + role.slice(1);
+  const input = $(`assignment${suffix}Search`);
+  input?.addEventListener("focus", () =>
+    atlasLoadRecentAssignmentUsers(role, input.value),
+  );
+  input?.addEventListener("input", () =>
+    atlasLoadRecentAssignmentUsers(role, input.value),
+  );
+});
+document.addEventListener("click", (event) => {
+  const addAssignment = event.target.closest("[data-add-assignment-role]");
+  if (addAssignment) {
+    event.preventDefault();
+    const role = atlasAssignmentRole(addAssignment.dataset.addAssignmentRole);
+    const index = Number.parseInt(
+      addAssignment.dataset.assignmentResultIndex,
+      10,
+    );
+    const user =
+      role && Number.isInteger(index)
+        ? state.assignmentUserSearch?.[role]?.results?.[index]
+        : null;
+    if (!atlasAddAuditAssignment(role, user))
+      status?.("The selected user could not be assigned.", "warn");
+    return;
+  }
+  const removeAssignment = event.target.closest(
+    "[data-remove-assignment-role]",
+  );
+  if (removeAssignment) {
+    event.preventDefault();
+    const removed = atlasRemoveAuditAssignment(
+      removeAssignment.dataset.removeAssignmentRole,
+      {
+        id: removeAssignment.dataset.assignmentUserId || "",
+        username: removeAssignment.dataset.assignmentUsername || "",
+      },
+    );
+    if (!removed)
+      status?.("The selected assignment could not be removed.", "warn");
+    return;
+  }
+  const allUsers = event.target.closest("[data-search-all-assignment-users]");
+  if (allUsers) {
+    event.preventDefault();
+    atlasSearchAllDirectoryUsers(allUsers.dataset.searchAllAssignmentUsers);
+  }
+});
+document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", () => {
+  atlasHydrateAssignedUserProfiles();
+});
+
+// Source selection repaints Sources immediately and dirties Logic Builder.
+document.addEventListener("change", (event) => {
+  const control = event.target.closest(
+    "[data-source-select], [data-source-picker], #sourceSelect, #activeSourceSelect",
+  );
+  if (!control) return;
+  queueMicrotask(() => atlasInvalidateSourceMutation?.({ immediate: true }));
+});

+/** Durable database audit persistence. IndexedDB remains local crash recovery only. */
+async function atlasAuditApi(path, options = {}) {
+  const response = await fetch(path, {
+    cache: "no-store",
+    headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", ...(options.headers || {}) },
+    ...options,
+  });
+  let payload = null;
+  try {
+    payload = await response.json();
+  } catch (_error) {}
+  if (!response.ok) {
+    const error = new Error(
+      payload?.error || `Request failed with status ${response.status}.`,
+    );
+    error.status = response.status;
+    error.currentRevisionId = payload?.currentRevisionId;
+    throw error;
+  }
+  return payload;
+}
+function atlasApplyLoadedAudit(payload, source = "database", options = {}) {
+  state.audit = normalizeLoadedAudit(payload.audit, source);
+  state.selectedStepId = state.audit.steps?.[0]?.id || null;
+  state.activeAuditValue = `database:${state.audit.id}`;
+  state.auditPersistence = {
+    auditId: state.audit.id,
+    revisionId: payload.revision?.id || null,
+    revisionNumber: payload.revision?.number || 0,
+    access: payload.access || null,
+    saving: false,
+    conflict: null,
+  };
+  markAuditSelected();
+  atlasInvalidateSourceDerivedState?.();
+  atlasMarkAllPagesDirty?.();
+  const targetPage = options.targetPage || atlasActivePage?.() || "overview";
+  showSection(targetPage);
+  updateActiveAuditBaseline();
+  renderActiveAuditSelect?.();
+  atlasResetDatabaseWriteState?.(state.audit.id, { acknowledged: true });
+}
+async function atlasLoadCurrentUser(options = {}) {
+  const { render = true } = options;
+  state.currentUserStatus = "loading";
+  state.currentUserError = "";
+  if (render) {
+    renderCurrentUserProfile?.();
+    renderMyAudits?.();
+  }
+  try {
+    state.currentUser = await atlasAuditApi("/api/me");
+    state.currentUserStatus = "ready";
+    await atlasAuditApi("/api/user-profiles/me", {
+      method: "POST",
+      body: "{}",
+    });
+  } catch (error) {
+    state.currentUser = null;
+    state.currentUserStatus = "error";
+    state.currentUserError = error.message;
+  }
+  if (render) {
+    renderCurrentUserProfile?.();
+    renderMyAudits?.();
+  }
+  return state.currentUser;
+}
+async function atlasSearchUsers(query, options = {}) {
+  const q = String(query || "").trim();
+  if (q.length < 2) return { items: [] };
+  return atlasAuditApi(
+    `/api/users?q=${encodeURIComponent(q)}&page=${options.page || 1}&size=${Math.min(25, options.size || 10)}`,
+    { signal: options.signal },
+  );
+}
+function atlasSetAuditLibraryLoading(loading) {
+  const spinner = document.querySelector("[data-audit-loading-spinner]");
+  if (spinner) spinner.hidden = !loading;
+}
+function atlasSetAuditLibraryStatus(message) {
+  const text = document.querySelector("[data-audit-library-status-text]");
+  if (text) text.textContent = message;
+}
+async function atlasRefreshDatabaseAudits(options = {}) {
+  const { render = true } = options;
+  state.databaseAuditsStatus = "loading";
+  state.databaseAuditsError = "";
+  if (render) renderMyAudits?.();
+  atlasSetAuditLibraryLoading(true);
+  atlasSetAuditLibraryStatus("Database audit library has not loaded yet.");
+  try {
+    const payload = await atlasAuditApi("/api/audits");
+    state.databaseAudits = Array.isArray(payload.audits) ? payload.audits : [];
+    state.databaseAuditsStatus = "ready";
+    if (render) renderMyAudits?.();
+    atlasSetAuditLibraryStatus(
+      `Loaded ${state.databaseAudits.length} database audit(s).`,
+    );
+    return state.databaseAudits;
+  } catch (error) {
+    state.databaseAuditsStatus = "error";
+    state.databaseAuditsError = error.message;
+    if (render) renderMyAudits?.();
+    atlasSetAuditLibraryStatus(
+      "The database audit library could not be loaded.",
+    );
+    throw error;
+  } finally {
+    atlasSetAuditLibraryLoading(false);
+  }
+}
+let atlasAuditLoadSequence = 0;
+async function atlasLoadDatabaseAudit(id) {
+  const sequence = ++atlasAuditLoadSequence;
+  return atlasMeasure?.("atlas.audit.load", async () => {
+    const payload = await atlasAuditApi(
+      `/api/audits/${encodeURIComponent(id)}`,
+    );
+    if (sequence !== atlasAuditLoadSequence) return null;
+    atlasApplyLoadedAudit(payload, "database", { targetPage: "overview" });
+    scheduleAutosave({ database: false });
+    return payload;
+  });
+}
+const ATLAS_DATABASE_WRITE_DELAY_MS = 2500;
+const ATLAS_DATABASE_MAX_DIRTY_MS = 20000;
+const atlasDatabaseWriteCoordinator = {
+  auditId: "",
+  editGeneration: 0,
+  acknowledgedGeneration: 0,
+  dirtySince: 0,
+  trailingTimer: null,
+  maximumTimer: null,
+  inFlight: null,
+  conflict: false,
+  error: null,
+  pendingReason: "",
+};
+function atlasClearDatabaseWriteTimers() {
+  clearTimeout(atlasDatabaseWriteCoordinator.trailingTimer);
+  clearTimeout(atlasDatabaseWriteCoordinator.maximumTimer);
+  atlasDatabaseWriteCoordinator.trailingTimer = null;
+  atlasDatabaseWriteCoordinator.maximumTimer = null;
+}
+function atlasResetDatabaseWriteState(
+  auditId = state.audit?.id || "",
+  options = {},
+) {
+  atlasClearDatabaseWriteTimers();
+  atlasDatabaseWriteCoordinator.auditId = auditId;
+  atlasDatabaseWriteCoordinator.editGeneration = 0;
+  atlasDatabaseWriteCoordinator.acknowledgedGeneration = options.acknowledged
+    ? 0
+    : -1;
+  atlasDatabaseWriteCoordinator.dirtySince = 0;
+  atlasDatabaseWriteCoordinator.inFlight = null;
+  atlasDatabaseWriteCoordinator.conflict = false;
+  atlasDatabaseWriteCoordinator.error = null;
+  atlasDatabaseWriteCoordinator.pendingReason = "";
+}
+function atlasDatabaseWriteMatchesActiveAudit() {
+  return (
+    !!state.audit?.id &&
+    atlasDatabaseWriteCoordinator.auditId === state.audit.id
+  );
+}
+function atlasHasPendingDatabaseWrite() {
+  return (
+    atlasDatabaseWriteMatchesActiveAudit() &&
+    (atlasDatabaseWriteCoordinator.inFlight ||
+      atlasDatabaseWriteCoordinator.editGeneration >
+        atlasDatabaseWriteCoordinator.acknowledgedGeneration)
+  );
+}
+function atlasSetDatabaseSaveStatus(message) {
+  if (e.saveStatus) e.saveStatus.textContent = message;
+}
+function atlasPatchDatabaseAuditListEntry(payload, snapshot) {
+  const audit = payload?.audit || snapshot;
+  if (!audit?.id) return;
+  const current = (state.databaseAudits || []).find(
+    (item) => item.id === audit.id,
+  );
+  const entry = {
+    ...(current || {}),
+    id: audit.id,
+    name: audit.name,
+    purpose: audit.purpose || "",
+    assignments: audit.assignments || current?.assignments,
+    currentRevisionId:
+      payload?.revision?.id || current?.currentRevisionId || null,
+    currentRevisionNumber:
+      payload?.revision?.number || current?.currentRevisionNumber || 0,
+    updatedAt:
+      payload?.revision?.createdAt ||
+      current?.updatedAt ||
+      new Date().toISOString(),
+    updatedByUsername:
+      payload?.revision?.createdByUsername || current?.updatedByUsername || "",
+  };
+  if (current) Object.assign(current, entry);
+  else state.databaseAudits.push(entry);
+  renderMyAudits?.();
+  renderActiveAuditSelect?.();
+  updateDashboardMetrics?.();
+}
+function atlasApplyAuditSaveMetadata(payload, snapshot, sentGeneration) {
+  if (!payload?.revision?.id)
+    throw new Error(
+      "The database save response did not include revision metadata.",
+    );
+  atlasDatabaseWriteCoordinator.acknowledgedGeneration = Math.max(
+    atlasDatabaseWriteCoordinator.acknowledgedGeneration,
+    sentGeneration,
+  );
+  atlasDatabaseWriteCoordinator.error = null;
+  atlasDatabaseWriteCoordinator.conflict = false;
+  atlasPatchDatabaseAuditListEntry(payload, snapshot);
+  if (state.audit?.id !== snapshot.id) return;
+  state.auditPersistence.auditId = snapshot.id;
+  state.auditPersistence.revisionId = payload.revision.id;
+  state.auditPersistence.revisionNumber = payload.revision.number || 0;
+  state.auditPersistence.access =
+    payload.access || state.auditPersistence.access || null;
+  state.auditPersistence.conflict = null;
+  state.activeAuditValue = `database:${snapshot.id}`;
+  state.activeAuditBaseline = JSON.stringify(snapshot);
+  renderActiveAuditSelect?.();
+  updateDashboardMetrics?.();
+}
+function atlasMarkDatabaseDirty(reason = "edit") {
+  if (!hasSelectedAudit?.() || !state.audit?.id || state.autosavePaused) return;
+  if (!atlasDatabaseWriteMatchesActiveAudit())
+    atlasResetDatabaseWriteState(state.audit.id);
+  atlasDatabaseWriteCoordinator.editGeneration += 1;
+  atlasDatabaseWriteCoordinator.pendingReason = reason;
+  atlasDatabaseWriteCoordinator.error = null;
+  if (!atlasDatabaseWriteCoordinator.dirtySince)
+    atlasDatabaseWriteCoordinator.dirtySince = Date.now();
+  const needsCreate =
+    !state.auditPersistence?.revisionId ||
+    state.auditPersistence.auditId !== state.audit.id;
+  if (needsCreate) {
+    queueMicrotask(() =>
+      atlasFlushDatabaseWrite("Created audit").catch((error) =>
+        status?.(`Database create failed: ${error.message}`, "bad"),
+      ),
+    );
+    return;
+  }
+  atlasScheduleDatabaseWrite(reason);
+}
+function atlasScheduleDatabaseWrite(reason = "edit") {
+  if (
+    !atlasDatabaseWriteMatchesActiveAudit() ||
+    atlasDatabaseWriteCoordinator.conflict
+  )
+    return;
+  clearTimeout(atlasDatabaseWriteCoordinator.trailingTimer);
+  atlasDatabaseWriteCoordinator.trailingTimer = setTimeout(
+    () =>
+      atlasFlushDatabaseWrite(`Autosaved after ${reason}`).catch((error) =>
+        status?.(`Database save failed: ${error.message}`, "bad"),
+      ),
+    ATLAS_DATABASE_WRITE_DELAY_MS,
+  );
+  if (!atlasDatabaseWriteCoordinator.maximumTimer) {
+    atlasDatabaseWriteCoordinator.maximumTimer = setTimeout(
+      () =>
+        atlasFlushDatabaseWrite("Maximum dirty interval").catch((error) =>
+          status?.(`Database save failed: ${error.message}`, "bad"),
+        ),
+      ATLAS_DATABASE_MAX_DIRTY_MS,
+    );
+  }
+  atlasSetDatabaseSaveStatus("Changes pending database save.");
+}
+async function atlasPerformDatabaseWrite(changeSummary) {
+  capture?.();
+  const auditId = state.audit.id;
+  const snapshot = structuredClone(state.audit);
+  const sentGeneration = atlasDatabaseWriteCoordinator.editGeneration;
+  const exists =
+    state.auditPersistence.auditId === auditId &&
+    !!state.auditPersistence.revisionId;
+  const expectedRevisionId = exists ? state.auditPersistence.revisionId : null;
+  state.auditPersistence.saving = true;
+  atlasSetDatabaseSaveStatus(
+    exists ? "Saving to database…" : "Creating database audit…",
+  );
+  try {
+    const payload = await atlasAuditApi(
+      exists ? `/api/audits/${encodeURIComponent(auditId)}` : "/api/audits",
+      {
+        method: exists ? "PUT" : "POST",
+        body: JSON.stringify({
+          audit: snapshot,
+          expectedRevisionId,
+          changeSummary: changeSummary || null,
+        }),
+      },
+    );
+    atlasApplyAuditSaveMetadata(payload, snapshot, sentGeneration);
+    atlasSetDatabaseSaveStatus(
+      `Saved to database as revision ${payload.revision.number}.`,
+    );
+    status?.(
+      `Saved ${snapshot.name} as revision ${payload.revision.number}.`,
+      "ok",
+    );
+    return true;
+  } catch (error) {
+    atlasDatabaseWriteCoordinator.error = error;
+    if (error.status === 409) {
+      atlasDatabaseWriteCoordinator.conflict = true;
+      state.auditPersistence.conflict = {
+        currentRevisionId: error.currentRevisionId,
+      };
+      atlasSetDatabaseSaveStatus(
+        "Database save conflict. Review the conflict before retrying.",
+      );
+      atlasOpenConflictDialog?.(error);
+      return false;
+    }
+    atlasSetDatabaseSaveStatus(`Database save failed: ${error.message}`);
+    throw error;
+  } finally {
+    if (state.audit?.id === auditId) state.auditPersistence.saving = false;
+  }
+}
+async function atlasFlushDatabaseWrite(reason = "explicit save") {
+  if (!hasSelectedAudit?.() || !state.audit?.id) return true;
+  if (!atlasDatabaseWriteMatchesActiveAudit())
+    atlasResetDatabaseWriteState(state.audit.id);
+  atlasClearDatabaseWriteTimers();
+  if (atlasDatabaseWriteCoordinator.conflict) return false;
+  if (atlasDatabaseWriteCoordinator.inFlight) {
+    await atlasDatabaseWriteCoordinator.inFlight;
+    if (atlasDatabaseWriteCoordinator.conflict) return false;
+    if (
+      atlasDatabaseWriteCoordinator.editGeneration <=
+      atlasDatabaseWriteCoordinator.acknowledgedGeneration
+    )
+      return true;
+  }
+  const needsCreate =
+    !state.auditPersistence?.revisionId ||
+    state.auditPersistence.auditId !== state.audit.id;
+  if (
+    !needsCreate &&
+    atlasDatabaseWriteCoordinator.editGeneration <=
+      atlasDatabaseWriteCoordinator.acknowledgedGeneration &&
+    !auditHasUnsavedChanges()
+  )
+    return true;
+  const write = atlasPerformDatabaseWrite(reason);
+  atlasDatabaseWriteCoordinator.inFlight = write;
+  let result;
+  try {
+    result = await write;
+  } finally {
+    if (atlasDatabaseWriteCoordinator.inFlight === write)
+      atlasDatabaseWriteCoordinator.inFlight = null;
+  }
+  if (!result) return false;
+  if (
+    atlasDatabaseWriteCoordinator.editGeneration >
+    atlasDatabaseWriteCoordinator.acknowledgedGeneration
+  ) {
+    return atlasFlushDatabaseWrite("Saved edits made during database write");
+  }
+  atlasDatabaseWriteCoordinator.dirtySince = 0;
+  return true;
+}
+async function atlasCreateCurrentAudit(reason = "Created audit") {
+  if (!state.audit?.id) return false;
+  atlasResetDatabaseWriteState(state.audit.id);
+  atlasDatabaseWriteCoordinator.editGeneration = 1;
+  atlasDatabaseWriteCoordinator.dirtySince = Date.now();
+  return atlasFlushDatabaseWrite(reason);
+}
+async function atlasSaveCurrentAudit(changeSummary = "") {
+  return atlasFlushDatabaseWrite(changeSummary || "Explicit save");
+}
+function atlasIsDatabaseAuditDirty() {
+  if (!hasSelectedAudit?.() || !state.audit?.id) return false;
+  const needsCreate =
+    !state.auditPersistence?.revisionId ||
+    state.auditPersistence.auditId !== state.audit.id;
+  return (
+    needsCreate || atlasHasPendingDatabaseWrite() || auditHasUnsavedChanges()
+  );
+}
+async function atlasSaveIfDirty(reason = "navigation") {
+  if (!atlasIsDatabaseAuditDirty()) return true;
+  return atlasFlushDatabaseWrite(`Saved on ${reason}`);
+}
+async function atlasNavigateToSection(section) {
+  const target = pageForSection(section);
+  const current = document.querySelector(".page.active")?.dataset.page || "";
+  if (target === current) return;
+  showSection(target);
+  if (atlasIsDatabaseAuditDirty())
+    atlasScheduleDatabaseWrite("page navigation");
+}
+function atlasSaveOnPageHide() {
+  if (!atlasIsDatabaseAuditDirty() || !state.auditPersistence?.revisionId)
+    return;
+  capture?.();
+  const auditId = state.auditPersistence.auditId;
+  const body = JSON.stringify({
+    audit: structuredClone(state.audit),
+    expectedRevisionId: state.auditPersistence.revisionId,
+    changeSummary: "Saved on window close",
+  });
+  fetch(`/api/audits/${encodeURIComponent(auditId)}`, {
+    method: "PUT",
+    credentials: "same-origin",
+    keepalive: true,
+    headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
+    body,
+  }).catch(() => {});
+}
+
+function atlasAuditDocumentsFromJson(json) {
+  const candidates = Array.isArray(json?.flows)
+    ? json.flows
+    : Array.isArray(json?.audits)
+      ? json.audits
+      : Array.isArray(json)
+        ? json
+        : [json];
+  return candidates
+    .map((item) => item?.audit || item)
+    .filter((item) => item && typeof item === "object" && !Array.isArray(item));
+}
+async function atlasImportAuditDocuments(
+  documents,
+  summary = "Imported JSON audit",
+) {
+  const flushed = await atlasSaveIfDirty("audit import");
+  if (!flushed)
+    throw new Error("Resolve the current audit save before importing.");
+  const normalized = documents.map((document) =>
+    normalizeLoadedAudit(document, "database-import"),
+  );
+  const payload = await atlasAuditApi("/api/audits/import", {
+    method: "POST",
+    body: JSON.stringify({ audits: normalized, changeSummary: summary }),
+  });
+  await atlasRefreshDatabaseAudits();
+  await refreshSaved?.();
+  renderAuditLibrarySelect?.();
+  renderActiveAuditSelect?.();
+  updateDashboardMetrics?.();
+  return payload;
+}
+async function atlasImportAuditDocument(
+  document,
+  summary = "Imported JSON audit",
+) {
+  return atlasImportAuditDocuments([document], summary);
+}
+async function atlasImportAuditJsonFile(file) {
+  const json = JSON.parse(await file.text());
+  const documents = atlasAuditDocumentsFromJson(json);
+  if (!documents.length)
+    throw new Error(
+      "The selected JSON file does not contain an audit or saved audit bundle.",
+    );
+  const result = await atlasImportAuditDocuments(
+    documents,
+    `Imported from ${file.name}`,
+  );
+  const summary = $("auditImportSummary");
+  const imported = result.imported?.length || 0;
+  const skipped = result.skipped?.length || 0;
+  const failed = result.failed?.length || 0;
+  if (summary)
+    summary.textContent = `Import complete: ${imported} imported, ${skipped} unchanged, ${failed} failed.`;
+  if (failed)
+    status(
+      `Imported ${imported} audit(s); ${failed} failed. Review the import summary.`,
+      "warn",
+    );
+  else status(`Imported ${imported} audit(s) into the database.`, "ok");
+  return result;
+}
+async function atlasArchiveCurrentAudit() {
+  const flushed = await atlasSaveIfDirty("archive");
+  if (!flushed) return;
+  if (!state.auditPersistence.revisionId)
+    throw new Error("Save this audit before archiving it.");
+  if (
+    !confirm(
+      `Archive ${state.audit.name}? Version history will remain in the database.`,
+    )
+  )
+    return;
+  await atlasAuditApi(`/api/audits/${encodeURIComponent(state.audit.id)}`, {
+    method: "DELETE",
+    body: JSON.stringify({
+      expectedRevisionId: state.auditPersistence.revisionId,
+    }),
+  });
+  await atlasRefreshDatabaseAudits();
+  state.audit = defaultAudit();
+  state.auditPersistence = {
+    auditId: null,
+    revisionId: null,
+    revisionNumber: 0,
+    access: null,
+    saving: false,
+    conflict: null,
+  };
+  clearAuditSelection();
+  atlasResetDatabaseWriteState("");
+  renderAll();
+}
+async function atlasInitAuditDatabase(options = {}) {
+  await atlasRefreshDatabaseAudits(options);
+}
+
+// Minimal profile-cache API. Avatar refreshes remain presentation state and do not save audits.
+async function atlasSearchCachedUsers(query = "", options = {}) {
+  const q = String(query || "").trim();
+  return atlasAuditApi(
+    `/api/user-profiles?q=${encodeURIComponent(q)}&size=${Math.min(25, options.size || 20)}`,
+    { signal: options.signal },
+  );
+}
+async function atlasSearchDirectoryProfiles(query, options = {}) {
+  const q = String(query || "").trim();
+  if (q.length < 2) return { items: [] };
+  return atlasAuditApi(
+    `/api/user-profiles/directory?q=${encodeURIComponent(q)}&page=${options.page || 1}&size=${Math.min(25, options.size || 10)}`,
+    { signal: options.signal },
+  );
+}
+async function atlasResolveUserProfiles(users, options = {}) {
+  const identities = (users || [])
+    .map((user) => ({
+      id: String(user?.id || "").trim(),
+      username: String(user?.username || "").trim(),
+    }))
+    .filter((user) => user.id || user.username);
+  return atlasAuditApi("/api/user-profiles/resolve", {
+    method: "POST",
+    body: JSON.stringify({ users: identities }),
+    signal: options.signal,
+  });
+}
+const atlasProfileHydrationApplyBase = atlasApplyLoadedAudit;
+atlasApplyLoadedAudit = function atlasApplyLoadedAuditWithProfileHydration(
+  payload,
+  source = "database",
+  options = {},
+) {
+  const result = atlasProfileHydrationApplyBase(payload, source, options);
+  queueMicrotask(() => atlasHydrateAssignedUserProfiles?.());
+  return result;
+};
+
+async function atlasRefreshDeletedAudits() {
+  if (!state.currentUser?.access?.canManageDeletedAudits) return [];
+  state.deletedAuditsStatus = "loading";
+  renderMyAudits();
+  try {
+    const p = await atlasAuditApi("/api/admin/audits/deleted");
+    state.deletedAudits = Array.isArray(p.audits) ? p.audits : [];
+    state.deletedAuditsStatus = "ready";
+    state.deletedAuditsError = "";
+    renderMyAudits();
+    return state.deletedAudits;
+  } catch (e) {
+    state.deletedAuditsStatus = "error";
+    state.deletedAuditsError = e.message;
+    renderMyAudits();
+    throw e;
+  }
+}
+async function atlasRestoreDeletedAudit(id) {
+  const a = (state.deletedAudits || []).find((x) => x.id === id);
+  if (!a || !confirm(`Restore "${a.name}" to the active Audit Library?`))
+    return;
+  await atlasAuditApi(`/api/admin/audits/${encodeURIComponent(id)}/restore`, {
+    method: "POST",
+  });
+  await Promise.all([
+    atlasRefreshDeletedAudits(),
+    atlasRefreshDatabaseAudits(),
+  ]);
+  status("Audit restored.", "ok");
+}
+async function atlasPermanentlyDeleteAudit(id) {
+  const a = (state.deletedAudits || []).find((x) => x.id === id);
+  if (!a || !confirm(`Permanently delete "${a.name}"? This cannot be undone.`))
+    return;
+  await atlasAuditApi(`/api/admin/audits/${encodeURIComponent(id)}`, {
+    method: "DELETE",
+  });
+  await atlasRefreshDeletedAudits();
+  status("Audit permanently deleted.", "ok");
+}

+BEGIN FILE: js/21-display-fields.js

+/** Display Fields page. */
+const atlasOriginalRequirementsFieldRows = requirementsFieldRows;
+function atlasDisplayPart(value) {
+  return String(value || "")
+    .trim()
+    .toLowerCase();
+}
+function atlasDisplayKey(value) {
+  return `${atlasDisplayPart(value?.sourceKey || value?.sourceFile || value?.uniqueId || "calculated field")}::${atlasDisplayPart(value?.fieldName || value?.fieldTitle || value?.name)}`;
+}
+function atlasDisplayPreferences() {
+  if (!Array.isArray(state.audit.displayFields)) state.audit.displayFields = [];
+  return state.audit.displayFields;
+}
+function atlasDisplayLogicKeys() {
+  return new Set(
+    requirementsLogicFieldReferences().map((item) =>
+      atlasDisplayKey({
+        sourceFile:
+          requirementsSourceIdentity(item.sourceFile).sourceKey ||
+          item.sourceFile,
+        fieldName: item.fieldName,
+      }),
+    ),
+  );
+}
+function atlasDisplayCatalogRow(preference) {
+  const identity = requirementsSourceIdentity(
+    preference.sourceFile || preference.sourceKey,
+  );
+  const field = (
+    fieldsForSource?.(identity.sourceFile || preference.sourceFile) || []
+  ).find((item) =>
+    [item.rawFieldName, item.fieldName, item.name, item.label].some(
+      (value) =>
+        atlasDisplayPart(value) === atlasDisplayPart(preference.fieldName),
+    ),
+  );
+  return {
+    uniqueId:
+      identity.sourceKey || preference.sourceKey || preference.sourceFile || "",
+    sourceFile: identity.sourceFile || preference.sourceFile || "",
+    fieldTitle:
+      field?.rawFieldName ||
+      field?.fieldName ||
+      field?.name ||
+      preference.fieldName ||
+      "",
+    vanityLabel:
+      field?.auditSpecificVanityLabel ||
+      field?.vanityLabel ||
+      field?.globalVanityLabel ||
+      field?.label ||
+      preference.fieldName ||
+      "",
+    displayFormat:
+      field?.displayFormat || field?.formatting || field?.format || "",
+    dataCategory: field?.dataCategory || field?.category || "",
+    notes: "Display-only field",
+  };
+}
+function atlasDisplayRows(options = {}) {
+  const base = atlasOriginalRequirementsFieldRows(options),
+    prefs = atlasDisplayPreferences(),
+    logic = atlasDisplayLogicKeys(),
+    rows = new Map();
+  base.forEach((row, index) => {
+    const key = atlasDisplayKey(row),
+      saved = prefs.find((item) => atlasDisplayKey(item) === key);
+    rows.set(key, {
+      ...row,
+      sourceFile: row.uniqueId || "",
+      key,
+      usedInLogic: true,
+      displayed: saved ? saved.displayed !== false : true,
+      order: saved?.order ?? index,
+    });
+  });
+  prefs.forEach((pref, index) => {
+    const key = atlasDisplayKey(pref);
+    if (!rows.has(key)) {
+      const row = atlasDisplayCatalogRow(pref);
+      rows.set(key, {
+        ...row,
+        key,
+        usedInLogic: logic.has(key),
+        displayed: pref.displayed !== false,
+        order: pref.order ?? base.length + index,
+      });
+    }
+  });
+  return [...rows.values()]
+    .sort(
+      (a, b) => a.order - b.order || a.fieldTitle.localeCompare(b.fieldTitle),
+    )
+    .map((row, index) => ({
+      ...row,
+      rowNumber: index + 1,
+      display: row.displayed ? "Yes" : "No",
+    }));
+}
+requirementsFieldRows = function requirementsFieldRowsWithDisplaySelection(
+  options = {},
+) {
+  const rows = atlasDisplayRows(options);
+  return options.includeHidden ? rows : rows.filter((row) => row.displayed);
+};
+function atlasSaveDisplayField(row, displayed) {
+  const prefs = atlasDisplayPreferences(),
+    key = atlasDisplayKey(row),
+    existing = prefs.find((item) => atlasDisplayKey(item) === key),
+    value = {
+      sourceKey: row.uniqueId || row.sourceFile || "",
+      sourceFile: row.sourceFile || row.uniqueId || "",
+      fieldName: row.fieldTitle,
+      displayed: !!displayed,
+      order: row.order ?? prefs.length,
+    };
+  existing ? Object.assign(existing, value) : prefs.push(value);
+}
+function renderDisplayFieldsPage() {
+  const host = $("displayFieldsTable");
+  if (!host) return;
+  const rows = requirementsFieldRows({
+    includeHidden: true,
+    includeNotes: false,
+  });
+  host.innerHTML = rows.length
+    ? `<div class="display-fields-table-wrap"><table class="display-fields-table"><thead><tr><th>Source</th><th>Field Title</th><th>Vanity Label</th><th>Display Format</th><th>Data Category</th><th>Used in Logic</th><th>Displayed</th></tr></thead><tbody>${rows.map((row) => `<tr><td>${esc(row.uniqueId)}</td><td>${esc(row.fieldTitle)}</td><td>${esc(row.vanityLabel)}</td><td>${esc(row.displayFormat)}</td><td>${esc(row.dataCategory)}</td><td>${row.usedInLogic ? "Yes" : "No"}</td><td><label class="display-fields-switch"><input type="checkbox" role="switch" data-display-field-toggle="${esc(row.key)}" aria-label="Display ${esc(row.vanityLabel || row.fieldTitle)}" ${row.displayed ? "checked" : ""}><span class="display-fields-switch-track" aria-hidden="true"></span><span>${row.displayed ? "Displayed" : "Hidden"}</span></label></td></tr>`).join("")}</tbody></table></div>`
+    : `<div class="empty-state"><strong>No fields are available.</strong><p>Add logic fields or select a display-only field from an included source.</p></div>`;
+  const enabled = (state.audit.sources || []).length > 0,
+    button = $("addDisplayFieldBtn");
+  if (button) {
+    button.disabled = !enabled;
+    button.title = enabled ? "" : "Add a source to this audit first.";
+  }
+}
+function atlasDisplayPickerSources() {
+  return (state.audit.sources || []).map((source) =>
+    requirementsSourceIdentity(source),
+  );
+}
+function atlasDisplayPickerEntries() {
+  const selectedKeys = new Set(
+    atlasDisplayRows({ includeHidden: true }).map((row) => row.key),
+  );
+  return atlasDisplayPickerSources().flatMap((identity) => {
+    const sourceFile = identity.sourceFile || identity.sourceKey || "";
+    const selectedSource = (state.audit.sources || []).find((source) =>
+      [source.name, source.sourceFile, source.sourceKey, source.alias]
+        .filter(Boolean)
+        .includes(sourceFile),
+    );
+    return (fieldsForSource?.(sourceFile) || []).map((field) => {
+      const fieldName =
+        field.rawFieldName ||
+        field.fieldName ||
+        field.name ||
+        field.label ||
+        "";
+      const key = atlasDisplayKey({
+        sourceKey: identity.sourceKey,
+        sourceFile,
+        fieldName,
+      });
+      return {
+        key,
+        label:
+          field.auditSpecificVanityLabel ||
+          field.vanityLabel ||
+          field.globalVanityLabel ||
+          field.label ||
+          fieldName,
+        rawName: fieldName,
+        sourceKey: identity.sourceKey || sourceFile,
+        sourceLabel:
+          identity.alias || sourceFile || identity.sourceKey || "Other",
+        sourceColor:
+          selectedSource?.color ||
+          atlasSelectedSourceColor?.(sourceFile) ||
+          "#7da2d8",
+        custom: Boolean(field.isCreatedField),
+        badge: field.isCreatedField ? "Custom" : "",
+        disabled: selectedKeys.has(key),
+        pickerSource: sourceFile,
+        pickerField: fieldName,
+      };
+    });
+  });
+}
+function atlasOpenDisplayPicker() {
+  if (!window.ATLASVisualPicker?.open) {
+    console.error("ATLAS Display Fields: shared Visual Picker is unavailable.");
+    status?.(
+      "The field picker is unavailable. Reload the page and try again.",
+      "error",
+    );
+    return false;
+  }
+  return window.ATLASVisualPicker.open({
+    mode: "field",
+    title: "Visual Field Picker",
+    guidance: "Select a field from a source included in this audit.",
+    searchPlaceholder: "Search selected sources and fields...",
+    entries: atlasDisplayPickerEntries(),
+    sort: "source",
+    closeOnSelect: false,
+    onSelect(entry) {
+      if (!entry) return;
+      atlasAddDisplayField(entry.pickerSource, entry.pickerField);
+    },
+  });
+}
+function atlasAddDisplayField(sourceFile, fieldName) {
+  const identity = atlasDisplayPickerSources().find((source) =>
+    [source.sourceFile, source.sourceKey, source.alias]
+      .filter(Boolean)
+      .includes(sourceFile),
+  );
+  const field = identity
+    ? (fieldsForSource?.(identity.sourceFile || sourceFile) || []).find(
+        (item) =>
+          [item.rawFieldName, item.fieldName, item.name, item.label].some(
+            (value) => atlasDisplayPart(value) === atlasDisplayPart(fieldName),
+          ),
+      )
+    : null;
+  const resolvedFieldName =
+    field?.rawFieldName ||
+    field?.fieldName ||
+    field?.name ||
+    field?.label ||
+    "";
+  if (!identity || !field || !resolvedFieldName) {
+    console.error(
+      "ATLAS Display Fields: rejected a stale or invalid picker entry.",
+      {
+        sourceAvailable: Boolean(identity),
+        fieldAvailable: Boolean(field),
+      },
+    );
+    status?.(
+      "That field is no longer available. Reopen the picker and try again.",
+      "error",
+    );
+    return false;
+  }
+  const key = atlasDisplayKey({
+    sourceKey: identity.sourceKey,
+    sourceFile: identity.sourceFile || sourceFile,
+    fieldName: resolvedFieldName,
+  });
+  if (
+    atlasDisplayRows({ includeHidden: true }).some((row) => row.key === key)
+  ) {
+    status?.("That field is already included in Display Fields.", "error");
+    return false;
+  }
+  const row = {
+    uniqueId: identity.sourceKey || sourceFile,
+    sourceFile: identity.sourceFile || sourceFile,
+    fieldTitle: resolvedFieldName,
+    order: atlasDisplayPreferences().length,
+  };
+  atlasSaveDisplayField(row, true);
+  scheduleAutosave?.();
+  atlasInvalidatePages?.(["display-fields", "preview", "save"]);
+  renderDisplayFieldsPage();
+  renderRequirementsFieldsPreview?.();
+  window.ATLASVisualPicker.close("select");
+  status?.(`Added ${resolvedFieldName} to displayed fields.`, "ok");
+  return true;
+}
+document.addEventListener("click", (event) => {
+  if (event.target.closest("#addDisplayFieldBtn")) atlasOpenDisplayPicker();
+});
+document.addEventListener("change", (event) => {
+  if (event.target.matches("[data-display-field-toggle]")) {
+    const row = requirementsFieldRows({
+      includeHidden: true,
+      includeNotes: false,
+    }).find((item) => item.key === event.target.dataset.displayFieldToggle);
+    if (!row) return;
+    atlasSaveDisplayField(row, event.target.checked);
+    scheduleAutosave?.();
+    atlasInvalidatePages?.(["preview", "save"]);
+    renderDisplayFieldsPage();
+    renderRequirementsFieldsPreview?.();
+  }
+});
+const atlasOriginalRenderPage = atlasRenderPage;
+atlasRenderPage = function atlasRenderPageWithDisplayFields(
+  page,
+  options = {},
+) {
+  const target = pageForSection?.(page) || page;
+  if (target === "display-fields") {
+    renderDisplayFieldsPage();
+    atlasPageRenderState.dirty.delete(target);
+    atlasPageRenderState.initialized.add(target);
+    return;
+  }
+  return atlasOriginalRenderPage(page, options);
+};
+const atlasStepsFlowIndex = AUDIT_FLOW_PAGES.findIndex(
+  (item) => item.page === "steps",
+);
+if (
+  atlasStepsFlowIndex >= 0 &&
+  !AUDIT_FLOW_PAGES.some((item) => item.page === "display-fields")
+) {
+  AUDIT_FLOW_PAGES[atlasStepsFlowIndex].nextLabel = "Display Fields";
+  AUDIT_FLOW_PAGES.splice(atlasStepsFlowIndex + 1, 0, {
+    page: "display-fields",
+    label: "Display Fields",
+    nextLabel: "Requirements Preview",
+  });
+}
+

+================================================================================

+================================================================================
+
+================================================================================
+BEGIN FILE: js/22-audit-issue-engine.js
+================================================================================
+
+/**
+ * Portable, persistence-free Audit Issue engine.
+ *
+ * The engine evaluates declarative rules against caller-owned context. It has
+ * no ATLAS state, DOM, navigation, persistence, API, database, or rendering
+ * dependency. Applications provide named evaluator/action registries.
+ *
+ * Classic-script usage:
+ *   const engine = AuditIssueEngine.create({ evaluators: { requiredValue } });
+ *   const result = engine.evaluate(context, rules);
+ */
+(function initializeAuditIssueEngine(global) {
+  "use strict";
+
+  const SEVERITIES = Object.freeze(["ok", "warning", "critical"]);
+  const SEVERITY_RANK = Object.freeze({ ok: 0, warning: 1, critical: 2 });
+  const SYSTEM_CATEGORY = "system";
+
+  function isPlainObject(value) {
+    if (!value || typeof value !== "object" || Array.isArray(value))
+      return false;
+    const prototype = Object.getPrototypeOf(value);
+    return (
+      prototype === null ||
+      Object.prototype.toString.call(value) === "[object Object]"
+    );
+  }
+
+  function text(value, fallback = "") {
+    return value == null ? fallback : String(value);
+  }
+
+  function nonBlank(value, fallback = "") {
+    const normalized = text(value).trim();
+    return normalized || text(fallback).trim();
+  }
+
+  function clone(value, seen = new WeakMap()) {
+    if (value == null || typeof value !== "object") return value;
+    if (seen.has(value)) return seen.get(value);
+    if (Array.isArray(value)) {
+      const result = [];
+      seen.set(value, result);
+      value.forEach((item) => result.push(clone(item, seen)));
+      return result;
+    }
+    const result = {};
+    seen.set(value, result);
+    Object.entries(value).forEach(([key, item]) => {
+      result[key] = clone(item, seen);
+    });
+    return result;
+  }
+
+  function deepFreeze(value, seen = new WeakSet()) {
+    if (!value || typeof value !== "object" || seen.has(value)) return value;
+    seen.add(value);
+    Object.values(value).forEach((item) => deepFreeze(item, seen));
+    return Object.freeze(value);
+  }
+
+  function registry(value, label) {
+    if (value == null) return Object.freeze({});
+    if (!isPlainObject(value))
+      throw new TypeError(`${label} must be an object.`);
+    const copy = {};
+    Object.entries(value).forEach(([name, handler]) => {
+      if (!nonBlank(name) || typeof handler !== "function")
+        throw new TypeError(`${label} entries must be named functions.`);
+      copy[name] = handler;
+    });
+    return Object.freeze(copy);
+  }
+
+  function ruleError(index, message) {
+    return `Rule ${index + 1}: ${message}`;
+  }
+
+  function validateRules(rules, registries = {}) {
+    if (!Array.isArray(rules))
+      return Object.freeze({
+        valid: false,
+        errors: Object.freeze(["Rules must be an array."]),
+        rules: Object.freeze([]),
+      });
+
+    const evaluators = registries.evaluators || {};
+    const jumpActions = registries.jumpActions || {};
+    const fixActions = registries.fixActions || {};
+    const errors = [];
+    const ids = new Set();
+    const normalized = [];
+
+    rules.forEach((input, index) => {
+      if (!isPlainObject(input)) {
+        errors.push(ruleError(index, "must be an object."));
+        return;
+      }
+      const id = nonBlank(input.id);
+      if (!id) errors.push(ruleError(index, "id is required."));
+      else if (ids.has(id))
+        errors.push(ruleError(index, `duplicate id "${id}".`));
+      else ids.add(id);
+
+      if (typeof input.enabled !== "boolean")
+        errors.push(ruleError(index, "enabled must be a boolean."));
+      const severity = nonBlank(input.severity);
+      if (!SEVERITIES.includes(severity))
+        errors.push(ruleError(index, `unsupported severity "${severity}".`));
+      const category = nonBlank(input.category);
+      if (!category) errors.push(ruleError(index, "category is required."));
+      const evaluator = nonBlank(input.evaluator);
+      if (!evaluator) errors.push(ruleError(index, "evaluator is required."));
+      else if (typeof evaluators[evaluator] !== "function")
+        errors.push(ruleError(index, `unknown evaluator "${evaluator}".`));
+      const jumpAction = nonBlank(input.jumpAction);
+      if (jumpAction && typeof jumpActions[jumpAction] !== "function")
+        errors.push(ruleError(index, `unknown jump action "${jumpAction}".`));
+      const fixAction = nonBlank(input.fixAction);
+      if (fixAction && typeof fixActions[fixAction] !== "function")
+        errors.push(ruleError(index, `unknown fix action "${fixAction}".`));
+      const priority = input.priority == null ? 100 : Number(input.priority);
+      if (!Number.isFinite(priority))
+        errors.push(ruleError(index, "priority must be a finite number."));
+      if (!nonBlank(input.title))
+        errors.push(ruleError(index, "title is required."));
+      if (!nonBlank(input.summary))
+        errors.push(ruleError(index, "summary is required."));
+      if (input.config != null && !isPlainObject(input.config))
+        errors.push(
+          ruleError(index, "config must be an object when supplied."),
+        );
+
+      normalized.push({
+        id,
+        enabled: input.enabled,
+        severity,
+        category,
+        priority: Number.isFinite(priority) ? priority : 100,
+        title: text(input.title),
+        summary: text(input.summary),
+        detail: text(input.detail),
+        evaluator,
+        jumpAction,
+        fixAction,
+        targetType: nonBlank(input.targetType),
+        config: clone(input.config || {}),
+        documentation: text(input.documentation),
+      });
+    });
+
+    return deepFreeze({
+      valid: errors.length === 0,
+      errors,
+      rules: normalized,
+    });
+  }
+
+  function targetDescriptor(value) {
+    return isPlainObject(value) ? clone(value) : {};
+  }
+
+  function stableTarget(target) {
+    return [
+      target.page,
+      target.stepId,
+      target.conditionId,
+      target.field,
+      target.control,
+    ]
+      .map((value) => nonBlank(value))
+      .join("|");
+  }
+
+  function normalizeIssue(rule, input, index) {
+    const candidate = isPlainObject(input) ? input : { summary: text(input) };
+    const target = targetDescriptor(candidate.target);
+    const severity = SEVERITIES.includes(candidate.severity)
+      ? candidate.severity
+      : rule.severity;
+    const instanceId = nonBlank(candidate.instanceId, String(index + 1));
+    const stableKey = nonBlank(
+      candidate.stableKey,
+      `${rule.id}|${instanceId}|${stableTarget(target)}`,
+    );
+    return {
+      ruleId: rule.id,
+      instanceId,
+      stableKey,
+      severity,
+      category: nonBlank(candidate.category, rule.category),
+      priority: Number.isFinite(Number(candidate.priority))
+        ? Number(candidate.priority)
+        : rule.priority,
+      title: text(candidate.title, rule.title),
+      summary: text(candidate.summary, rule.summary),
+      detail: text(candidate.detail, rule.detail),
+      target,
+      canJump: Boolean(rule.jumpAction && candidate.canJump !== false),
+      canFix: Boolean(rule.fixAction && candidate.canFix !== false),
+      jumpAction: rule.jumpAction || null,
+      fixAction: rule.fixAction || null,
+      actionData: isPlainObject(candidate.actionData)
+        ? clone(candidate.actionData)
+        : {},
+    };
+  }
+
+  function evaluatorOutput(value) {
+    if (value == null || value === false) return [];
+    if (value === true) return [{}];
+    return Array.isArray(value) ? value : [value];
+  }
+
+  function systemIssue(rule, error) {
+    return {
+      ruleId: rule.id,
+      instanceId: "evaluator-error",
+      stableKey: `system|${rule.id}|evaluator-error`,
+      severity: "warning",
+      category: SYSTEM_CATEGORY,
+      priority: -1,
+      title: "Issue check could not be completed",
+      summary: `The ${rule.title} check could not be completed.`,
+      detail: nonBlank(error && error.message, "Evaluator failed."),
+      target: {},
+      canJump: false,
+      canFix: false,
+      jumpAction: null,
+      fixAction: null,
+      actionData: {},
+    };
+  }
+
+  function compareIssues(a, b) {
+    return (
+      SEVERITY_RANK[b.severity] - SEVERITY_RANK[a.severity] ||
+      a.priority - b.priority ||
+      a.category.localeCompare(b.category) ||
+      a.title.localeCompare(b.title) ||
+      stableTarget(a.target).localeCompare(stableTarget(b.target)) ||
+      a.stableKey.localeCompare(b.stableKey)
+    );
+  }
+
+  function aggregate(issues) {
+    const counts = { ok: 0, warning: 0, critical: 0, total: issues.length };
+    issues.forEach((issue) => {
+      if (issue.severity === "critical") counts.critical += 1;
+      else if (issue.severity === "warning") counts.warning += 1;
+    });
+    const status = counts.critical
+      ? "critical"
+      : counts.warning
+        ? "warning"
+        : "ok";
+    counts.ok = status === "ok" ? 1 : 0;
+    return deepFreeze({ status, counts });
+  }
+
+  function create(options = {}) {
+    if (!isPlainObject(options))
+      throw new TypeError("Engine options must be an object.");
+    const registries = Object.freeze({
+      evaluators: registry(options.evaluators, "evaluators"),
+      jumpActions: registry(options.jumpActions, "jumpActions"),
+      fixActions: registry(options.fixActions, "fixActions"),
+    });
+
+    function validate(ruleDefinitions) {
+      return validateRules(ruleDefinitions, registries);
+    }
+
+    function evaluate(context, ruleDefinitions) {
+      const validation = validate(ruleDefinitions);
+      if (!validation.valid) {
+        const error = new TypeError(
+          `Invalid issue rules: ${validation.errors.join(" ")}`,
+        );
+        error.validationErrors = [...validation.errors];
+        throw error;
+      }
+      const safeContext = deepFreeze(clone(context));
+      const collected = [];
+      validation.rules
+        .filter((rule) => rule.enabled)
+        .forEach((rule) => {
+          try {
+            const output = registries.evaluators[rule.evaluator](
+              safeContext,
+              deepFreeze(clone(rule.config)),
+              rule,
+            );
+            evaluatorOutput(output).forEach((item, index) =>
+              collected.push(normalizeIssue(rule, item, index)),
+            );
+          } catch (error) {
+            collected.push(systemIssue(rule, error));
+          }
+        });
+      const unique = new Map();
+      collected.forEach((issue) => {
+        if (!unique.has(issue.stableKey)) unique.set(issue.stableKey, issue);
+      });
+      const issues = [...unique.values()]
+        .sort(compareIssues)
+        .map((issue) => deepFreeze(issue));
+      return deepFreeze({ issues, ...aggregate(issues) });
+    }
+
+    return Object.freeze({
+      evaluate,
+      validateRules: validate,
+      aggregate,
+      severities: SEVERITIES,
+    });
+  }
+
+  if (!global.AuditIssueEngine)
+    global.AuditIssueEngine = Object.freeze({
+      create,
+      validateRules,
+      severities: SEVERITIES,
+    });
+})(typeof window !== "undefined" ? window : globalThis);
+
+
+================================================================================
+END FILE: js/22-audit-issue-engine.js
+================================================================================
+
+================================================================================
+BEGIN FILE: js/23-atlas-audit-issues.js
+================================================================================
+
+
+/**
+ * ATLAS adapter for the portable Audit Issue engine.
+ *
+ * Owns ATLAS context normalization, named evaluators, catalog loading,
+ * transient caching/invalidation, legacy validation compatibility, and the
+ * shared floating/Readiness issue presentation. It does not navigate, mutate
+ * audits, or persist issue results.
+ */
+(function initializeAtlasAuditIssues(global) {
+  "use strict";
+
+  const CATALOG_URL = "./catalogs/audit-issue-rules.json";
+  let catalog = null;
+  let catalogError = null;
+  let catalogPromise = null;
+  let generation = 0;
+  let cache = { generation: -1, signature: "", result: null };
+
+  const string = (value) => (value == null ? "" : String(value));
+  const blank = (value) => !string(value).trim();
+  const array = (value) => (Array.isArray(value) ? value : []);
+  const object = (value) =>
+    value && typeof value === "object" && !Array.isArray(value) ? value : {};
+
+  function assignmentEntries(assignments, role) {
+    return array(object(assignments)[role]);
+  }
+
+  function sourceIdentity(source) {
+    return string(
+      object(source).sourceKey ||
+        object(source).sourceFile ||
+        object(source).alias ||
+        object(source).name,
+    ).trim();
+  }
+
+  function fieldIdentity(field) {
+    const item = object(field);
+    return `${string(item.sourceKey || item.sourceFile || item.source).trim()}::${string(item.rawFieldName || item.fieldName || item.name).trim()}`;
+  }
+
+  function conditionRows(step) {
+    if (typeof global.parseStepConditions === "function") {
+      try {
+        return array(global.parseStepConditions(step));
+      } catch (_error) {}
+    }
+    const params = object(step.params);
+    if (params.conditions_json) {
+      try {
+        return array(JSON.parse(params.conditions_json));
+      } catch (_error) {
+        return [{ __parseError: true }];
+      }
+    }
+    return array(step.conditions);
+  }
+
+  function meaningfulAction(step) {
+    const item = object(step);
+    const params = object(item.params);
+    const type = string(params.then_action_type || params.action_type).trim();
+    if (string(item.action).trim()) return true;
+    if (type === "do_not_display") return true;
+    if (type === "display_text")
+      return !blank(params.then_text) && !blank(params.then_output_column);
+    if (type === "set_column_value")
+      return (
+        !blank(params.then_target_column) && !blank(params.then_value_field)
+      );
+    if (type === "set_column_calculation")
+      return (
+        !blank(params.then_target_column) && !blank(params.then_value_field)
+      );
+    if (type === "set_column_string_transform")
+      return (
+        !blank(params.then_target_column) &&
+        !blank(params.then_string_source_field) &&
+        !blank(params.then_string_operation)
+      );
+    if (type === "freeform") return !blank(params.then_action);
+    return false;
+  }
+
+  function stepNeedsAction(step) {
+    const type = string(step.step_type || step.type).trim();
+    return ["if_then", "else", "flag_records", "filter_rows"].includes(type);
+  }
+
+  function descendantHasAction(step, stepsByParent, visited = new Set()) {
+    if (!step || visited.has(step.id)) return false;
+    visited.add(step.id);
+    return array(stepsByParent.get(step.id)).some(
+      (child) =>
+        child.enabled !== false &&
+        (meaningfulAction(child) ||
+          descendantHasAction(child, stepsByParent, visited)),
+    );
+  }
+
+  function buildContext(
+    auditInput = (
+      typeof hasSelectedAudit === "function"
+        ? hasSelectedAudit()
+        : Boolean(state?.auditSelected)
+    )
+      ? state.audit
+      : null,
+  ) {
+    const audit = object(auditInput);
+    const sources = array(audit.sources);
+    const fields = array(audit.fields);
+    const steps = array(audit.steps);
+    const displayFields = array(audit.displayFields);
+    const catalogSources = array(global.repoLibraryState?.sourceFiles);
+    const sourceIdentityMap = Object.create(null);
+    const registerSourceIdentity = (value, canonical) => {
+      const key = string(value).trim().toLocaleLowerCase();
+      if (!key || !canonical) return;
+      if (!(key in sourceIdentityMap)) sourceIdentityMap[key] = canonical;
+      else if (sourceIdentityMap[key] !== canonical) sourceIdentityMap[key] = null;
+    };
+    const canonicalSource = (sourceLike) => {
+      if (typeof global.atlasResolveSourceIdentity === "function") {
+        const resolved = global.atlasResolveSourceIdentity(sourceLike, catalogSources);
+        if (resolved?.ambiguous) return "";
+        return string(resolved?.sourceKey || resolved?.sourceFile).trim();
+      }
+      return typeof sourceLike === "string"
+        ? string(sourceLike).trim()
+        : sourceIdentity(sourceLike);
+    };
+    catalogSources.forEach((source) => {
+      const canonical = canonicalSource(source);
+      [source.sourceKey, source.sourceFile, source.alias, source.name]
+        .forEach((value) => registerSourceIdentity(value, canonical));
+    });
+    sources.forEach((source) => {
+      const canonical = canonicalSource(source);
+      [source.sourceKey, source.sourceFile, source.alias, source.name]
+        .forEach((value) => registerSourceIdentity(value, canonical));
+    });
+    const canonicalForValue = (value) => {
+      const raw = string(value).trim();
+      const key = raw.toLocaleLowerCase();
+      if (key in sourceIdentityMap) return sourceIdentityMap[key] || "";
+      const canonical = canonicalSource(raw);
+      if (canonical) registerSourceIdentity(raw, canonical);
+      return canonical;
+    };
+    const sourceIds = new Set(
+      sources.map((source) => canonicalSource(source)).filter(Boolean),
+    );
+    const knownSourceIds = new Set(
+      catalogSources.map((source) => canonicalSource(source)).filter(Boolean),
+    );
+    const knownFieldIds = new Set();
+    catalogSources.forEach((source) => {
+      const canonical = canonicalSource(source);
+      array(source.fields).forEach((field) => {
+        const fieldName = string(
+          field.rawFieldName || field.fieldName || field.name || field.label,
+        ).trim();
+        if (canonical && fieldName)
+          knownFieldIds.add(`${canonical}::${fieldName}`);
+      });
+    });
+    fields.forEach((field) => {
+      const item = object(field);
+      const canonical = canonicalForValue(
+        item.sourceKey || item.sourceFile || item.source,
+      );
+      const fieldName = string(
+        item.rawFieldName || item.fieldName || item.name,
+      ).trim();
+      if (canonical && fieldName)
+        knownFieldIds.add(`${canonical}::${fieldName}`);
+    });
+
+    const stepsById = new Map();
+    const stepsByParent = new Map();
+    steps.forEach((step, index) => {
+      const id = string(step.id).trim();
+      if (id && !stepsById.has(id)) stepsById.set(id, step);
+      const parent = string(step.parentId).trim();
+      if (!stepsByParent.has(parent)) stepsByParent.set(parent, []);
+      stepsByParent.get(parent).push(step);
+    });
+
+    const normalizedSteps = steps.map((step, index) => {
+      const params = object(step.params);
+      const conditions = conditionRows(step).map(
+        (condition, conditionIndex) => ({
+          ...object(condition),
+          __index: conditionIndex,
+        }),
+      );
+      return {
+        id: string(step.id).trim(),
+        index,
+        title: string(step.title).trim(),
+        type: string(step.step_type || step.type).trim(),
+        enabled: step.enabled !== false,
+        parentId: string(step.parentId).trim(),
+        branchType: string(step.branchType || "root").trim(),
+        source: string(step.source || params.source_file).trim(),
+        field: string(step.field || params.field_name).trim(),
+        action: string(step.action || params.then_action).trim(),
+        params: { ...params },
+        conditions,
+        hasMeaningfulAction: meaningfulAction(step),
+        descendantHasMeaningfulAction: descendantHasAction(step, stepsByParent),
+      };
+    });
+
+    const supportedRelationshipJoinTypes = new Set([
+      "INNER JOIN",
+      "LEFT JOIN",
+      "RIGHT JOIN",
+      "FULL OUTER JOIN",
+    ]);
+    const joinRelationships = normalizedSteps.flatMap((step) => {
+      const params = step.params;
+      const joinType = string(params.join_type).trim().toUpperCase();
+      if (
+        !step.enabled ||
+        step.type !== "join" ||
+        !supportedRelationshipJoinTypes.has(joinType) ||
+        blank(params.left_source) ||
+        blank(params.right_source) ||
+        blank(params.left_field) ||
+        blank(params.right_field)
+      )
+        return [];
+      const left = canonicalForValue(params.left_source);
+      const right = canonicalForValue(params.right_source);
+      if (
+        !left ||
+        !right ||
+        left === right ||
+        !sourceIds.has(left) ||
+        !sourceIds.has(right)
+      )
+        return [];
+      return [[left, right]];
+    });
+    return {
+      audit: {
+        name: string(audit.name),
+        purpose: string(audit.purpose || audit.description),
+        assignments: {
+          assigned: assignmentEntries(audit.assignments, "assigned"),
+          backup: assignmentEntries(audit.assignments, "backup"),
+          developer: assignmentEntries(audit.assignments, "developer"),
+        },
+        sources: sources.map((source) => ({
+          ...object(source),
+          identity: sourceIdentity(source),
+          canonicalIdentity: canonicalSource(source),
+        })),
+        fields: fields.map((field) => ({
+          ...object(field),
+          identity: fieldIdentity(field),
+        })),
+        steps: normalizedSteps,
+        displayFields: displayFields.map((field) => ({ ...object(field) })),
+      },
+      knownSourceIds: [...knownSourceIds],
+      knownFieldIds: [...knownFieldIds],
+      selectedSourceIds: [...sourceIds],
+      sourceIdentityMap: { ...sourceIdentityMap },
+      joinRelationships,
+      workbookWarnings: array(global.workbookState?.warnings).map(string),
+    };
+  }
+
+  function issue(instanceId, target = {}, values = {}) {
+    return { instanceId: string(instanceId), target, ...values };
+  }
+
+  const evaluators = {
+    missingTextValue(context, config) {
+      const value =
+        config.path === "audit.name"
+          ? context.audit.name
+          : context.audit.purpose;
+      return blank(value)
+        ? [issue(config.path, { page: "overview", field: config.path })]
+        : [];
+    },
+    missingAssignment(context, config) {
+      return array(context.audit.assignments[config.role]).length
+        ? []
+        : [
+            issue(config.role, {
+              page: "overview",
+              field: `assignments.${config.role}`,
+            }),
+          ];
+    },
+    missingSources(context) {
+      return context.audit.sources.length
+        ? []
+        : [issue("sources", { page: "fields" })];
+    },
+    missingSteps(context) {
+      if (!context.audit.sources.length) return [];
+      return context.audit.steps.length
+        ? []
+        : [issue("steps", { page: "steps" })];
+    },
+    workbookWarnings(context) {
+      return context.workbookWarnings.map((message, index) =>
+        issue(index, { page: "review" }, { summary: message }),
+      );
+    },
+    sourceAndFieldIntegrity(context) {
+      const knownSources = new Set(context.knownSourceIds);
+      const knownFields = new Set(context.knownFieldIds);
+      const selectedSources = new Set(context.selectedSourceIds);
+      const issues = [];
+      context.audit.sources.forEach((source, index) => {
+        if (!source.identity)
+          issues.push(
+            issue(
+              `source-${index}-identity`,
+              { page: "fields" },
+              {
+                severity: "critical",
+                summary: "A selected source has no usable identity.",
+              },
+            ),
+          );
+        else if (
+          knownSources.size &&
+          !knownSources.has(source.canonicalIdentity || source.identity)
+        )
+          issues.push(
+            issue(
+              `source-${index}-missing`,
+              { page: "fields" },
+              {
+                severity: "critical",
+                summary:
+                  "A selected source is no longer available in the source catalog.",
+              },
+            ),
+          );
+      });
+      context.audit.steps.forEach((step) => {
         const references = [];
         if (step.source || step.field)
           references.push({
@@ -9203,162 +16187,605 @@ END FILE: js/23-atlas-audit-issues.js

+================================================================================
+BEGIN FILE: test/audit-issue-engine.test.js
+================================================================================
+
+import test from "node:test";
+import assert from "node:assert/strict";
+import { readFileSync } from "node:fs";
+import vm from "node:vm";
+
+const engineSource = readFileSync(
+  new URL("../js/22-audit-issue-engine.js", import.meta.url),
+  "utf8",
+);
+const catalog = JSON.parse(
+  readFileSync(
+    new URL("../catalogs/audit-issue-rules.json", import.meta.url),
+    "utf8",
+  ),
+);
+
+function loadApi() {
+  const context = vm.createContext({ console });
+  vm.runInContext(engineSource, context);
+  return context.AuditIssueEngine;
+}
+
+function enabledRule(overrides = {}) {
+  return {
+    id: "test.required",
+    enabled: true,
+    severity: "warning",
+    category: "setup",
+    priority: 10,
+    title: "Required value missing",
+    summary: "A value is missing.",
+    detail: "Provide a value.",
+    evaluator: "requiredValue",
+    jumpAction: "jump",
+    fixAction: "fix",
+    targetType: "field",
+    config: { key: "name" },
+    ...overrides,
+  };
+}
+
+function handlers() {
+  return {
+    evaluators: {
+      requiredValue(context, config) {
+        return context[config.key]
+          ? []
+          : [{ instanceId: config.key, target: { field: config.key } }];
+      },
+      duplicate() {
+        return [
+          { stableKey: "same", summary: "first" },
+          { stableKey: "same", summary: "second" },
+        ];
+      },
+      explode() {
+        throw new Error("expected evaluator failure");
+      },
+      critical() {
+        return [{}];
+      },
+    },
+    jumpActions: { jump() {} },
+    fixActions: { fix() {} },
+  };
+}
+
+test("catalog is valid JSON with declarative rules and no executable strings", () => {
+  assert.equal(catalog.schemaVersion, 1);
+  assert.ok(Array.isArray(catalog.rules));
+  assert.ok(catalog.rules.length > 0);
+  assert.equal(JSON.stringify(catalog).includes("function("), false);
+  assert.equal(JSON.stringify(catalog).includes("=>"), false);
+});
+
+test("creates independent engine instances", () => {
+  const api = loadApi();
+  const first = api.create(handlers());
+  const second = api.create(handlers());
+  assert.notEqual(first, second);
+  assert.equal(first.evaluate({}, [enabledRule()]).counts.warning, 1);
+  assert.equal(
+    second.evaluate({ name: "Ready" }, [enabledRule()]).status,
+    "ok",
+  );
+});
+
+test("validates malformed rules, duplicate ids, severity, and registered names", () => {
+  const api = loadApi();
+  const engine = api.create(handlers());
+  const result = engine.validateRules([
+    enabledRule({ id: "duplicate" }),
+    enabledRule({
+      id: "duplicate",
+      severity: "urgent",
+      evaluator: "missing",
+      jumpAction: "missing",
+      fixAction: "missing",
+    }),
+  ]);
+  assert.equal(result.valid, false);
+  assert.match(result.errors.join(" "), /duplicate id/);
+  assert.match(result.errors.join(" "), /unsupported severity/);
+  assert.match(result.errors.join(" "), /unknown evaluator/);
+  assert.match(result.errors.join(" "), /unknown jump action/);
+  assert.match(result.errors.join(" "), /unknown fix action/);
+});
+
+test("normalizes issues without mutating caller context, rules, or evaluator values", () => {
+  const api = loadApi();
+  const context = { name: "", nested: { value: 1 } };
+  const rules = [enabledRule()];
+  const beforeContext = structuredClone(context);
+  const beforeRules = structuredClone(rules);
+  const result = api.create(handlers()).evaluate(context, rules);
+  assert.deepEqual(context, beforeContext);
+  assert.deepEqual(rules, beforeRules);
+  assert.equal(result.issues[0].ruleId, "test.required");
+  assert.equal(result.issues[0].stableKey, "test.required|name||||name|");
+  assert.equal(result.issues[0].canJump, true);
+  assert.equal(result.issues[0].canFix, true);
+  assert.ok(Object.isFrozen(result));
+  assert.ok(Object.isFrozen(result.issues[0]));
+});
+
+test("deduplicates by stable key and sorts critical before warning", () => {
+  const api = loadApi();
+  const rules = [
+    enabledRule({
+      id: "warning",
+      evaluator: "duplicate",
+      jumpAction: "",
+      fixAction: "",
+    }),
+    enabledRule({
+      id: "critical",
+      evaluator: "critical",
+      severity: "critical",
+      priority: 99,
+      jumpAction: "",
+      fixAction: "",
+    }),
+  ];
+  const result = api.create(handlers()).evaluate({}, rules);
+  assert.equal(result.issues.length, 2);
+  assert.equal(result.issues[0].severity, "critical");
+  assert.equal(result.issues[1].summary, "first");
+  assert.equal(result.status, "critical");
+  assert.deepEqual(
+    { ...result.counts },
+    { ok: 0, warning: 1, critical: 1, total: 2 },
+  );
+});
+
+test("disabled rules emit no issues", () => {
+  const api = loadApi();
+  const result = api
+    .create(handlers())
+    .evaluate({}, [enabledRule({ enabled: false })]);
+  assert.equal(result.issues.length, 0);
+  assert.equal(result.status, "ok");
+  assert.deepEqual(
+    { ...result.counts },
+    { ok: 1, warning: 0, critical: 0, total: 0 },
+  );
+});
+
+test("evaluator failures produce a safe system warning and do not stop other rules", () => {
+  const api = loadApi();
+  const rules = [
+    enabledRule({
+      id: "broken",
+      evaluator: "explode",
+      jumpAction: "",
+      fixAction: "",
+    }),
+    enabledRule({
+      id: "working",
+      evaluator: "requiredValue",
+      jumpAction: "",
+      fixAction: "",
+    }),
+  ];
+  const result = api.create(handlers()).evaluate({}, rules);
+  assert.equal(result.issues.length, 2);
+  assert.ok(result.issues.some((issue) => issue.category === "system"));
+  assert.ok(result.issues.some((issue) => issue.ruleId === "working"));
+});
+
+test("invalid catalogs are rejected before evaluation", () => {
+  const api = loadApi();
+  const engine = api.create(handlers());
+  assert.throws(
+    () => engine.evaluate({}, [enabledRule({ evaluator: "unknown" })]),
+    /Invalid issue rules/,
+  );
+});
+
+
+================================================================================
+END FILE: test/audit-issue-engine.test.js
 ================================================================================

+  async function executeFix(stableKey, trigger) {
+    if (renderedAuditKey !== currentAuditKey()) {
+      status?.("The active audit changed. Audit issues were refreshed before applying the fix.", "warn");
+      invalidate();
+      return false;
+    }
+    const entry = current().issues.find((item) => item.stableKey === stableKey);
+    const action = entry && fixActions[entry.fixAction];
+    if (!entry || !entry.canFix || typeof action !== "function") {
+      status?.("This audit issue no longer has an available fix.", "warn");
+      invalidate();
+      return false;
+    }
+    try {
+      return Boolean(await action(entry, trigger));
+    } catch (error) {
+      console.error("ATLAS Audit Issues: fix action failed safely.", {
+        ruleId: entry.ruleId,
+        message: string(error?.message),
+      });
+      status?.("The assignment selector could not be opened.", "bad");
+      return false;
+    }
+  }
